The ledger outlives the metric. dicipler-pixel/gravity-ledger-lean, commit 885932f
(30 theorems), proves that every tangent to an idempotent is off-diagonal, that a moving signed
metric has an indefinite side (moving_metric_indefinite_side), and that the overlap of equaltrace idempotents has an exact quadratic limit (overlap_quadratic_limit). In the frame of
Section 2 this is the finite algebra behind “the ledger outlives the metric” [28]: the overlap of the
kept projector stays controlled where a metric reading changes sign.
The square case. dicipler-pixel/square-case-lean, commit 6769949 (63 theorems), the
Lean companion of [29], proves that the intrinsic flow keeps a tangent on a binary-collision wall
(intrinsic_preserves_wall_tangent) and that for orthogonal projections of equal rank k,
Tr(P Q) = k − 12 kP − Qk2F . The first is the wall-side half of one-way transport (what is in the wall
stays in it); the second is the overlap used when the kept sector is compared across the cut.
The three-body fold. dicipler-pixel/three-body-lean, commit 1f3ddcd, the Lean companion of [30], proves the fold λmin (π/2+ε) = sin2 ε with λmin /ε2 → 1 (fold_lambda, fold_quadratic)
and the shifted ledger minimum (ledger_min): the same quadratic touch as the fold of [12]. Its
report 3-6-9: straw man to the max (28 October 2026) does not concern neutrinos and is not used.
Other branches read. The five test-* branches of dicipler-pixel/dirac-time-lean (26
September 2026) each carry a sibling library (Elemental Peeling, the gravity ledger, the light
ledger, Universal Projector Geometry, Yang–Mills) for build testing, not new Dirac Time statements. The UPG branch (4c76b9e) states when a retained sector evolves on its own, the question the cut of Section 4 answers for the neutrinos. Branch atlas-207-coverage-2026-10-23 of
dicipler-pixel/relational-atlas-lean is already merged into main@060c904.

20 Summary
The neutrino collision operator is a cut whose whole non-normality sits on one link, density to
flux, with weight equal to the sound speed; its kept-sector mode lives only above Romatschke’s
onset κ = 2k/π, where its non-orthogonality diverges. McCormick’s code, run on cases outside his
paper, shows that the collapse time alone does not set the neutrino clock, and that the decoupling
history changes the threshold, not only its end point. The kept-sector memory, read mostly at
the first compaction maximum and fitted once, predicted three histories it had not seen to within
0.071, 0.014 and 0.037, while a history-blind rule failed. The steep-history thresholds do not move
with the rate cap or the grid. The memory sits in the angular structure a fluid closure drops: set
at or after decoupling, it returns into density, flux and stress; set deep in the coupled era, it is
erased. And the anisotropic stress carries the threshold: with the shear relaxing at r times the flux
rate, the offset of the surviving share moves close to a straight line in the link rapidity 21 ln r, at a
rate of 1.10 ± 0.01 (not converged) inside the admissible window, and at 1.19–1.20 when the shear
alone is damped. The direction of the flux–shear mismatch, not its connection energy, is what the
collapse reads; of the rulers tested, the law lives in the collision operator’s own link rapidity, not in
the displacement of the linear kept state; and the rate is a measured number at finite resolution,
34

1.10 ± 0.01, the ±0.01 an error budget rather than a converged value or a statistical interval, 1.102
and 1.101 at the two finest of three grid levels, with no closed form attached. The readout Gramian
of the hidden moments has no kernel and ranks what must be kept, and collision histories that
agree in integrated opacity and integrated connection energy still differ in the memory. In the
full collapse, hidden angular structure planted at decoupling, invisible to density, flux, stress and
Einstein’s equations at the restart, moves the black-hole threshold by up to 5.5 × 10−3 at one
engineered amplitude, from result lines whose per-run logs are missing, with the sign set by its
parity and the size ranked by the Gramian; planted in the coupled era it moves it by less than
one per cent of that. McCormick’s physical collision term is the split link itself, and self-scattering
acts on the threshold mostly through timing, with the flux–shear split carrying +0.0003. What the
neutrino state must keep is not its density, flux and stress alone, but the hidden angular structure
the future reads, and the order of the history that made it.

A.3a The onset of the kept-sector pole (Romatschke’s onset [13, Eq. (37)], with
its non-orthogonality)
A pole of u0 = g/(1 − κg) needs κg = 1, that is arctan[k/(s + κ)] = ϕ with ϕ = k/κ. For
real s > −κ the principal arctangent takes values in (0, π/2), so a solution exists exactly when
ϕ < π/2, and then s + κ = k cot ϕ. Because the principal arctangent is one-to-one, there is no
other solution on the physical sheet. As ϕ → π/2, s → −κ, the centre of the cut of g, which joins
the branch points −κ ± ik. The eigenvector is u(µ) = 1/(s + κ + ikµ). The operator is complex
symmetric

A.8 The link coordinate is odd
For r > 0, Γ(1/r) = (1/r − 1)/(1/r + 1) = (1 − r)/(1 + r) = −Γ(r), and 21 ln(1/r) = − 21 ln r. Hence
Γ(r) = tanh( 12 ln r), since tanh u = (e2u − 1)/(e2u + 1) with e2u = r. □

A.10 The streaming weights
c2ℓ = (ℓ + 1)2 /((2ℓ + 1)(2ℓ + 3)) gives c20 = 1/3, c21 = 4/15, c22 = 9/35, so c20 /c21 = 5/4 and
c21 /c22 = 28/27: the adjacent weights differ by 1.8% from the second link on, which is why a rule
additive in link-weight ratios nearly cancels when two adjacent links jump by opposite rapidities
(Section 9). Checked in Lean. □

A.12 The discrete dipole and shear projections

P
On the midpoint bins µk with weight ∆µ, write hf, gi =
k fk gk ∆µ, m2 = hµ, µi, and P2 =
µ2 − m2 /2. Then h1, µi = 0 by the symmetry of the bins, h1, P2 i = m2 − m22 h1, 1i = 0 because
h1, 1i = 2, and hµ, P2 i = 0 by symmetry. So d = hµ, Iiµ/m2 and s = hP2 , IiP2 /hP2 , P2 i are the
orthogonal projections of the intensity onto the discrete ℓ = 1 and ℓ = 2 directions, orthogonal to
each other and to the isotropic part. The collision terms of Section 9, κ2 (ε/4π − I) + (κ2 − κ1 )d
and κ1 (ε/4π − I) + (κ1 − κ2 )s, therefore conserve the bin-summed energy exactly, leave the dipole
relaxing at κ1 in both, and relax the ℓ = 2 part at κ2 in both (together with every ℓ ≥ 3 in the
first, alone in the second); m2 = 2/3 − ∆µ2 /6 differs from the continuum 2/3 by 0.1% at Nµ = 32.
Checked numerically to 10−16 (Appendix B.15). □

A.14 The hidden sector of the chain is fully observable
Let B = −ikM − D with M the tridiagonal streaming matrix of Section 4 (couplings cℓ 6= 0)
and D diagonal; let R project onto ℓ ≤ 2 and Q embed ℓ ≥ 3. The observability matrix of the
pair (R, B) has rows RB j . Because B is tridiagonal with nonzero off-diagonal entries −ikcℓ , the
entry (RB j )2,2+j collects only the path that climbs one rung per step from ℓ = 2 to ℓ = 2 + j, so
(RB j )2,2+j = (−ik)j c2 c3 · · · cj+1 6= 0 for k 6= 0, whatever D is. The rows RB j , j = 0, . . . , L − 2,
therefore reach every moment, the pair is observable, and N∞ = {0} for the frozen-time operator.
For a smooth time-dependent D(τ ) the Gramian GT is positive definite for every T > τs . Suppose
h∗ GT h = 0; then y(τ ) = RU (τ, τs )Qh vanishes on [τs , T ], so all its derivatives vanish at τs . Let
ℓ0 ≥ 3 be the lowest moment with hℓ0 6= 0. Each derivative of U is a sum of products of B and of
derivatives of D; the diagonal factors cannot change ℓ, and only the streaming part climbs one rung
per factor. So the ℓ = 2 component of the (ℓ0 −2)-th derivative of y at τs is (−ik)ℓ0 −2 c2 · · · cℓ0 −1 hℓ0 6=
0, a contradiction. Its eigenvalues
R T then rank the hidden directions by the integrated squared
readout they produce; h∗ GT h = τs kRU (τ, τs )Qhk2 dτ follows from the definition. Averaging over
±k commutes with the parity ℓ 7→ (−1)ℓ , so the averaged Gramian’s eigenvectors can be chosen of
pure parity. □

A.15 Matched states at a restart surface
Let pℓ (ℓ ≥ 3) be orthogonal on the 32 bins to 1, µ and µ2 under the bin weights. Then
δIik = f bi mi (εi /4π) pℓ (µk ) has zero discrete moments of orders 0, 1 and 2 in every cell, so the
neutrino energy density, flux and radial pressure of the two states I ± δI are equal cell by cell.
The stress–energy tensor of the neutrinos in the spherical solver is built from exactly these three
moments, so the sources of the Hamiltonian and momentum constraints, the lapse equation and
the fluid equations are equal; with the fluid and metric arrays copied unchanged, every field of
the restart state except the hidden part of I is identical, and the constraint residual is identical. Positivity of I ± δI in every bin holds if |f | bi mi |pℓ (µk )| < 4πIik /εi for all k; the margin
mi = mink [4πIik /εi ]/ maxk |pℓ (µk )| makes |f | < 1 suﬀicient, and f = ± 21 uses half of it. On an
isotropic cell the bound is |a| < 1/ maxk |p3 (µk )| = 1.21498 for ℓ = 3, and +2.3509, −1.3885 for
ℓ = 4. □

B.1 Identity checks
# SCRIPT: PBH-NU-IDENTITIES checks of every derived identity used in the paper
# Kill conditions (written first): each identity must hold to 1e-10 relative, else the statement is withdrawn.
import numpy as np
from scipy.integrate import quad
LM=80
c=lambda l:(l+1)/np.sqrt((2*l+1)*(2*l+3))
# <l+1|mu|l> in the normalised Legendre basis
Mu=np.zeros((LM+1,LM+1))
for l in range(LM): Mu[l,l+1]=Mu[l+1,l]=c(l)
def L_op(k,k1,k2):
D=np.diag([0.0,-k1]+[-k2]*(LM-1)); A=-1j*k*Mu; return A,D,A+D
fro2=lambda X:np.sum(np.abs(X)**2)
print('I1 single rate: (1/8)|[L,L*]|^2 = kappa^2 k^2/3')
for k,kap in [(1.0,0.3),(2.0,1.5),(0.7,4.0)]:
A,D,L=L_op(k,kap,kap); lhs=fro2(L@L.conj().T-L.conj().T@L)/8
print('
k=%.1f kappa=%.1f lhs=%.12f rhs=%.12f'%(k,kap,lhs,kap**2*k**2/3))
print('I2 split rates: (1/8)|[L,L*]|^2 = k^2 k1^2/3 + (4k^2/15)(k2-k1)^2')
for k,k1,k2 in [(1.0,0.3,2.0),(2.0,1.5,0.4),(0.7,4.0,9.0)]:
A,D,L=L_op(k,k1,k2); lhs=fro2(L@L.conj().T-L.conj().T@L)/8
print('
k=%.1f k1=%.1f k2=%.1f lhs=%.12f rhs=%.12f'%(k,k1,k2,lhs,k**2*k1**2/3+4*k**2/15*(k2-k1)**2))
print('I3 weight across the cut: |P (k mu) (1-P)|^2 = k^2/3, so sqrt(E_B) = k/sqrt3 = c_s k')
P=np.zeros((LM+1,LM+1)); P[0,0]=1; Q=np.eye(LM+1)-P
for k in [1.0,2.5]: print('

B.2 The one change to McCormick’s code
In a copy of run_nuc.py, the collision rate line became
kap = (1/(2*t))*min((eps_dec/(2*np.sqrt(t*t0)))**nexp, kapcap)
with nexp (default 3) and kapcap = 5 × 105 as inputs. Nothing else was changed.
# SCRIPT: BEYOND-STEVE-BISECT threshold bisection with gradual decoupling, profile alpha and rate exponent as
inputs
import sys, json, time, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.environ['SIMDIR']); sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

41

from run_nuc_n import run
tag=sys.argv[1]; qshape=float(sys.argv[2]); epsd=float(sys.argv[3]); nexp=float(sys.argv[4])
lo,hi,tol=float(sys.argv[5]),float(sys.argv[6]),float(sys.argv[7]); emax=float(sys.argv[8])
log=[]; t0=time.time()
while hi-lo>tol:
mid=0.5*(lo+hi); t1=time.time()
out=run(mid,fnu=0.409,N=400,K=32,eps0=0.1,eps_dec=epsd,eps_max=emax,qshape=qshape,nexp=nexp)
print(f'{tag} delta={mid:.5f}: {out["status"]} eps={out["eps"]:.2f} ({time.time()-t1:.0f}s)',flush=True)
log.append((mid,out['status'],float(out['eps'])))
if out['status']=='BH': hi=mid
elif out['status']=='dispersed': lo=mid
else: break

# SCRIPT: PBH-NU-MEMORY-CURVE kept-sector survival (memory form) curves for the figures; same object as WBSTEVE-07
import numpy as np, json
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
from scipy.special import roots_legendre

B.6 The kept-sector readings against McCormick’s curve and the history prediction
# SCRIPT: WB-STEVE-03-PAPER-OBJECTS
# D2 (memory survival of the kept sector under the time-dependent relaxing operator) and D3 (energy that has
crossed into the
# hidden sector) as predictors of Steve's G(eps_dec). Kill conditions are those stated in WB-STEVE-02-LINEAR
(D2, D3, H1 and
# opposite runs); restated:
# D2 g = (1-A)/(1-A_inst), A = population of the kept sector l=0 at readout epoch eps* in {1.7, 4.0} (the
two epochs Steve's
#
own numbers give), no fitted parameter. Dies if max_eps |g - G| >= 0.10 at BOTH epochs.
# D3 g = X/X_inst, X = sum_{l>=2} |F_l|^2/(2l+1) summed over the Gaussian perturbation (weights k^2 zeta^2),
same epochs, same kill.
# Opposite runs: kept sector = l in {0,1} (flux kept), and k r_m fixed at 2 instead of the profile average.
# Fitted-epoch scan is diagnostic only (a fitted epoch is not a pass).
import numpy as np
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
from scipy.special import roots_legendre
import wb_lin
Gs = {0.25: 0.960, 0.5: 0.868, 1.0: 0.684, 2.0: 0.431, 4.0: 0.190, 8.0: 0.063}
kn, kw = roots_legendre(36); kmin, kmax = 0.25, 9.0; ks = 0.5*(kmax-kmin)*(kn+1)+kmin; wk = 0.5*(kmax-kmin)*kw
wE = wk*ks**2*np.exp(-ks**2/2)
# energy weights k^2 zeta(k)^2 of the Gaussian perturbation
wE /= wE.sum()
LM = 60; a = lambda l: l/np.sqrt(4.0*l*l-1)
M = np.zeros((LM+1, LM+1))
for l in range(1, LM+1): M[l, l-1] = M[l-1, l] = a(l)
tau0 = 0.02; eg = np.linspace(tau0, 6.0, 1200)
def survival(k, ed, keep):
"""population of kept sector (keep=1: l=0; keep=2: l=0,1) vs tau, under dv/dtau = -(i k M + Kc (1-P)) v, v
(tau0)=|0>."""
n = LM+1; P = np.zeros(n); P[:keep] = 1
def rhs(t, v):
K = 0.0 if ed is None else ed**3/t**4
x, y = v[:n], v[n:]
return np.concatenate([-K*(1-P)*x+k*(M @ y), -K*(1-P)*y-k*(M @ x)])
v0 = np.zeros(2*n); v0[0] = 1
sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (tau0, eg[-1]), v0, method='BDF', t_eval=eg, rtol=1e-9, atol=1e-12)
pop = sol.y[:keep]**2+sol.y[n:n+keep]**2
return np.sum(pop, axis=0)
def avg_surv(ed, keep, single=None):
if single is not None: return survival(single, ed, keep)

B.7 Shear and flux links, and which mean of the rates
# SCRIPT: WB-STEVE-01-ALGEBRA
# Tests T0-T4 mapping "The Weight Between" v1.1 onto McCormick's free-streaming PBH preprint (algebraic part).
# KILL CONDITIONS (written before any run):
# T0 Steve-internal consistency (so the targets are trustworthy). Table II: recompute Delta C_l,c/C_l,c from

# T1 Prop 5 / Def 4 on Steve's operator L = -i k mu - kappa (I-P), P = projector on l=0 (orthonormal
Legendre basis).
#
H = k mu, E_B = ||P H (1-P)||_F^2 must equal k^2/3 = (1/2)||[H,P]||_F^2 = Var_{|0>}(H) to 1e-12
relative. Dies otherwise.
#
Connection-energy identity (scalar form, H' = -L = D + iK, D=diag(kappa_l), K = k mu):
#
(1/4)||[H',H'^dag]||^2 = ||[D,K]||^2 = S_2. Dies if off by > 1e-10 relative.
# T2 Hidden-walk (Schur) formula of the paper: G_B(z) = [z - E - W (z-F)^{-1} W^dag]^{-1} with E=0, W the l
=0->l>=1 block,
#
F the hidden block of L, must equal the closed form G(z) = g(s)/(1 - kappa g(s)), g(s)=arctan(k/s)/k, s
=z+kappa,

# SCRIPT: WB-STEVE-04-CHANNELS
# (i) Non-normality channels of a collision operator with different rates per multipole (Steve open item (c)),
via the paper's
#
connection-energy identity. (ii) Item (e): neutrinos as a self-interacting fluid that exchanges NO
momentum with the plasma.
# KILL CONDITIONS (written before the run):
# C1 For L = -i k mu - diag(kappa_l), S_2 = ||[D,K]||^2 = (1/4)||[H',H'^dag]||^2 splits over Legendre edges
l<->l+1 as
#
sum_l 2 k^2 a_{l+1}^2 (kappa_l - kappa_{l+1})^2, a_l^2 = l^2/(4l^2-1). Dies if off by > 1e-10 relative
.
#
Consequence (derived): the relaxation-time model (all kappa_l equal for l>=1) puts ALL non-normality on
edge 0-1; any
#
model with kappa_1 != kappa_2 puts weight on edge 1-2.
# E1 Two perfect fluids with equal sound speed and no momentum exchange are one fluid: in pure radiation (p
= e/3) the shift of
#
the first maximum must vanish as the neutrino self-interaction rate K0 = kappa/H -> infinity.
Prediction from the paper's
#
rung -1 (viscosity weight of the 1-2 edge, S_-1 = (4/15) k^2/kappa): the shift times K0 tends to a
constant.
#
Dies if (a) shift does not decrease monotonically with K0, or (b) shift*K0 differs by more than 15
percent between
#
K0 = 100 and K0 = 1000. Opposite run: the same operator with the drag rate on (flux relaxed to the
plasma) instead.
import numpy as np
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
from scipy.special import roots_legendre
fro2 = lambda X: float(np.real(np.vdot(X, X)))
lmax = 60; a2 = lambda l: l*l/(4.0*l*l-1)
M = np.zeros((lmax+1, lmax+1))
for l in range(1, lmax+1): M[l, l-1] = M[l-1, l] = np.sqrt(a2(l))
print('=== C1 non-normality channels ===')
k = 1.3
for name, kap in [('relaxation time (all l>=1 equal) kappa_e=1', [0]+[1.0]*lmax),
('nu_e: flux damped by e+- (1), stress by e+- + nu-nu (1+1.5)', [0, 1.0]+[2.5]*(lmax-1)),
('nu_mu,tau: flux (1), stress (1+7)', [0, 1.0]+[8.0]*(lmax-1))]:
D = np.diag(kap); Hp = D+1j*k*M
S2 = fro2(D @ (k*M)-(k*M) @ D); chk = 0.25*fro2(Hp @ Hp.conj().T-Hp.conj().T @ Hp)
edges = [2*k*k*a2(l+1)*(kap[l]-kap[l+1])**2 for l in range(0, 6)]
tot = sum(2*k*k*a2(l+1)*(kap[l]-kap[l+1])**2 for l in range(0, lmax))
print('%-62s S_2 = %.6f (commutator form %.6f, edge sum %.6f)' % (name, S2, chk, tot))
assert abs(S2/chk-1) < 1e-10 and abs(S2/tot-1) < 1e-10
print('
edge energies 0-1, 1-2, 2-3, 3-4: %s ; share on edge 1-2: %.1f %%' % (np.round(edges[:4], 4),
100*edges[1]/tot))
wt = np.array([1/3, 2/3]); e12 = np.array([2.25*(4/15), 49*(4/15)]); e01 = np.array([1/3, 1/3])
print('flavour-weighted ratio (stress edge)/(flux edge) in units of kappa_e^2 k^2 : %.2f' % (np.sum(wt*e12)/np
.sum(wt*e01)))
print('E_B for the cut P0 (plasma-coupled monopole): k^2/3 ; for the cut P01 (flux kept, i.e. nu as own fluid)
: 4 k^2/15 ; ratio %.4f' % ((4/15)/(1/3)))
E_B01 = fro2(np.diag([1, 1]+[0]*(lmax-1)) @ (k*M) @ np.diag([0, 0]+[1]*(lmax-1)))
print('numerical E_B(P01) = %.10f ; 4k^2/15 = %.10f' % (E_B01, 4*k*k/15))

print('\n=== E1 self-interacting neutrinos with no momentum exchange, pure radiation (first-max shift of Delta
) ===')
LM = 40
def run(k, Rn, K0, drag, tau0=0.02, tg=None):
n = 4+(LM-1); LS = np.arange(3, LM+1)
def rhs(t, y):
dg, tg_, dn, tn = y[:4]; F = np.zeros(LM+2); F[2:LM+1] = y[4:]; F[LM+1] = (2*LM+1)/(k*t)*F[LM]-F[LM-1]
th = (1-Rn)*tg_+Rn*tn; de = (1-Rn)*dg+Rn*dn; sg = F[2]/2; Dl = de+4*th/(k*k*t)
phi = -1.5*Dl/(k*t)**2; psi = phi-6*Rn*sg/(k*t)**2; dphi = -psi/t+2*th/(k*k*t*t)
K = K0/t; dy = np.empty_like(y)
# a*kappa = K0 * (a H) = K0 / tau
Kd = K if drag else 0.0
dy[0] = -4/3*tg_+4*dphi
dy[1] = k*k*dg/4+k*k*psi-Kd*(Rn/(1-Rn))*(tg_-tn)
dy[2] = -4/3*tn+4*dphi
dy[3] = k*k*(dn/4-sg)+k*k*psi+Kd*(tg_-tn)
dy[4] = 8/15*tn-0.6*k*F[3]-K*F[2]
dy[5:] = k/(2*LS+1)*(LS*F[2:LM]-(LS+1)*F[4:LM+2])-K*F[3:LM+1]
return dy
psi0 = 2/3; d0 = -2*psi0-(2/3)*(k*tau0)**2*psi0; t0 = 0.5*k*k*tau0*psi0
y0 = np.zeros(n); y0[:4] = [d0, t0, d0, t0]
sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (tau0, tg[-1]), y0, method='BDF', t_eval=tg, rtol=1e-10, atol=1e-13)
Y = sol.y; th = (1-Rn)*Y[1]+Rn*Y[3]; de = (1-Rn)*Y[0]+Rn*Y[2]
return de+4*th/(k*k*tg)
def fmax(x, y):
s = -y
for i in range(1, len(s)-1):
if s[i] > s[i-1] and s[i] >= s[i+1]:
A, B, C = s[i-1], s[i], s[i+1]; off = 0.5*(A-C)/(A-2*B+C); return B-0.25*(A-C)*off
tg = np.linspace(0.02, 8.0, 3000); kk = 1.0
m_pf = fmax(tg, run(kk, 0.0, 0.0, False, tg=tg))
for drag, tag in [(False, 'self-interaction only (no momentum exchange with plasma)'), (True, 'drag on the
plasma included (Steve relaxation model)')]:
print(tag)
prev = None; vals = {}
for K0 in [3, 10, 30, 100, 300, 1000, 3000]:
r = fmax(tg, run(kk, 0.409, K0, drag, tg=tg))/m_pf; vals[K0] = 1/r-1
print('
K0 = %5d : first-max ratio %.5f ; implied threshold rise %.4f %% ; rise*K0 = %.3f' % (K0, r,
100*vals[K0], 100*vals[K0]*K0))
if not drag:
mono = all(vals[a] > vals[b] for a, b in zip([3, 10, 30, 100, 300, 1000], [10, 30, 100, 300, 1000,
3000]))
const = abs(vals[1000]*1000/(vals[100]*100)-1)
print('
E1 (a) monotone decrease: %s ; (b) rise*K0 changes by %.1f %% between K0=100 and 1000 -> %s'
% (mono, 100*const, 'PASS' if mono and const < .15 else 'DIES'))
=== C1 non-normality channels ===
relaxation time (all l>=1 equal) kappa_e=1
S_2 = 1.126667 (commutator form 1.126667, edge

B.8 The full operator and the truncated wall
# SCRIPT: OSL-STEVE-B-EP-FULL-OPERATOR
# Question: Steve's neutrino operator L = -i k mu - kappa (I - P0) (momentum given to plasma, so l=1 is damped
too).
# Paper-derived claim to test: the two-moment truncation has its exceptional point at kappa_EP = 2 sqrt(E_B) =
2k/sqrt3
# (critical damping of a pair coupled with weight E_B = k^2/3). Does that EP survive when all l are kept?
# KILL CONDITIONS (written before the run):
# KB1 (truncation control) N=2 EP must come out at 2/sqrt3 = 1.1547 (k=1) to 1e-6, else the code is wrong.
# KB2 the claim 'kappa_EP = 2 sqrt(E_B)' survives in the full operator iff the N=200 value is within 10% of
1.1547.
# KB3 (opposite / inverse reading) the statement 'above kappa_EP the neutrinos are overdamped (no oscillating
mode)' survives
#
iff for N=200 every eigenvalue with Re > -kappa/2... is real for kappa > 1.5*kappa_EP; we report the
least-damped eigenvalue's Im.
import numpy as np
def L(N, kap, k=1.0):
n = np.arange(1, N); a = n / np.sqrt(4.0 * n**2 - 1.0)
# mu P_n = a_{n+1} P_{n+1} + a_n P_{n-1},
orthonormal Legendre
J = np.diag(a, 1) + np.diag(a, -1)
D = np.eye(N); D[0, 0] = 0.0
return -1j * k * J - kap * D
def slowest(N, kap):
ev = np.linalg.eigvals(L(N, kap)); i = np.argmax(ev.real); return ev[i], ev
def ep(N, lo=0.01, hi=6.0):
# smallest kappa such that the least-damped eigenvalue is real (|Im| < 1e-9) and stays real above it
ks = np.linspace(lo, hi, 5901); flag = np.array([abs(slowest(N, x)[0].imag) < 1e-7 for x in ks])
# last kappa at which a non-real least-damped eigenvalue occurs
bad = np.where(~flag)[0]
return ks[bad.max() + 1] if len(bad) and bad.max() + 1 < len(ks) else (ks[0] if len(bad) == 0 else np.nan)
print('2/sqrt3 = %.6f' % (2 / 3**0.5))
res = {}
for N in [2, 3, 5, 10, 40, 200]:
res[N] = ep(N); print('N = %3d : least-damped eigenvalue becomes real (and stays real) for kappa >= %.4f'
% (N, res[N]))
print('KB1: |EP(N=2) - 2/sqrt3| = %.2e' % abs(res[2] - 2 / 3**0.5))
print('KB2: EP(N=200)/(2/sqrt3) = %.3f' % (res[200] / (2 / 3**0.5)))
for kap in [0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 6.0]:
for N in [2, 200]:
lam, ev = slowest(N, kap); print('kappa=%.1f N=%3d least-damped lambda = %+.4f %+.4fi ; diffusion
estimate -k^2/(3 kappa) = %+.4f' % (kap, N, lam.real, lam.imag, -1 / (3 * kap)))

B.9 The annihilation contest
# SCRIPT: OSL-STEVE-C-SPEED-CONTEST-EOS
# Mapping under test (from the paper + Check A): the neutrino rms streaming speed is sqrt(E_B)/k = sqrt(<mu
^2>) = 1/sqrt3 (exact) and the
# plasma sound speed is c_s = sqrt(dp/de). In pure radiation they coincide; during e+- annihilation c_s falls
below 1/sqrt3, so a
# 'speed contest' reading predicts the free-streaming advantage GROWS there. Pre-registered quantitative form
(no fit):
#
shift_pred(T_H) = 0.15 * R_nu(T_H) * G(eps_dec = T_H/T_dec) * f, f = (1 - c_s(T_H)) / (1 - 1/sqrt3)
(
front speed 1 against sound)
#
baseline
= 0.15 * R_nu(T_H) * G(...)
(
no contrast factor)
# Steve's published envelope: relative shift of delta_c is 5.3% to 6.2% at ALL sample masses 3e5 .. 7e7 Msun (
Sec. IV D).
# VALIDATION (must pass first): my plasma p/e minimum must be 0.274 +- 0.003 near T = 0.16 MeV; R_nu = 0.488 (
T>>m_e) and 0.405 (T<<m_e).
# KILL CONDITIONS (written before the run):
# KC0 validation fails -> stop, code not trusted.
# KC1 'contrast enhancement' mapping dies if shift_pred (with f) exceeds 6.5% at any sample mass between 3e6
and 7e7 Msun (G ~ 1 there).
# KC2 opposite reading (the contrast SHRINKS the effect: f' = 1/f) dies if shift_pred(f') is below 5.3% at any
sample mass 3e5..7e7.
import numpy as np
from scipy.integrate import quad
me = 0.511
def fd(T, n): # fermi-dirac integrals for g=4 (e+-), zero chem. potential
pass
def eP(T):
f = lambda p, w: p**2 * w(p) / (np.exp(np.sqrt(p**2 + me**2) / T) + 1)
e = quad(lambda p: p**2 * np.sqrt(p**2 + me**2) / (np.exp(np.sqrt(p**2 + me**2) / T) + 1), 0, 60 * T + 10)
[0] * 4 / (2 * np.pi**2)
P = quad(lambda p: p**4 / np.sqrt(p**2 + me**2) / (np.exp(np.sqrt(p**2 + me**2) / T) + 1), 0, 60 * T + 10)
[0] * 4 / (6 * np.pi**2)
return e + np.pi**2 / 15 * T**4, P + np.pi**2 / 45 * T**4
def sdens(T): e, P = eP(T); return (e + P) / T
s_hi_coef = 2 * np.pi**2 / 45 * 5.5
def Tnu(T): return (sdens(T) / s_hi_coef)**(1 / 3)
def Rnu(T): enu = 6 * 7 / 8 * np.pi**2 / 30 * Tnu(T)**4; e, P = eP(T); return enu / (enu + e)
def cs2(T): h = 1e-4 * T; (e1, P1), (e2, P2) = eP(T - h), eP(T + h); return (P2 - P1) / (e2 - e1)

print('KC0 PASS' if ok0 else 'KC0 FAIL')

e, P = eP(T); enu = 6 * 7 / 8 * np.pi**2 / 30 * Tnu(T)**4; return (e + enu) / (np.pi**2 / 30 * T**4)

B.10 Linear first maximum under gradual decoupling
# SCRIPT: LIN-FS-01 linear free-streaming + relaxation collisions, radiation era, Newtonian gauge (MaBertschinger)
import numpy as np
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
LMAX=36
def build(k,Rnu,eps_dec,r_m=1.0):
n=4+(LMAX-1) # dg,tg,dn,tn,F2..FLMAX
iF=lambda l:4+(l-2)
def A(t):
M=np.zeros((n,n))
kt2=(k*t)**2
# phi, thetaT, deltaT, sigma as linear functionals
dT=np.zeros(n); dT[0]=1-Rnu; dT[2]=Rnu
thT=np.zeros(n); thT[1]=1-Rnu; thT[3]=Rnu
sig=np.zeros(n); sig[iF(2)]=0.5
phi=-1.5/kt2*(dT+4*thT/(k*k*t))
psi=phi-6*Rnu*sig/kt2
phid=-psi/t+2*thT/(t*t*k*k)
aK=0.0 if eps_dec is None else (1.0/t)*((eps_dec*r_m/t)**3)
M[0]=4*phid; M[0,1]+=-4/3.
M[1]=k*k*psi; M[1,0]+=k*k/4
if Rnu>0:
c=aK*Rnu/(1-Rnu); M[1,3]+=c; M[1,1]+=-c
M[2]=4*phid; M[2,3]+=-4/3.
M[3]=k*k*psi; M[3,2]+=k*k/4; M[3]-=k*k*sig; M[3,3]+=-aK; M[3,1]+=aK

54

# F2
M[iF(2),3]+=8/15.; M[iF(2),iF(3)]+=-0.6*k; M[iF(2),iF(2)]+=-aK
for l in range(3,LMAX+1):
r=iF(l)
M[r,iF(l-1)]+=k*l/(2*l+1.)
if l<LMAX: M[r,iF(l+1)]+=-k*(l+1)/(2*l+1.)
else:
# closure F_{L+1}=(2L+1)/(k t) F_L - F_{L-1}
M[r,iF(l)]+=-k*(l+1)/(2*l+1.)*((2*l+1)/(k*t)); M[r,iF(l-1)]+=k*(l+1)/(2*l+1.)
M[r,r]+=-aK
return M,dT,thT,phi
return n,A
def run(k,Rnu,eps_dec,t0,tmax,r_m=1.0,npts=400):
n,A=build(k,Rnu,eps_dec,r_m)
zeta=1.0; psi0=2/3.*zeta
th0=0.5*k*k*t0*psi0
d0=-2*psi0-(2/3.)*(k*t0)**2*psi0
y0=np.zeros(n); y0[0]=d0; y0[1]=th0; y0[2]=d0; y0[3]=th0
f=lambda t,y:A(t)[0]@y
J=lambda t,y:A(t)[0]
ts=np.linspace(t0,tmax,npts)
s=solve_ivp(f,(t0,tmax),y0,method='Radau',jac=J,t_eval=ts,rtol=1e-8,atol=1e-11)
Y=s.y; out={}
dT=(1-Rnu)*Y[0]+Rnu*Y[2]; thT=(1-Rnu)*Y[1]+Rnu*Y[3]
out['t']=s.t
out['Delta']=dT+4*thT/(k*k*s.t)
# zero-momentum slices
out['dpl']=dT+4*Y[1]/(k*k*s.t)
# plasma-orthogonal slices
return out
def firstmax(t,y):
i=np.where((y[1:-1]>y[:-2])&(y[1:-1]>=y[2:]))[0]
if len(i)==0: return None
j=i[0]+1
# parabolic refinement
a,b,c=y[j-1],y[j],y[j+1]
d=0.5*(a-c)/(a-2*b+c); return t[j]+d*(t[1]-t[0]), b-0.25*(a-c)*d
if __name__=="__main__":
# validation: single mode, nu decoupled from start (instant) vs perfect fluid
for k in [1.0]:
for Rnu in [0.409,0.488]:
pf=run(k,0.0,None,0.01/k,12/k)
fs=run(k,Rnu,None,0.01/k,12/k)
for key in ['Delta','dpl']:
m1=firstmax(pf['t'],pf[key]); m2=firstmax(fs['t'],fs[key])
print(k,Rnu,key,m1,m2,'ratio',m2[1]/m1[1])
# SCRIPT: LIN-FS-03 central first-max factor vs gradual decoupling kappa=H(eps_dec/eps)^3 (linear theory,

def job(a):
Rnu,ed,k=a
r=run(k,Rnu,ed,0.1,4.0,npts=len(T))
return (-r['Delta'],-r['dpl'])
def centre(Rnu,ed,pool):
out=pool.map(job,[(Rnu,ed,k) for k in ks])
D=sum(w*k*k*np.exp(-k*k/4.)*o[0] for o,k,w in zip(out,ks,ws))/(2*np.pi**2)
P=sum(w*k*k*np.exp(-k*k/4.)*o[1] for o,k,w in zip(out,ks,ws))/(2*np.pi**2)
return D,P

B.11 Rate-ratio and wall readings
# SCRIPT: CONN-ENERGY-STEVE-04 corrected scaling k/(aH) = k r_m * eps
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import brentq
G=np.array([5.90,5.33,4.20,2.65,1.17,0.39])/6.15; ed=np.array([.25,.5,1,2,4,8])
print('EP crossing (k r_m=2): kappa/H=(edec/eps)^3 equals (2/sqrt3)*2*eps')
print([round(brentq(lambda e:(x/e)**3-(4/np.sqrt(3))*e,1e-3,100),3) for x in ed], ' vs 0.811*edec^0.75 =',np.
round(0.811*ed**0.75,3))
forms={'wall sqrt(1-3kap^2/4k^2)':lambda kap,k:np.sqrt(np.clip(1-0.75*kap**2/k**2,0,1)),
'A k/(k+kappa)':lambda kap,k:k/(k+kap),'B exp(-kappa/k)':lambda kap,k:np.exp(-kap/k),'C 1-exp(-k/kappa)
':lambda kap,k:1-np.exp(-k/kap)}
for lo in [1.0,0.1]:
eps=np.linspace(lo,4,6001)
for kr in [1.0,2.0]:
for name,f in forms.items():
for wname,wt in [('uniform',np.ones_like(eps)),('log',1/eps)]:
pred=np.array([np.sum(f((e/eps)**3,kr*eps)*wt)/np.sum(wt) for e in ed]); err=np.max(np.abs(pred-G))

B.12 Diffusive escape
The folder csl_checks holding this script is named for the author’s Colour Selects the Leader [11].
# SCRIPT: K6-DIFFUSIVE-LEADER-G-CURVE
# STATUS DISCLOSURE: this model was written AFTER the Table II G column had been read (the log-log slope of G
/(1-G) ~ -1.6 suggested the
# exponent); the derivation below is independent of the data except for the choice of decision time eps* and
of the interpolation rule.
# Derivation (all from Steve's Eq.7,10 and the paper's gap-clock reading, Sec.6/8): units r_m = 1, comoving,
conformal time tau = eps.
#
kappa = H (e_dec/e)^3 -> scattering rate a*kappa = sigma(tau) = e_dec^3 / tau^4
[Steve Eq.10 +
gradual-decoupling law]
#
neutrinos (speed 1) diffuse with D = 1/(3 sigma) while sigma*L >> 1, so the NEUTRINO channel's decay rate
(its principal Metzler
#
eigenvalue, K1-H2 above) is gamma ~ D (pi/L)^2 and its displacement by tau is l_diff^2 = c * Int D dtau =
c * tau^5/(15 e_dec^3).
#
Free-streaming (instantaneous decoupling) displaces by l_ball = tau. Series (resistance) interpolation 1/
l = 1/l_diff + 1/l_ball and
#
G := l/l_ball = 1/(1 + l_ball/l_diff) = 1/(1 + sqrt(15/c) (e_dec/e*)^(3/2)).
[c = 1: l^2 = Int D
dtau ; c = 2: 1-D rms ; c = 6: 3-D rms]
# PRE-STATED PASS (this script): max|G_pred - G_pub| <= 0.10 with eps* = 4.0 (Steve's PF apparent-horizon time
) and c = 1. Because c is a convention
#
the other c are reported and the one-parameter best fit is reported so that the size of the freedom is
visible.
# KILL for the exponent claim: a free-exponent fit must give n = 3/2 within its own 2-sigma bootstrap/fit
uncertainty, else the diffusion scaling is dead.
# OPPOSITE: ballistic-limited regime reading G = 1 - l_diff/l_ball clipped, and G = exp(-l_ball/l_diff).
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import least_squares
ed=np.array([.25,.5,1,2,4,8]); G=np.array([.959,.867,.683,.431,.190,.063])
def V1(e,es=4.0,c=1.0): return 1/(1+np.sqrt(15/c)*(e/es)**1.5)
print('published
',G)
for es in (3.5,4.0,4.5):
for c in (1.0,2.0,6.0):
g=V1(ed,es,c); print('V1 e*=%.1f c=%.0f'%(es,c),np.round(g,3),'max|err| %.3f'%np.max(np.abs(g-G)))
# one-parameter fits
r=least_squares(lambda p:1/(1+p[0]*(ed/4.0)**1.5)-G,[3.0]); A=r.x[0]; print('1-param (exponent 3/2, e*=4):
A_fit=%.3f (c-equivalent %.2f) max|err| %.3f'%(A,15/A**2,np.max(np.abs(r.fun))))
r=least_squares(lambda p:1/(1+np.sqrt(15)*(ed/p[0])**1.5)-G,[4.0]); print('1-param (c=1): e*_fit=%.3f max|err|
%.3f'%(r.x[0],np.max(np.abs(r.fun))))
# free exponent with covariance
def res(p): return 1/(1+p[0]*(ed/4.0)**p[1])-G
r=least_squares(res,[3.0,1.5]); J=r.jac; s2=np.sum(r.fun**2)/(len(ed)-2); cov=np.linalg.inv(J.T@J)*s2
print('free exponent fit: A=%.3f+-%.3f n=%.3f+-%.3f max|err| %.3f'%(r.x[0],np.sqrt(cov[0,0]),r.x[1],np.sqrt(
cov[1,1]),np.max(np.abs(r.fun))))

57

print('KILL (exponent): n=1.5 is %.1f sd from fitted n'%(abs(r.x[1]-1.5)/np.sqrt(cov[1,1])))
# opposites
l=np.sqrt(1/15)*(ed/4.0)**-1.5*0+ (4.0**2.5)/np.sqrt(15*ed**3)
# l_diff with c=1, eps*=4
lb=4.0
print('OPP1 G=1-l_diff/l_ball clipped ',np.round(np.clip(1-l/lb,0,1),3),'max|err| %.3f'%np.max(np.abs(np.clip
(1-l/lb,0,1)-G)))
print('OPP2 G=exp(-l_ball/l_diff)
',np.round(np.exp(-lb/l),3),'max|err| %.3f'%np.max(np.abs(np.exp(-lb/l)G)))
print('OPP3 quadrature G=(1+15 x^3)^-1/2',np.round((1+15*(ed/4.0)**3)**-.5,3),'max|err| %.3f'%np.max(np.abs
((1+15*(ed/4.0)**3)**-.5-G)))
# predictions (new, untested)
for es,tag in ((4.0,'Gaussian'),(8.0,'alpha=0.3 (decided at eps~7-9, Steve Sec.III B)')):
print('PREDICT %-48s'%tag,{e:round(float(V1(e,es,1.0)),3) for e in (0.1,1,2,4,8,16)})
# Rnu check: odds linear in R? G is defined as ratio so the model predicts the R_nu-independence of G

Steve ran gradual decoupling only at R=0.409)')

B.13 Tests C and D and the controls: predictions, search script and run logs
# SCRIPT: PREREGISTERED_NEXT (written 2026-10-31T17:09:37Z, before any run below)
Follow-up to "When the Neutrinos Leave" v2. Frozen predictor: kept-sector memory model, two-epoch weight w
=0.890 (fitted on n=3 only),
values taken unchanged from paper/code/main/memory_curve.json (file written 2026-10-30 13:47 CDT, never
refitted).
TEST C (fresh history, out of sample): Gaussian profile (alpha=1), rate kappa/H=(eps_dec/eps)^6, eps_dec=1.
Memory model predicts G = 0.6625. History-blind rule (Steve's n=3 measured G at the same eps_dec, Table II)
= 0.683.
Kill: memory model dies if |G_measured - 0.6625| > 0.08. History-blind dies if |G - 0.683| > 0.08.
(The two are only 0.02 apart here, so this test checks the model's survival, not discrimination.)
TEST D (fresh history, discriminating): Gaussian, n=6, eps_dec=3.
Memory model predicts G = 0.1176. History-blind (Steve n=3, eps_dec interpolated at 3: between 0.431 (2)
and 0.190 (4)) ~ 0.30.
Kill: memory model dies if |G - 0.1176| > 0.08. History-blind dies if |G - 0.30| > 0.08.
CONTROLS on Test B (n=6, eps_dec=2, measured G=0.317+-0.004 at N=400, K=32, kapcap=5e5):
B-res: N=600, K=48. B-cap: kapcap=5e6. Pass if each shifts delta_c by less than 0.0005 (i.e. G by < 0.017)
.
If a control fails, Test B's G is reported as resolution/cap dependent and the 0.071 pass is suspended until
resolved.

58

# SCRIPT: TRISECT3 three-valued two-point bracket search for delta_c (collapse / disperse / undecided-orerror)
# Usage: trisect3.py TAG qshape eps_dec nexp lo hi tol eps_max kapcap N K
# Outcome of one run: 'C' (collapse = status BH), 'D' (disperse = status dispersed), 'U' (anything else:
undecided at eps_max, crash).
# An 'U' point NEVER moves the bracket. It is retried once with eps_max*1.5; if still 'U' it is recorded in '
undecided' and reported.
import sys, json, time, os
from multiprocessing import Pool
sys.path.insert(0, os.environ['SIMDIR']); sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
def classify(status):
return 'C' if status == 'BH' else ('D' if status == 'dispersed' else 'U')
def one(a):
from run_nuc_n3 import run
d, q, ed, n, em, kc, N, K = a
t1 = time.time()
try:
o = run(d, fnu=0.409, N=N, K=K, eps0=0.1, eps_dec=ed, eps_max=em, qshape=q, nexp=n, kapcap=kc)
info = dict(d=d, status=o['status'], eps=float(o['eps']), eps_max=em, rawmax=float(o['rawmax']),
kapcap=kc,
capsteps=int(o['capsteps']), steps=int(o['steps']), eps_cap_last=float(o['eps_cap_last']),
sec=time.time()-t1)
except Exception as ex:
info = dict(d=d, status='error:' + type(ex).__name__ + ':' + str(ex)[:80], eps=float('nan'), eps_max=
em, rawmax=float('nan'),
kapcap=kc, capsteps=-1, steps=-1, eps_cap_last=float('nan'), sec=time.time()-t1)
info['out'] = classify(info['status'])
return info
def fmt(tag, i):
return (f"{tag} delta={i['d']:.5f}: {i['out']} [{i['status']}] eps={i['eps']:.2f} eps_max={i['eps_max']} "
f"rawmax(kappa/H)={i['rawmax']:.3e} cap={i['kapcap']:.1e} capped_steps={i['capsteps']}/{i['steps']
} "
f"cap_released_at_eps~{i['eps_cap_last']:.3f} ({i['sec']:.0f}s)")
def search(tag, q, ed, n, lo, hi, tol, em, kc, N, K, mapper, log_path=None, retry_factor=1.5):
log = []; undecided = []; nonmono = False; t0 = time.time()
while hi - lo > tol:
a = lo + (hi - lo) / 3; b = lo + 2 * (hi - lo) / 3
res = mapper(one, [(a, q, ed, n, em, kc, N, K), (b, q, ed, n, em, kc, N, K)])
for i in res: print(fmt(tag, i), flush=True); log.append(i)
# retry each undecided/error point ONCE with raised eps_max (the other core is used for the second one
)
bad = [k for k, i in enumerate(res) if i['out'] == 'U']
if bad:
rr = mapper(one, [(res[k]['d'], q, ed, n, em * retry_factor, kc, N, K) for k in bad])
for k, i in zip(bad, rr):
i['retry'] = True; print('RETRY ' + fmt(tag, i), flush=True); log.append(i)
if i['out'] == 'U':
undecided.append((i['d'], i['status'], i['eps']))
res[k] = i
ia, ib = res
oa, ob = ia['out'], ib['out']
if oa == 'U' and ob == 'U':
print(f'{tag} STOP: both points undecided/error after retry; bracket [{lo:.5f},{hi:.5f}] unchanged
', flush=True); break
if oa == 'D' and ob == 'C':
# ordinary case, delta_c between a and b
lo, hi = a, b
elif oa == 'C' and ob == 'D':
print(f'{tag} STOP: non-monotonic outcomes (collapse at a, disperse at b); bracket unchanged',
flush=True); nonmono = True; break
elif oa == 'C':
# a collapses (b is C or U): threshold below a
hi = a
elif ob == 'D':
# b disperses (a is D or U): threshold above b
lo = b

59

elif oa == 'D':
# a disperses, b undecided: threshold above a
lo = a
elif ob == 'C':
# b collapses, a undecided: threshold below b
hi = b
if log_path:
json.dump(dict(tag=tag, lo=lo, hi=hi, log=log, undecided=undecided), open(log_path, 'w'))
print(f'{tag} bracket now [{lo:.5f},{hi:.5f}]', flush=True)
res = dict(tag=tag, qshape=q, eps_dec=ed, nexp=n, kapcap=kc, N=N, K=K, lo=lo, hi=hi, dc=0.5 * (lo + hi), w
=0.5 * (hi - lo),
minutes=(time.time() - t0) / 60, undecided=undecided, nonmonotonic=nonmono, log=log)
print(f'RESULT {tag}: delta_c = {0.5*(lo+hi):.5f} +- {0.5*(hi-lo):.5f} bracket [{lo:.5f},{hi:.5f}] ({res["
minutes"]:.1f} min) undecided_points={undecided}', flush=True)
return res
if __name__ == '__main__':
tag = sys.argv[1]; q = float(sys.argv[2]); ed = float(sys.argv[3]); n = float(sys.argv[4])
lo, hi, tol = float(sys.argv[5]), float(sys.argv[6]), float(sys.argv[7]); em = float(sys.argv[8])
kc = float(sys.argv[9]); N = int(sys.argv[10]); K = int(sys.argv[11])
with Pool(2) as p:
res = search(tag, q, ed, n, lo, hi, tol, em, kc, N, K, lambda f, args: p.map(f, args), log_path=f'
state_{tag}.json')
json.dump(res, open(f'res_{tag}.json', 'w'))

B.14 The matched-pair witness
# SCRIPT: PREREGISTERED_WITNESS (preregistration text, not code)
Written: 2026-10-31T17:12:31Z (UTC, before any production run; no witness numbers computed yet)
MODEL (identical to paper/code/main/memory_curve.py)
dv/dtau = -(i k M + sigma(tau)(I-P0)) v, orthonormal Legendre moments v_l, l=0..LM,
M_{l,l-1}=M_{l-1,l}=l/sqrt(4l^2-1), sigma = eps^n / tau^(n+1), n in {3,6}.
k-weights: 36-node Gauss-Legendre on [0.25,9], weight k^2 exp(-k^2/2), normalised.
eps_dec fixed at 0.5 (inside the paper's scanned range) so all plants sit before tau=1.7.
PAIR
base b at tau_s: v0=1, v1=0.2, v2=0.1, rest 0. Pair: b +/- delta e_L, delta=0.05,
hidden plant L=3 (main) and L=4; opposite check L=1, L=2 (retained plant, hidden matched).
Both members scaled by the SAME constant to unit norm (keeps the match; needed for the bound).
Positivity: f(mu)=sum v_l sqrt((2l+1)/2) P_l(mu) >= 0 on a 2001-point mu grid, both members.
tau_s / eps in {0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1, 2} -> tau_s in {0.05, 0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0}.
Readout epochs tau = 1.7, 4.0, late 6.0.
READOUTS (difference = member+ minus member-, exact by linearity = 2 delta U(t,s) e_L)
R_ret = sqrt(sum_k w ||Dv_{0,1,2}||^2) / sqrt(sum_k w ||b_{0,1,2}||^2)
(PRIMARY)
R_A
= |A_w+ - A_w-| / mean(A_w+,A_w-), A_w = sum_k w |v0|^2
(k-weighted monopole)
R_F
= same with |v1|^2
(k-weighted flux)
WITNESS ON at an epoch <=> R_ret > 1e-3 at that epoch.
DERIVED BEFORE RUNNING (block elimination, a=v0, h=(v1,...)):
In the hidden block the collision term is sigma*I (scalar), so EXACTLY
U_Q(t,r) = exp(-(Sig(t)-Sig(r))) exp(-i k (t-r) M_QQ), Sig(t) = -eps^n/(n t^n).
a' = S(t) - k^2 int_s^t K(t,r) a(r) dr, K = M01^2 [U_Q(t,r)]_{11},
S(t) = -i k M01 [U_Q(t,s) h0]_1. The pair has Da(s)=0, Dh0 = 2 delta e_L, so the
difference obeys the same Volterra equation driven ONLY by S[Dh0]: no source, no witness.
PREDICTION (stated before running)
Two channels carry a hidden difference into the retained moments:
(i) remnant: the hidden plant survives decoupling with factor exp(-(eps/tau_s)^n / n)
(int_s^inf sigma converges because collisions die off) and then free-streams: O(delta*remnant);

61

(ii) leakage during coupling through L damped rungs: ~ delta*(k/sigma(tau_s))^L.
Expected witness strength ~ delta*max(remnant, leakage), so with delta=0.05:
tau_s/eps=0.1 : OFF for n=3 and n=6 (remnant e^-333 / e^-1e6, leakage <1e-11)
tau_s/eps=0.25: OFF both (n=3 remnant 7e-10, leakage ~2e-7)
tau_s/eps=0.5 : n=3 ON (remnant e^-2.67=0.07), n=6 OFF or marginal (remnant 2e-5)
tau_s/eps=1,2 : ON both, O(delta)
n=6 suppresses more than n=3 at the same tau_s/eps for tau_s<eps, less (closer to 1) for tau_s>=eps.
Opposite check: deep in coupling L=1 > L=2 > L=3 > L=4 (one factor k/sigma per rung);
after decoupling all four O(delta) and comparable.
KILL / SUCCESS CONDITIONS
K1 (witness dead): R_ret <= 1e-3 at both tau=1.7 and 4.0 for every plant with tau_s >= eps
-> the retained (density/flux/stress) closure suffices; witness killed in this model.
K2 (not converged): for any ON case, LM 60->120 or rtol 1e-9->1e-11 (atol 1e-12->1e-14)
changes R_ret, R_A or R_F by >= 1% -> that entry is not reported as a pass.
K3 (invalid pair): positivity fails for either member -> pair discarded.
K4 (prediction killed): any tau_s/eps<=0.25 entry ON, or any tau_s/eps>=1 entry OFF.
K5 (elimination claim killed): Da computed from the Volterra equation driven by the
independently computed source S (closed-form U_Q, no full-system solve) differs from the
full-system Da by > 1% of max|Da|, or fails to converge with grid refinement.
K6 (speed bound violated): max over all trajectories/times of (A')^2/(4 (k^2/3) A(1-A)) > 1+1e-6.
SUCCESS: K1-K6 all not triggered.
# SCRIPT: PBH-NU-WITNESS-RUN matched retained-state witness in the kept-sector model (same object as
memory_curve.py)
import os, sys, json, time
os.environ['OMP_NUM_THREADS']='1'
import numpy as np, scipy.sparse as sp
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
from scipy.special import roots_legendre, eval_legendre
kn,kw=roots_legendre(36); kmin,kmax=0.25,9.0; ks=0.5*(kmax-kmin)*(kn+1)+kmin; wk=0.5*(kmax-kmin)*kw
wE=wk*ks**2*np.exp(-ks**2/2); wE/=wE.sum()
EPS=0.5; DELTA=0.05; TEP=[1.7,4.0,6.0]
def Mmat(LM):
d=np.array([l/np.sqrt(4.0*l*l-1) for l in range(1,LM+1)])
return sp.diags([d,d],[-1,1],format='csr')
def evolve(k,n,ts,LM,rtol,atol,cols):
N=LM+1; M=Mmat(LM); Q=sp.diags(np.r_[0.0,np.ones(LM)],format='csr'); I=sp.identity(len(cols),format='csr')
def A(t): return (-1j*k*M - (EPS**n/t**(n+1))*Q)
def rhs(t,y): return (A(t)@y.reshape(len(cols),N).T).T.ravel()
def jac(t,y): return sp.kron(I,A(t),format='csc')
Y0=np.zeros((len(cols),N),complex)
for j,c in enumerate(cols): Y0[j,:len(c)]=c
tg=np.unique(np.r_[np.geomspace(ts,6.0,500),TEP])
s=solve_ivp(rhs,(ts,6.0),Y0.ravel(),method='BDF',t_eval=tg,jac=jac,rtol=rtol,atol=atol)
assert s.success, s.message
return s.t, s.y.reshape(len(cols),N,-1)
# [col, l, t]
def positivity(v):
mu=np.linspace(-1,1,2001)
return float(min(sum(v[l]*np.sqrt((2*l+1)/2)*eval_legendre(l,mu) for l in range(len(v)))))
def run(n,ratio,L,LM=60,rtol=1e-9,atol=1e-12):
ts=ratio*EPS; b=np.array([1.0,0.2,0.1,0,0]); e=np.zeros(5); e[L]=1
c=1/np.sqrt(b@b+DELTA**2)
pos=(positivity(c*(b+DELTA*e)),positivity(c*(b-DELTA*e)))
rows={tt:dict(dret=0.0,bret=0.0,Ap=0.0,Am=0.0,Fp=0.0,Fm=0.0) for tt in TEP}
worst=0.0; worstN=0.0
for k,w in zip(ks,wE):
t,Y=evolve(k,n,ts,LM,rtol,atol,[c*b,c*DELTA*e])
U_b,U_d=Y[0],Y[1]
for sgn in (1,-1):
v=U_b+sgn*U_d; A=np.abs(v[0])**2; Ap=2*np.real(np.conj(v[0])*(-1j*k*(1/np.sqrt(3))*v[1]))
Nrm=np.sum(np.abs(v)**2,axis=0)
den=4*(k*k/3)*A*(1-A); ok=den>1e-300
worst=max(worst,float(np.max(Ap[ok]**2/den[ok])))
denN=4*(k*k/3)*A*(Nrm-A); ok=denN>1e-300
worstN=max(worstN,float(np.max(Ap[ok]**2/denN[ok])))
for tt in TEP:
i=np.argmin(abs(t-tt)); r=rows[tt]

62

r['dret']+=w*np.sum(np.abs(2*U_d[:3,i])**2); r['bret']+=w*np.sum(np.abs(U_b[:3,i])**2)
vp=U_b[:,i]+U_d[:,i]; vm=U_b[:,i]-U_d[:,i]
r['Ap']+=w*abs(vp[0])**2; r['Am']+=w*abs(vm[0])**2; r['Fp']+=w*abs(vp[1])**2; r['Fm']+=w*abs(vm
[1])**2
res={}
for tt in TEP:
r=rows[tt]
res[str(tt)]=dict(R_ret=float(np.sqrt(r['dret']/r['bret'])),
R_A=float(abs(r['Ap']-r['Am'])/(0.5*(r['Ap']+r['Am']))),
R_F=float(abs(r['Fp']-r['Fm'])/(0.5*(r['Fp']+r['Fm']))))
return dict(n=n,ratio=ratio,tau_s=ts,L=L,LM=LM,rtol=rtol,pos_min=pos,bound_worst=worst,bound_worst_N=
worstN,res=res)
if __name__=='__main__':
mode=sys.argv[1]; out=[]
if mode=='prod':
jobs=[(n,r,L) for n in (3,6) for r in (0.1,0.25,0.5,1,2) for L in (3,4,1,2)]
kw_={}
else:
jobs=[(n,r,L) for n in (3,6) for r in (0.5,1,2) for L in (3,4)]
kw_=dict(LM=120) if mode=='convLM' else dict(rtol=1e-11,atol=1e-14)
for j in jobs:
t0=time.time(); d=run(*j,**kw_); out.append(d)
print(j,'%.1fs'%(time.time()-t0),' '.join('%s:%.3e/%.3e/%.3e'%(tt,v['R_ret'],v['R_A'],v['R_F']) for tt
,v in d['res'].items()),
'pos %.3f %.3f bound %.6f %.6f'%(*d['pos_min'],d['bound_worst'],d['bound_worst_N']),flush=True)
json.dump(out,open('witness_%s.json'%mode,'w'),indent=1)
# SCRIPT: PBH-NU-WITNESS-KERNEL exact block elimination: hidden source S = -ik B U_Q(t,s) Dh0 drives the
whole retained difference
import os; os.environ['OMP_NUM_THREADS']='1'
import numpy as np, json, scipy.sparse as sp
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
EPS=0.5; DELTA=0.05; LM=60; N=LM+1
M=np.zeros((N,N))
for l in range(1,N): M[l,l-1]=M[l-1,l]=l/np.sqrt(4.0*l*l-1)
MQQ=M[1:,1:]; lam,V=np.linalg.eigh(MQQ); M01=M[0,1]
Sig=lambda t,n: -EPS**n/(n*t**n)
# Sig' = sigma
def full_Da(k,n,ts,L,tg,c):
Q=np.diag(np.r_[0.0,np.ones(LM)])
A=lambda t: -1j*k*M-(EPS**n/t**(n+1))*Q
y0=np.zeros(N,complex); y0[L]=2*DELTA*c
s=solve_ivp(lambda t,y:A(t)@y,(ts,tg[-1]),y0,method='BDF',jac=lambda t,y:A(t),t_eval=tg,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e
-15)
return s.y[0]
def memory_Da(k,n,ts,L,tg,c):
# closed-form hidden propagator, no full-system solve
h=tg[1]-tg[0]; Nt=len(tg); Sg=Sig(tg,n)
Dh0=np.zeros(LM,complex); Dh0[L-1]=2*DELTA*c
coef=V.T@Dh0
S=-1j*k*M01*np.exp(-(Sg-Sg[0]))*((V[0,:]*coef)[None,:]*np.exp(-1j*k*np.outer(tg-ts,lam))).sum(1)
g=M01**2*((V[0,:]**2)[None,:]*np.exp(-1j*k*np.outer(np.arange(Nt)*h,lam))).sum(1)
# g(t-r)
a=np.zeros(Nt,complex); F=np.zeros(Nt,complex); F[0]=S[0]
for i in range(Nt-1):
j=np.arange(i+2); Kr=np.exp(-(Sg[i+1]-Sg[j]))*g[i+1-j]
w=np.full(i+2,h); w[0]=w[-1]=h/2
Jknown=np.sum((w*Kr*a[j])[:-1])
# a_{i+1} = a_i + h/2 (F_i + S_{i+1} - k^2 (Jknown + w_last K_ii a_{i+1}))
a[i+1]=(a[i]+h/2*(F[i]+S[i+1]-k*k*Jknown))/(1+h/2*k*k*w[-1]*Kr[-1])
F[i+1]=S[i+1]-k*k*(Jknown+w[-1]*Kr[-1]*a[i+1])
Sint=np.concatenate([[0],np.cumsum(h/2*(S[1:]+S[:-1]))])
return a,Sint
out=[]
b=np.array([1.0,0.2,0.1]); c=1/np.sqrt(b@b+DELTA**2)
for (n,ratio,L,k) in [(3,1,3,2.0),(3,0.5,3,1.5),(6,1,4,3.0),(6,2,3,1.0)]:
ts=ratio*EPS; row=dict(n=n,ratio=ratio,L=L,k=k)
for h in (4e-3,2e-3,1e-3):
tg=ts+np.arange(int(round((4.0-ts)/h))+1)*h
ref=full_Da(k,n,ts,L,tg,c); a,Sint=memory_Da(k,n,ts,L,tg,c)

63

err=np.max(abs(a-ref))/np.max(abs(ref))
row['h=%g'%h]=dict(rel_err=float(err),max_Da=float(np.max(abs(ref))),
src_only_rel=float(np.max(abs(Sint-ref))/np.max(abs(ref))))
print(n,ratio,L,k,h,'max|Da|=%.3e memory-vs-full rel err=%.2e (source integral alone, no kernel: %.2
e)'%(np.max(abs(ref)),err,row['h=%g'%h]['src_only_rel']),flush=True)
out.append(row)
json.dump(out,open('witness_kernel.json','w'),indent=1)
# SCRIPT: PBH-NU-WITNESS-CONV compare production vs LM=120 and tight-tolerance runs (K2)
import json

worst=0
for mode in ('convLM','convTol'):

for tt in ('1.7','4.0','6.0'):

worst=max(worst,max(ch)); print(mode,d['n'],d['ratio'],d['L'],'max rel change %.2e'%max(ch),'bound %.6
f'%d['bound_worst'])
print('WORST',worst)

B.15 The flux–shear link and the neutrino fraction: predictions, changed solvers,
drivers, run logs
Every prediction and kill line of Section 9 was written before the run it judges; the full preregistration file, with each result appended under its test and including the intermediate readings
that the final runs superseded, is code/v4_runs/PREREGISTERED_V4.txt in the archive and on
GitHub. The final runs are the ones listed here. The changed solvers: McCormick’s ms_nuc.py
(MIT licence, copyright S. McCormick; the licence file ships with the archive) with the collision
term split, and the shear-only variant; each differs from McCormick’s file in its header line, the
signature of rhs, the lines that compute the discrete projection and κ2 , and the collision term.
The runners label a breakdown after 2M/R > 0.95 separately from a detected horizon and log the
minimum intensity; the driver moves a bracket only on a detected horizon or a dispersal.
# SCRIPT: NU-V4-MS-NUC-SPLIT McCormick ms_nuc.py with ONE change: flux relaxes at kap, l>=2 at kap2 = rr*kap
(dipole split off by discrete projection)
"""
Misner-Sharp perfect fluid (p = e/3) + massless free-streaming neutrinos (energy-integrated intensity
I(t, r, mu) in the fluid frame, finite volume in (r, mu), MUSCL/MC slopes, upwind). Fluid-comoving gauge.
Transport (non-conservative form for I, using dV/dt = A theta V with V = int R^2 B dr):
dI/dt = -A th I - [F_r(i+1)-F_r(i)]/V - [F_mu(k+1/2)-F_mu(k-1/2)]/(dmu V) - A (U/R + mu a + mu^2 S) I
F_r = A R^2 mu I (edges), F_mu = A (1-mu^2) [G1 - a V - mu S V] I, G1 = (R_{i+1}^2 - R_i^2)/2
"""
import numpy as np
from numba import njit

67

PI = np.pi
@njit(cache=True)
def rho_b(t):
return 3.0/(32.0*PI*t*t)
@njit(cache=True)
def mc(dm, dp):
if dm*dp <= 0.0:
return 0.0
s = 1.0 if dm > 0 else -1.0
return s*min(2*abs(dm), 2*abs(dp), 0.5*abs(dm+dp))
@njit(cache=True)
def state(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, fnu, kap):
"""Derived quantities. I has shape (N, K)."""
N, K = I.shape
dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]
eps = np.zeros(N); q = np.zeros(N); Pr = np.zeros(N)
for i in range(N):
s0 = 0.0; s1 = 0.0; s2 = 0.0
for k in range(K):
m0 = mue[k]; m1 = mue[k+1]
s0 += I[i, k]
s1 += I[i, k]*0.5*(m0+m1)
s2 += I[i, k]*(m1**3-m0**3)/(3*dmu)
eps[i] = 2*PI*s0*dmu; q[i] = 2*PI*s1*dmu; Pr[i] = 2*PI*s2*dmu
M = np.zeros(N+1)
for i in range(N):
dV = (4.0*PI/3.0)*(R[i+1]**3 - R[i]**3)
Ubar = 0.5*(U[i]+U[i+1]); Gbar = 0.5*(Gam[i]+Gam[i+1])
M[i+1] = M[i] + dV*(e[i] + eps[i] + q[i]*Ubar/Gbar)
eb = (1.0-fnu)*rho_b(t)
Ac = np.empty(N); Ae = np.empty(N+1); p = np.empty(N)
for i in range(N):
p[i] = e[i]/3.0
# fluid acceleration a = (-Gam dp/dR + kap q)/(e+p) at edges (0 at the centre; outer edge: drag only)
ae = np.zeros(N+1)
for i in range(1, N):
Rc0 = 0.5*(R[i-1]+R[i]); Rc1 = 0.5*(R[i]+R[i+1])
dpdR = (p[i]-p[i-1])/(Rc1-Rc0)
ae[i] = (-Gam[i]*dpdR + kap*0.5*(q[i-1]+q[i]))/(0.5*(e[i-1]+p[i-1]+e[i]+p[i]))
ae[N] = kap*q[N-1]/(e[N-1]+p[N-1])
# lapse: A = (eb/e)^(1/4) at the outer cell, then d ln A = a * (proper distance) inward
Ac[N-1] = (eb/e[N-1])**0.25
for i in range(N-2, -1, -1):
Rc0 = 0.5*(R[i]+R[i+1]); Rc1 = 0.5*(R[i+1]+R[i+2])
Ac[i] = Ac[i+1]*np.exp(-ae[i+1]*(Rc1-Rc0)/Gam[i+1])
Ae[0] = Ac[0]; Ae[N] = Ac[N-1]
for i in range(1, N):
Ae[i] = np.sqrt(Ac[i-1]*Ac[i])
return eps, q, Pr, M, p, Ac, Ae, ae
@njit(cache=True)
def rhs(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, fnu, Ib_out, kap, rr, dR, dUt, dG, de, dI):
N, K = I.shape
dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]
eps, q, Pr, M, p, Ac, Ae, ae = state(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, fnu, kap)
# ---- fluid + metric ---dR[0] = 0.0; dUt[0] = 0.0; dG[0] = 0.0
for i in range(1, N+1):
if i < N:
pe = 0.5*(p[i-1]+p[i]); Pre = 0.5*(Pr[i-1]+Pr[i]); qe = 0.5*(q[i-1]+q[i])
else:
pe = p[N-1]; Pre = Pr[N-1]; qe = q[N-1]
dR[i] = Ae[i]*U[i]
dUt[i] = Ae[i]*(-M[i]/R[i]**2 - 4.0*PI*R[i]*(pe+Pre) + Gam[i]*ae[i])

68

dG[i] = Ae[i]*(4.0*PI*R[i]*qe + ae[i]*U[i])
# D_t Gamma = 4 pi R q + a U (evolved: avoids U^2 - 2
M/R cancellation)
th = np.empty(N); Gc = np.empty(N); V = np.empty(N); S = np.empty(N); ac = np.empty(N); UR = np.empty(N)
for i in range(N):
dR3 = R[i+1]**3 - R[i]**3
Rc = 0.5*(R[i]+R[i+1]); Gc[i] = 0.5*(Gam[i]+Gam[i+1])
th[i] = 3.0*(R[i+1]**2*U[i+1] - R[i]**2*U[i])/dR3 - 4.0*PI*Rc*q[i]/Gc[i]
de[i] = -Ac[i]*(e[i]+p[i])*th[i]
V[i] = dR3/(3.0*Gc[i])
UR[i] = (U[i+1]+U[i])/(R[i+1]+R[i])
S[i] = (U[i+1]-U[i])/(R[i+1]-R[i]) - 4.0*PI*Rc*q[i]/Gc[i] - UR[i]
ac[i] = 0.5*(ae[i]+ae[i+1])
# ---- neutrinos ---# radial fluxes at edges
Fr = np.zeros((N+1, K))
for k in range(K):
mu = 0.5*(mue[k]+mue[k+1])
for i in range(1, N+1):
if mu > 0:
j = i-1
if j >= 1 and j <= N-2:
sl = mc(I[j, k]-I[j-1, k], I[j+1, k]-I[j, k])
else:
sl = 0.0
Iface = I[j, k] + 0.5*sl
else:
if i == N:
Iface = Ib_out
else:
j = i
if j >= 1 and j <= N-2:
sl = mc(I[j, k]-I[j-1, k], I[j+1, k]-I[j, k])
else:
sl = 0.0
Iface = I[j, k] - 0.5*sl
Fr[i, k] = Ae[i]*R[i]**2*mu*Iface
smu2 = 0.0
for k in range(K):
mk = 0.5*(mue[k]+mue[k+1]); smu2 += mk*mk*dmu
kap2 = rr*kap
for i in range(N):
G1 = 0.5*(R[i+1]**2 - R[i]**2)
# angular fluxes at interior bin edges
Fm = np.zeros(K+1)
for k in range(1, K):
m = mue[k]
c = Ac[i]*(1.0-m*m)*(G1 - ac[i]*V[i] - m*S[i]*V[i])
if c > 0:
sl = mc(I[i, k-1]-I[i, k-2], I[i, k]-I[i, k-1]) if k-1 >= 1 else 0.0
Iface = I[i, k-1] + 0.5*sl
else:
sl = mc(I[i, k]-I[i, k-1], I[i, k+1]-I[i, k]) if k+1 <= K-1 else 0.0
Iface = I[i, k] - 0.5*sl
Fm[k] = c*Iface
for k in range(K):
mu = 0.5*(mue[k]+mue[k+1]); mu2 = (mue[k+1]**3-mue[k]**3)/(3*dmu)
dI[i, k] = (-Ac[i]*th[i]*I[i, k] - (Fr[i+1, k]-Fr[i, k])/V[i] - (Fm[k+1]-Fm[k])/(dmu*V[i])
- Ac[i]*(UR[i] + mu*ac[i] + mu2*S[i])*I[i, k]
+ Ac[i]*(kap2*(eps[i]/(4*PI) - I[i, k]) + (kap2-kap)*q[i]*mu/(2*PI*smu2)))
return M, Ac, ac, S, V
@njit(cache=True)
def timestep(R, U, e, I, mue, Gam, Ac, ac, S, cfl_r, cfl_mu):
N, K = I.shape
dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]
dt = 1e30
for i in range(N):
dRc = R[i+1]-R[i]; Gc = 0.5*(Gam[i]+Gam[i+1])

69

dt = min(dt, cfl_r*dRc/(abs(Gc)*Ac[i]))
dR3 = R[i+1]**3 - R[i]**3
V = dR3/(3.0*Gc); G1 = 0.5*(R[i+1]**2 - R[i]**2)
cmax = Ac[i]*(abs(G1/V) + abs(ac[i]) + abs(S[i]))
dt = min(dt, cfl_mu*dmu/cmax)
return dt
# SCRIPT: NU-V4-MS-NUC-SHEAR McCormick ms_nuc.py with ONE change: only the l = 2 (shear) part relaxes at kap2
= rr*kap; flux and l >= 3 at kap
"""
Misner-Sharp perfect fluid (p = e/3) + massless free-streaming neutrinos (energy-integrated intensity
I(t, r, mu) in the fluid frame, finite volume in (r, mu), MUSCL/MC slopes, upwind). Fluid-comoving gauge.
Transport (non-conservative form for I, using dV/dt = A theta V with V = int R^2 B dr):
dI/dt = -A th I - [F_r(i+1)-F_r(i)]/V - [F_mu(k+1/2)-F_mu(k-1/2)]/(dmu V) - A (U/R + mu a + mu^2 S) I
F_r = A R^2 mu I (edges), F_mu = A (1-mu^2) [G1 - a V - mu S V] I, G1 = (R_{i+1}^2 - R_i^2)/2
"""
import numpy as np
from numba import njit
PI = np.pi
@njit(cache=True)
def rho_b(t):
return 3.0/(32.0*PI*t*t)
@njit(cache=True)
def mc(dm, dp):
if dm*dp <= 0.0:
return 0.0
s = 1.0 if dm > 0 else -1.0
return s*min(2*abs(dm), 2*abs(dp), 0.5*abs(dm+dp))
@njit(cache=True)
def state(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, fnu, kap):
"""Derived quantities. I has shape (N, K)."""
N, K = I.shape
dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]
eps = np.zeros(N); q = np.zeros(N); Pr = np.zeros(N)
for i in range(N):
s0 = 0.0; s1 = 0.0; s2 = 0.0
for k in range(K):
m0 = mue[k]; m1 = mue[k+1]
s0 += I[i, k]
s1 += I[i, k]*0.5*(m0+m1)
s2 += I[i, k]*(m1**3-m0**3)/(3*dmu)
eps[i] = 2*PI*s0*dmu; q[i] = 2*PI*s1*dmu; Pr[i] = 2*PI*s2*dmu
M = np.zeros(N+1)
for i in range(N):
dV = (4.0*PI/3.0)*(R[i+1]**3 - R[i]**3)
Ubar = 0.5*(U[i]+U[i+1]); Gbar = 0.5*(Gam[i]+Gam[i+1])
M[i+1] = M[i] + dV*(e[i] + eps[i] + q[i]*Ubar/Gbar)
eb = (1.0-fnu)*rho_b(t)
Ac = np.empty(N); Ae = np.empty(N+1); p = np.empty(N)
for i in range(N):
p[i] = e[i]/3.0
# fluid acceleration a = (-Gam dp/dR + kap q)/(e+p) at edges (0 at the centre; outer edge: drag only)
ae = np.zeros(N+1)
for i in range(1, N):
Rc0 = 0.5*(R[i-1]+R[i]); Rc1 = 0.5*(R[i]+R[i+1])
dpdR = (p[i]-p[i-1])/(Rc1-Rc0)
ae[i] = (-Gam[i]*dpdR + kap*0.5*(q[i-1]+q[i]))/(0.5*(e[i-1]+p[i-1]+e[i]+p[i]))
ae[N] = kap*q[N-1]/(e[N-1]+p[N-1])
# lapse: A = (eb/e)^(1/4) at the outer cell, then d ln A = a * (proper distance) inward
Ac[N-1] = (eb/e[N-1])**0.25
for i in range(N-2, -1, -1):
Rc0 = 0.5*(R[i]+R[i+1]); Rc1 = 0.5*(R[i+1]+R[i+2])
Ac[i] = Ac[i+1]*np.exp(-ae[i+1]*(Rc1-Rc0)/Gam[i+1])
Ae[0] = Ac[0]; Ae[N] = Ac[N-1]

70

for i in range(1, N):
Ae[i] = np.sqrt(Ac[i-1]*Ac[i])
return eps, q, Pr, M, p, Ac, Ae, ae
@njit(cache=True)
def rhs(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, fnu, Ib_out, kap, rr, dR, dUt, dG, de, dI):
N, K = I.shape
dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]
eps, q, Pr, M, p, Ac, Ae, ae = state(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, fnu, kap)
# ---- fluid + metric ---dR[0] = 0.0; dUt[0] = 0.0; dG[0] = 0.0
for i in range(1, N+1):
if i < N:
pe = 0.5*(p[i-1]+p[i]); Pre = 0.5*(Pr[i-1]+Pr[i]); qe = 0.5*(q[i-1]+q[i])
else:
pe = p[N-1]; Pre = Pr[N-1]; qe = q[N-1]
dR[i] = Ae[i]*U[i]
dUt[i] = Ae[i]*(-M[i]/R[i]**2 - 4.0*PI*R[i]*(pe+Pre) + Gam[i]*ae[i])
dG[i] = Ae[i]*(4.0*PI*R[i]*qe + ae[i]*U[i])
# D_t Gamma = 4 pi R q + a U (evolved: avoids U^2 - 2
M/R cancellation)
th = np.empty(N); Gc = np.empty(N); V = np.empty(N); S = np.empty(N); ac = np.empty(N); UR = np.empty(N)
for i in range(N):
dR3 = R[i+1]**3 - R[i]**3
Rc = 0.5*(R[i]+R[i+1]); Gc[i] = 0.5*(Gam[i]+Gam[i+1])
th[i] = 3.0*(R[i+1]**2*U[i+1] - R[i]**2*U[i])/dR3 - 4.0*PI*Rc*q[i]/Gc[i]
de[i] = -Ac[i]*(e[i]+p[i])*th[i]
V[i] = dR3/(3.0*Gc[i])
UR[i] = (U[i+1]+U[i])/(R[i+1]+R[i])
S[i] = (U[i+1]-U[i])/(R[i+1]-R[i]) - 4.0*PI*Rc*q[i]/Gc[i] - UR[i]
ac[i] = 0.5*(ae[i]+ae[i+1])
# ---- neutrinos ---# radial fluxes at edges
Fr = np.zeros((N+1, K))
for k in range(K):
mu = 0.5*(mue[k]+mue[k+1])
for i in range(1, N+1):
if mu > 0:
j = i-1
if j >= 1 and j <= N-2:
sl = mc(I[j, k]-I[j-1, k], I[j+1, k]-I[j, k])
else:
sl = 0.0
Iface = I[j, k] + 0.5*sl
else:
if i == N:
Iface = Ib_out
else:
j = i
if j >= 1 and j <= N-2:
sl = mc(I[j, k]-I[j-1, k], I[j+1, k]-I[j, k])
else:
sl = 0.0
Iface = I[j, k] - 0.5*sl
Fr[i, k] = Ae[i]*R[i]**2*mu*Iface
smu2 = 0.0
for k in range(K):
mk = 0.5*(mue[k]+mue[k+1]); smu2 += mk*mk*dmu
c2 = smu2/2.0
# P2_k = mu_k^2 - c2 is orthogonal to 1 and to mu_k on the midpoint
bins
sp2 = 0.0
for k in range(K):
mk = 0.5*(mue[k]+mue[k+1]); sp2 += (mk*mk - c2)**2*dmu
kap2 = rr*kap
for i in range(N):
G1 = 0.5*(R[i+1]**2 - R[i]**2)
# angular fluxes at interior bin edges
Fm = np.zeros(K+1)
for k in range(1, K):

71

m = mue[k]
c = Ac[i]*(1.0-m*m)*(G1 - ac[i]*V[i] - m*S[i]*V[i])
if c > 0:
sl = mc(I[i, k-1]-I[i, k-2], I[i, k]-I[i, k-1]) if k-1 >= 1 else 0.0
Iface = I[i, k-1] + 0.5*sl
else:
sl = mc(I[i, k]-I[i, k-1], I[i, k+1]-I[i, k]) if k+1 <= K-1 else 0.0
Iface = I[i, k] - 0.5*sl
Fm[k] = c*Iface
a2 = 0.0
for k in range(K):
mk = 0.5*(mue[k]+mue[k+1]); a2 += I[i, k]*(mk*mk - c2)*dmu
a2 = a2/sp2
for k in range(K):
mu = 0.5*(mue[k]+mue[k+1]); mu2 = (mue[k+1]**3-mue[k]**3)/(3*dmu)
dI[i, k] = (-Ac[i]*th[i]*I[i, k] - (Fr[i+1, k]-Fr[i, k])/V[i] - (Fm[k+1]-Fm[k])/(dmu*V[i])
- Ac[i]*(UR[i] + mu*ac[i] + mu2*S[i])*I[i, k]
+ Ac[i]*(kap*(eps[i]/(4*PI) - I[i, k]) + (kap-kap2)*a2*(mu*mu - c2)))
return M, Ac, ac, S, V
@njit(cache=True)
def timestep(R, U, e, I, mue, Gam, Ac, ac, S, cfl_r, cfl_mu):
N, K = I.shape
dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]
dt = 1e30
for i in range(N):
dRc = R[i+1]-R[i]; Gc = 0.5*(Gam[i]+Gam[i+1])
dt = min(dt, cfl_r*dRc/(abs(Gc)*Ac[i]))
dR3 = R[i+1]**3 - R[i]**3
V = dR3/(3.0*Gc); G1 = 0.5*(R[i+1]**2 - R[i]**2)
cmax = Ac[i]*(abs(G1/V) + abs(ac[i]) + abs(S[i]))
dt = min(dt, cfl_mu*dmu/cmax)
return dt
# SCRIPT: NU-V4-RUN-SPLIT-V2 run_nuc_n.py importing ms_nuc_split and passing rr; nothing else changed
import numpy as np, time
from ms_nuc_split import rhs, state, timestep, rho_b
from ms_fluid import initial_data, make_grid
def run(delta_m, fnu=0.409, N=400, K=32, rmax=9.0, beta=3.0, eps0=0.1, cfl_r=0.4, cfl_mu=0.4, cH=0.005,
eps_max=30.0, qshape=1.0, snap_eps=(), hist_every=20, stop_rule=True, disperse_rule=True, kap=0.0,
eps_dec=None, nexp=3, kapcap=5.0e5, rr=1.0):
r = make_grid(N, rmax, beta)
t0, R, U, rho = initial_data(r, delta_m, eps0, 0.0, qshape)
e = (1-fnu)*rho
mue = np.linspace(-1, 1, K+1)
I = np.repeat((fnu*rho/(4*np.pi))[:, None], K, axis=1)
Mi = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum((4*np.pi/3)*np.diff(R**3)*rho)])
Gam = np.ones(N+1); Gam[1:] = np.sqrt(1.0 + U[1:]**2 - 2*Mi[1:]/R[1:])
t = t0
dR = np.zeros(N+1); dU = np.zeros(N+1); dG = np.zeros(N+1); de = np.zeros(N); dI = np.zeros((N, K))
Imin = float(I.min()); mtwo = 0.0
peakC = -1.0; hist = []; snaps = {}; step = 0; status = 'undecided'
snap_eps = list(snap_eps)
while True:
Ib = fnu/(1-fnu)*e[-1]/(4*np.pi)
# incoming background tied to the outer cell's fluid density
if eps_dec is not None:
kap = (1/(2*t))*min((eps_dec/(2*np.sqrt(t*t0)))**nexp, kapcap)
try:
M, Ac, ac, S, V = rhs(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, fnu, Ib, kap, rr, dR, dU, dG, de, dI)
except (ZeroDivisionError, FloatingPointError):
status = 'crash'; break
epsn = 2*np.sqrt(t*t0)
twoMR = 2*M[1:]/R[1:]
C = 2*(M[1:] - (4*np.pi/3)*rho_b(t)*R[1:]**3)/R[1:]
iC = np.argmax(C); peakC = max(peakC, C[iC])
if step % hist_every == 0:
dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]

72

inner = r[1:] < 3.0
hcv = np.max(np.abs(Gam[1:]**2 - 1 - U[1:]**2 + twoMR)[inner])
# Hamiltonian-constraint check
hist.append((t, epsn, C[iC], r[1+iC], e[0]/rho_b(t), 2*np.pi*I[0].sum()*dmu/rho_b(t),
e[-1]/rho_b(t), 2*np.pi*I[-1].sum()*dmu/rho_b(t), hcv))
if snap_eps and epsn >= snap_eps[0]:
snaps[snap_eps.pop(0)] = dict(t=t, eps=epsn, R=R.copy(), U=U.copy(), e=e.copy(), I=I.copy(), M=M.
copy())
if stop_rule:
if np.any((twoMR >= 1.0) & (U[1:] < 0)):
status = 'BH'; break
if disperse_rule and epsn > 3.0 and C[iC] < 0.5*peakC:
status = 'dispersed'; break
if epsn > eps_max: break
try:
dt = min(timestep(R, U, e, I, mue, Gam, Ac, ac, S, cfl_r, cfl_mu), cH*2*t, (0.5/(kap*max(1.0, rr)*
np.max(Ac)) if kap > 0 else 1e30))
# SSP RK3 (the outer-boundary intensity is evaluated at each stage time)
R1 = R + dt*dR; U1 = U + dt*dU; G1 = Gam + dt*dG; e1 = e + dt*de; I1 = I + dt*dI
rhs(t+dt, R1, U1, G1, e1, I1, mue, fnu, fnu/(1-fnu)*e1[-1]/(4*np.pi), kap, rr, dR, dU, dG, de, dI)
R2 = 0.75*R + 0.25*(R1+dt*dR); U2 = 0.75*U + 0.25*(U1+dt*dU); G2 = 0.75*Gam + 0.25*(G1+dt*dG)
e2 = 0.75*e + 0.25*(e1+dt*de); I2 = 0.75*I + 0.25*(I1+dt*dI)
rhs(t+0.5*dt, R2, U2, G2, e2, I2, mue, fnu, fnu/(1-fnu)*e2[-1]/(4*np.pi), kap, rr, dR, dU, dG, de,
dI)
R = R/3 + 2/3*(R2+dt*dR); U = U/3 + 2/3*(U2+dt*dU); Gam = Gam/3 + 2/3*(G2+dt*dG)
e = e/3 + 2/3*(e2+dt*de); I = I/3 + 2/3*(I2+dt*dI)
except (ZeroDivisionError, FloatingPointError):
coll = U[1:] < 0
status = 'BH_breakdown' if (coll.any() and np.max(twoMR[coll]) > 0.95) else 'crash'
# v2:
inferred, not a horizon
# breakdown just before collapse
break
t += dt; step += 1
Imin = min(Imin, float(I.min())); mtwo = max(mtwo, float(np.max(twoMR)))
if np.any(e <= 0) or not np.all(np.isfinite(U)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(I)):
status = 'crash'; break
return dict(Imin=Imin, max2MR=mtwo, status=status, t=t, t0=t0, eps=2*np.sqrt(t*t0), steps=step, peakC=
peakC, hist=np.array(hist),
snaps=snaps, r=r, mue=mue, R=R, U=U, e=e, I=I, Gam=Gam)
if __name__ == '__main__':
t1 = time.time()
out = run(0.0, fnu=0.409, N=200, K=16, eps_max=5.0, stop_rule=False)
t = out['t']; Ib = 0.409*rho_b(t)/(4*np.pi); eb = 0.591*rho_b(t)
print(f"FRW test to eps={out['eps']:.2f} in {out['steps']} steps ({time.time()-t1:.1f}s)")
print(" max|I/Ib-1| =", np.abs(out['I']/Ib-1).max(), " max|e/eb-1| =", np.abs(out['e']/eb-1).max())
a = np.sqrt(t/out['t0']); print(" max|R/(a r)-1| =", np.abs(out['R'][1:]/(a*out['r'][1:])-1).max())
# SCRIPT: NU-V4-TRISECT-V2 trisection in which only a detected horizon ('BH') or 'dispersed' moves the
bracket.
# 'BH_breakdown' (numerical breakdown after 2M/R > 0.95) and 'crash' get one retry at CFL 0.3, then are
reported and skipped.
# Every run reports min I over the run (positivity monitor) and max 2M/R.
import sys, json, time, os, importlib
from multiprocessing import Pool
sys.path.insert(0, os.environ['SIMDIR']); sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
def one(a):
d,kw=a; mod=importlib.import_module(RUNNER); o=mod.run(d,**kw); s=o['status']; note=''
if s not in ('BH','dispersed'):
kw2=dict(kw,cfl_r=0.3,cfl_mu=0.3); o2=mod.run(d,**kw2); note=' (retry at CFL 0.3 after %s)'%s; o=o2; s
=o['status']
return d,s,float(o['eps']),float(o['Imin']),float(o['max2MR']),note
if __name__=='__main__':
tag=sys.argv[1]; RUNNER=sys.argv[2]; lo,hi,tol=float(sys.argv[3]),float(sys.argv[4]),float(sys.argv[5]);
kw=json.loads(sys.argv[6])
log=[]; t0=time.time()
with Pool(2) as p:
while hi-lo>tol:
a=lo+(hi-lo)/3; b=lo+2*(hi-lo)/3
res=p.map(one,[(a,kw),(b,kw)])

73

for d,s,e,im,m2,note in res: print(f'{tag} delta={d:.8f}: {s} eps={e:.2f} minI={im:.3e} max2M/R={
m2:.4f}{note}',flush=True); log.append((d,s,e,im,m2,note))
ok=[(d,s) for d,s,*_ in res if s in ('BH','dispersed')]
if not ok: print('both points undecided; stop',flush=True); break
for d,s in ok:
if s=='BH': hi=min(hi,d)
else: lo=max(lo,d)
if lo>=hi: print('inconsistent outcomes; stop',flush=True); break
print(f'RESULT {tag}: delta_c = {0.5*(lo+hi):.8f} +- {0.5*(hi-lo):.8f} bracket [{lo:.8f}, {hi:.8f}] ({(
time.time()-t0)/60:.1f} min)',flush=True)
json.dump(dict(tag=tag,dc=0.5*(lo+hi),w=hi-lo,lo=lo,hi=hi,log=log),open(f'res_{tag}.json','w'))
# SCRIPT: NU-V4-TRISECT-CLEAN trisect in which a crash never moves the bracket: one retry at CFL 0.3, else
reported and skipped
import sys, json, time, os
from multiprocessing import Pool
sys.path.insert(0, os.environ['SIMDIR']); sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from run_split import run
def one(a):
d,q,ed,n,em=a
o=run(d,fnu=FNU,N=400,K=32,eps0=0.1,eps_dec=ed,eps_max=em,qshape=q,nexp=n,rr=RR); s=o['status']; note=''
if s not in ('BH','dispersed'):
o=run(d,fnu=FNU,N=400,K=32,eps0=0.1,eps_dec=ed,eps_max=em,qshape=q,nexp=n,rr=RR,cfl_r=0.3,cfl_mu=0.3);
note=' (retry at CFL 0.3 after %s)'%s; s=o['status']
return d,s,float(o['eps']),note
if __name__=='__main__':
tag=sys.argv[1]; q=float(sys.argv[2]); ed=float(sys.argv[3]); n=float(sys.argv[4]); lo,hi,tol=float(sys.
argv[5]),float(sys.argv[6]),float(sys.argv[7]); em=float(sys.argv[8]); FNU=float(sys.argv[9]); RR=float(
sys.argv[10])
log=[]; t0=time.time()
with Pool(2) as p:
while hi-lo>tol:
a=lo+(hi-lo)/3; b=lo+2*(hi-lo)/3
res=p.map(one,[(a,q,ed,n,em),(b,q,ed,n,em)])
for d,s,e,note in res: print(f'{tag} delta={d:.8f}: {s} eps={e:.2f}{note}',flush=True); log.append
((d,s,e,note))
ok=[(d,s) for d,s,e,_ in res if s in ('BH','dispersed')]
if not ok: print('both points undecided; stop',flush=True); break
for d,s in ok:
if s=='BH': hi=min(hi,d)
else: lo=max(lo,d)
if lo>=hi: print('inconsistent outcomes; stop',flush=True); break
print(f'RESULT {tag}: delta_c = {0.5*(lo+hi):.8f} +- {0.5*(hi-lo):.8f} bracket [{lo:.8f}, {hi:.8f}] ({(
time.time()-t0)/60:.1f} min)',flush=True)
json.dump(dict(tag=tag,dc=0.5*(lo+hi),w=hi-lo,lo=lo,hi=hi,log=log),open(f'res_{tag}.json','w'))

# SCRIPT: NU-V4-TRISECT-FNU two trial amplitudes per round in parallel (thirds of the bracket)
import sys, json, time, os
from multiprocessing import Pool
sys.path.insert(0, os.environ['SIMDIR']); sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from run_nuc_n import run
def one(a):
d,q,ed,n,em=a; o=run(d,fnu=FNU,N=400,K=32,eps0=0.1,eps_dec=ed,eps_max=em,qshape=q,nexp=n); return d,o['
status'],float(o['eps'])
if __name__=='__main__':
tag=sys.argv[1]; q=float(sys.argv[2]); ed=float(sys.argv[3]); n=float(sys.argv[4]); lo,hi,tol=float(sys.
argv[5]),float(sys.argv[6]),float(sys.argv[7]); em=float(sys.argv[8]); FNU=float(sys.argv[9])
log=[]; t0=time.time()
with Pool(2) as p:
while hi-lo>tol:
a=lo+(hi-lo)/3; b=lo+2*(hi-lo)/3
res=p.map(one,[(a,q,ed,n,em),(b,q,ed,n,em)])
for d,s,e in res: print(f'{tag} delta={d:.5f}: {s} eps={e:.2f}',flush=True); log.append((d,s,e))
(da,sa,_),(db,sb,_)=res
if 'BH' not in (sa,sb) and 'dispersed' not in (sa,sb): break
if sa=='BH': hi=da
elif sb=='BH': lo,hi=da,db
else: lo=db
print(f'RESULT {tag}: delta_c = {0.5*(lo+hi):.5f} +- {0.5*(hi-lo):.5f} ({(time.time()-t0)/60:.1f} min)',
flush=True)
json.dump(dict(tag=tag,dc=0.5*(lo+hi),w=hi-lo,log=log),open(f'res_{tag}.json','w'))
# SCRIPT: NU-V4-MEMORY-SPLIT frozen memory form (w = 0.890, unchanged) with flux rate kappa1 and shear+ rate
kappa2 = r kappa1
# Same object as PBH-NU-MEMORY-CURVE; the only change is the collision diagonal (0, 1, r, r, ...) in place of
(0, 1, 1, 1, ...).
import numpy as np, json, sys
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
from scipy.special import roots_legendre
kn,kw=roots_legendre(36); kmin,kmax=0.25,9.0; ks=0.5*(kmax-kmin)*(kn+1)+kmin; wk=0.5*(kmax-kmin)*kw
wE=wk*ks**2*np.exp(-ks**2/2); wE/=wE.sum()
LM=60; M=np.zeros((LM+1,LM+1))
for l in range(1,LM+1): M[l,l-1]=M[l-1,l]=l/np.sqrt(4.0*l*l-1)
tau0=0.02; eg=np.linspace(tau0,6.0,1200); n=LM+1
def surv(k,ed,p,r):
d=np.full(n,r); d[0]=0.0; d[1]=1.0
def rhs(t,v):
K=0.0 if ed is None else ed**p/t**(p+1)
x,y=v[:n],v[n:]
return np.concatenate([-K*d*x+k*(M@y),-K*d*y-k*(M@x)])
v0=np.zeros(2*n); v0[0]=1
s=solve_ivp(rhs,(tau0,eg[-1]),v0,method='BDF',t_eval=eg,rtol=1e-9,atol=1e-12)
return s.y[0]**2+s.y[n]**2
f=lambda ed,p,r: sum(w_*surv(kk,ed,p,r) for kk,w_ in zip(ks,wE))
i17,i40=np.searchsorted(eg,1.7),np.searchsorted(eg,4.0); w=0.890; Ai=f(None,3,1.0)
out={}
for r in [1.0,0.5,2.0]:
g=(1-f(2.0,3,r))/(1-Ai); G=float(w*g[i17]+(1-w)*g[i40]); out[str(r)]=G
print(f'n=3 eps_dec=2 r=kappa2/kappa1={r}: Gamma12={(r-1)/(r+1):+.4f} memory-form G={G:.4f}',flush=True)
json.dump(out,open('memory_split.json','w'))

B.16 The check of the 3 November review
# SCRIPT: NU-V4-RUN-STEP run_nuc_n.py with the review's step history (kappa/H = 1e4 until eps_b, then 0 for
good)
import numpy as np, time
from ms_nuc import rhs, state, timestep, rho_b
from ms_fluid import initial_data, make_grid
def run(delta_m, fnu=0.409, N=400, K=32, rmax=9.0, beta=3.0, eps0=0.1, cfl_r=0.4, cfl_mu=0.4, cH=0.005,
eps_max=30.0, qshape=1.0, snap_eps=(), hist_every=20, stop_rule=True, disperse_rule=True, kap=0.0,
eps_dec=None, nexp=3, kapcap=5.0e5):
r = make_grid(N, rmax, beta)
t0, R, U, rho = initial_data(r, delta_m, eps0, 0.0, qshape)
e = (1-fnu)*rho
mue = np.linspace(-1, 1, K+1)
I = np.repeat((fnu*rho/(4*np.pi))[:, None], K, axis=1)
Mi = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum((4*np.pi/3)*np.diff(R**3)*rho)])
Gam = np.ones(N+1); Gam[1:] = np.sqrt(1.0 + U[1:]**2 - 2*Mi[1:]/R[1:])
t = t0
dR = np.zeros(N+1); dU = np.zeros(N+1); dG = np.zeros(N+1); de = np.zeros(N); dI = np.zeros((N, K))
peakC = -1.0; hist = []; snaps = {}; step = 0; status = 'undecided'
snap_eps = list(snap_eps)
while True:
Ib = fnu/(1-fnu)*e[-1]/(4*np.pi)
# incoming background tied to the outer cell's fluid density
if eps_dec is not None:
kap = (1/(2*t))*(1.0e4 if 2*np.sqrt(t*t0) < eps_dec else 0.0)
# step: kappa/H = 1e4 until eps_b
(= eps_dec), then 0
try:
M, Ac, ac, S, V = rhs(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, fnu, Ib, kap, dR, dU, dG, de, dI)
except (ZeroDivisionError, FloatingPointError):
status = 'crash'; break
epsn = 2*np.sqrt(t*t0)
twoMR = 2*M[1:]/R[1:]
C = 2*(M[1:] - (4*np.pi/3)*rho_b(t)*R[1:]**3)/R[1:]
iC = np.argmax(C); peakC = max(peakC, C[iC])
if step % hist_every == 0:
dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]
inner = r[1:] < 3.0
hcv = np.max(np.abs(Gam[1:]**2 - 1 - U[1:]**2 + twoMR)[inner])
# Hamiltonian-constraint check
hist.append((t, epsn, C[iC], r[1+iC], e[0]/rho_b(t), 2*np.pi*I[0].sum()*dmu/rho_b(t),
e[-1]/rho_b(t), 2*np.pi*I[-1].sum()*dmu/rho_b(t), hcv))
if snap_eps and epsn >= snap_eps[0]:

80

snaps[snap_eps.pop(0)] = dict(t=t, eps=epsn, R=R.copy(), U=U.copy(), e=e.copy(), I=I.copy(), M=M.
copy())
if stop_rule:
if np.any((twoMR >= 1.0) & (U[1:] < 0)):
status = 'BH'; break
if disperse_rule and epsn > 3.0 and C[iC] < 0.5*peakC:
status = 'dispersed'; break
if epsn > eps_max: break
try:
dt = min(timestep(R, U, e, I, mue, Gam, Ac, ac, S, cfl_r, cfl_mu), cH*2*t, (0.5/(kap*np.max(Ac))
if kap > 0 else 1e30))
# SSP RK3 (the outer-boundary intensity is evaluated at each stage time)
R1 = R + dt*dR; U1 = U + dt*dU; G1 = Gam + dt*dG; e1 = e + dt*de; I1 = I + dt*dI
rhs(t+dt, R1, U1, G1, e1, I1, mue, fnu, fnu/(1-fnu)*e1[-1]/(4*np.pi), kap, dR, dU, dG, de, dI)
R2 = 0.75*R + 0.25*(R1+dt*dR); U2 = 0.75*U + 0.25*(U1+dt*dU); G2 = 0.75*Gam + 0.25*(G1+dt*dG)
e2 = 0.75*e + 0.25*(e1+dt*de); I2 = 0.75*I + 0.25*(I1+dt*dI)
rhs(t+0.5*dt, R2, U2, G2, e2, I2, mue, fnu, fnu/(1-fnu)*e2[-1]/(4*np.pi), kap, dR, dU, dG, de, dI)
R = R/3 + 2/3*(R2+dt*dR); U = U/3 + 2/3*(U2+dt*dU); Gam = Gam/3 + 2/3*(G2+dt*dG)
e = e/3 + 2/3*(e2+dt*de); I = I/3 + 2/3*(I2+dt*dI)
except (ZeroDivisionError, FloatingPointError):
coll = U[1:] < 0
status = 'BH' if (coll.any() and np.max(twoMR[coll]) > 0.95) else 'crash'
# breakdown just
before collapse
break
t += dt; step += 1
if np.any(e <= 0) or not np.all(np.isfinite(U)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(I)):
status = 'crash'; break
return dict(status=status, t=t, t0=t0, eps=2*np.sqrt(t*t0), steps=step, peakC=peakC, hist=np.array(hist),
snaps=snaps, r=r, mue=mue, R=R, U=U, e=e, I=I, Gam=Gam)
if __name__ == '__main__':
t1 = time.time()
out = run(0.0, fnu=0.409, N=200, K=16, eps_max=5.0, stop_rule=False)
t = out['t']; Ib = 0.409*rho_b(t)/(4*np.pi); eb = 0.591*rho_b(t)
print(f"FRW test to eps={out['eps']:.2f} in {out['steps']} steps ({time.time()-t1:.1f}s)")
print(" max|I/Ib-1| =", np.abs(out['I']/Ib-1).max(), " max|e/eb-1| =", np.abs(out['e']/eb-1).max())
a = np.sqrt(t/out['t0']); print(" max|R/(a r)-1| =", np.abs(out['R'][1:]/(a*out['r'][1:])-1).max())
# SCRIPT: NU-V4-QUEUE-G independent check of the Open Math OS review's measurements: two runs either side of
each quoted threshold
: "${SIMDIR:?set SIMDIR to PBH_neutrino_simulation/simulation (McCormick, doi:10.5281/zenodo.23808376)}"
cd "$(dirname "$0")"
python3 - > log_G.txt 2>&1 <<'PY'
import sys,os;sys.path.insert(0,os.environ['SIMDIR']);sys.path.insert(0,'.')

from run_step import run; o=run(d,fnu=0.409,N=400,K=32,eps0=0.1,eps_dec=1.0,eps_max=15.0,nexp=3); return
('step eps_b=1',d,o['status'],float(o['eps']))
def k5(d):
from run_nuc_n import run; o=run(d,fnu=0.409,N=400,K=32,eps0=0.1,eps_dec=1.20,eps_max=15.0,nexp=3); return
('K5 n=3 eps_dec=1.20',d,o['status'],float(o['eps']))
jobs=[(step,0.5132),(step,0.5138),(k5,0.5165),(k5,0.5171)]
def go(a): return a[0](a[1])
with Pool(2) as p:
for t,d,s,e in p.map(go,jobs): print(f'{t} delta={d}: {s} eps={e:.2f}',flush=True)
print('review: step eps_b=1 delta_c=0.513512 (expect 0.5132 dispersed, 0.5138 BH); K5 n=3 eps_dec=1.20 delta_c
