B.17 The coordinate of the law: the intrinsic-ruler check
# SCRIPT: PREREGISTERED_RULER (written 2026-11-05 before any number below was computed)
The intrinsic-ruler check: are the measured threshold offsets a law in the kept sector's own geometry
(IQGT, Paper I Sec. 4: "the parameter manifold does not generate the geometry") rather than in the parameter
chart x = (1/2) ln r?
DATA (fixed, no new collapse runs): the 15 thresholds of v4.1 + A1, read as tau_G = ln((1-G)/G), G = (dc 0.49773)/(0.52832 - 0.49773),
offsets taken relative to the measured anchor r = 1 (dc = 0.51074074).
all-higher family (every l >= 2 at kappa2 = r kappa1): r = 1/16, 1/4, 1/2, 1/1.2, 0.8, 1, 1.2, 1.25, 2, 4,
16 (clean K points).
shear-only family (l = 2 alone at kappa2):
r = 1/4, 1/1.2, 1.2, 4.
Bracket corners carried through every statistic.
THE OBJECT. The moment chain of the kept sector, L(r) = -i k M - kappa D_r, M the Legendre streaming matrix
(M[l,l-1] = l/sqrt(4l^2-1)), D_r = diag(0,1,r,r,...) (all-higher) or diag(0,1,r,1,1,...) (shear-only), LM+1 =
61 moments
(the chain of memory_curve.py). L is non-normal. Projector: the Riesz projector Pi_r onto the least-damped
eigenvalue
(largest real part), the mode the density survives in; built from right/left eigenvectors, Pi = |R><L|/<L|R>,
Pi^2 = Pi.
Fork (both reported, decided now): rank-1 (least damped) is PRIMARY; rank-2 (two least damped) is the
comparison.
Scale: kappa/k at readout. The memory form reads at eps* = 1.7 where kappa = eps_dec^3/tau^4 = 8/1.7^4 =
0.958 (n = 3,
eps_dec = 2); k runs over the profile nodes with weights k^2 exp(-k^2/2) (memory_curve.py, 36 Gauss nodes on
[0.25, 9]).
PRIMARY: kappa = 0.958, profile-weighted. Sensitivity (reported, not used for verdicts): kappa = 0.3, 3.0;
single k = 2; LM = 20.
THE RULER. s(r) = signed intrinsic distance of Pi_r from Pi_1 along the family, three readings computed side
by side:
(a) arc length of the Hermitian pullback metric, g_rr = (1/2)||d Pi/dr||_F^2, s = int sqrt(g) dr
[IQGT Sec
. 4, Hermitian form]
(b) chord: d_HS(r) = ||Pi_r - Pi_1||_F / sqrt 2, signed by the side of r = 1
[
control on (a)]
(c) his biorthogonal trace form q(V) = (1/2) Re Tr V^2 with V = d Pi/dr
[cone
/ B57: may be <= 0]
(d) principal angle theta(r) between the right eigenvectors R_r and R_1 (and the left pair)
[Gr
(1,n) geodesic]
Profile average: s is averaged over k with the same weights as the memory form (that is how the threshold saw
the chain).
Each reading is a separate ruler; the verdicts below are stated for (a) and repeated for (b),(d). (c) is
reported with
its sign; if q < 0 anywhere on the family the Hermitian arc length is not a length there and that is stated.
WHAT IS ALREADY KNOWN IN THE CHART (the thing to beat):
all-higher odd rates in x: 1.1128 (|x| = 0.112), 1.1006 (0.091, A1), 1.1093 (0.347), 1.1177 (0.693), 1.1384
(1.386):
spread 0.038 (3.4 % of the mean), a monotone rise beyond |x| = 0.35 ("the bend").
shear-only odd rate in x: 1.2038 (0.693), 1.1895 (0.091). Family ratio shear/all-higher: 1.077 (0.693),
1.081 (0.091).
Linear fit of all 11 all-higher offsets against x: residual RMS to be computed and reported as the chart
baseline.
READINGS AND KILL LINES (fixed now):
R1 "the chart was the whole discrepancy": in ruler (a) the all-higher and shear-only families fall on ONE
line.
Test: slope_shear / slope_all in s lies in [0.97, 1.03] (it is 1.08 in x). Dies otherwise.
R2 "the chart explains the bend": in ruler (a) the all-higher odd rates (same pairs as above, now per unit
of s)
have spread below 1.2 % of their mean (it is 3.4 % in x), i.e. less than 0.35 of the chart spread. Dies
otherwise.
R3 "the ruler is not it": R1 and R2 both die. Then the discrepancy is not the chart (failed this way; other
projectors / scales remain open and are listed, not run).
Controls that must come out as stated or the check is void:

82

C1 positive control (the pipeline can see a chart effect): synthetic offsets tau_syn = 1.0 * s(r) (one law
in s, both
families), read back in x, must show a family split and a bend of the measured kind (ratio != 1, spread
> 1.2 %);
read back in s must give ratio 1 and spread 0 to machine precision.
C2 negative control (the pipeline can fail): the wrong ruler, s built from the OTHER family's projector
(shear-only data read on the all-higher projector and vice versa), must NOT pass R1 better than the
right ruler.
C3 chart control: the ruler Gamma12 = (r-1)/(r+1) (also a function of parameters only) must not pass R1/R2
either;
if it does, the rulers are not discriminating and the verdict is "undecided".
C4 bracket corners: every statistic is recomputed at both corners of every bracket; a verdict counts only if
it
holds at every corner.
C5 eigen-solve control: Pi^2 = Pi to 1e-10, Tr Pi = 1, left/right biorthonormality to 1e-10 at every r, k.
Opposite built in: if the slope ratio in s moves AWAY from 1 (beyond 1.08) the chart was hiding a larger
family split.
Nothing above is edited after the run; results are appended below.
AMENDMENT (before any run; a definition, not a data decision). L = -ikM - kappa D has S L S = conj(L) with S
= diag((-1)^l),
so eigenvalues come in conjugate pairs with EQUAL real part whenever they are complex (the two-moment pair kappa/2 +- sqrt(kappa^2/4 - k^2/3)
is the model case). "The least-damped eigenvalue" is therefore a conjugate PAIR wherever it is complex and
rank-1 is not well
defined there. PRIMARY projector := the Riesz projector onto that pair (rank 2 when complex, rank 1 when real
; it is real and
tracks continuously). The rank-1 projector onto the +Im member is the comparison. Minimum gap (real part) to
the next mode
is logged at every r, k; a crossing voids the tracking at that k and is reported.
RESULT 1 (primary run, kappa = 0.958, LM = 60, profile-weighted; out_primary.txt).
Chart baseline: all-higher slope 1.1328, rms 0.0142, odd-rate spread 3.39 % (corners 2.90-3.87); shear-only
slope 1.2036; ratio 1.0624 (1.058-1.067).
C3 (Gamma12 chart): spread 53 %, ratio 0.877: not discriminating in favour of Gamma12; fine.
Rulers (a),(b),(d) on the least-damped Riesz projector: R1 DIES (ratios 1.27, 0.67, 0.73), R2 DIES (spreads
195 %, 255 %, 201 %).
C1 passes (the pipeline can see a chart effect), C2 passes (wrong ruler no better), eigen checks 1e-13.
INSTRUMENT FINDING (diag_modes.py), which voids this ruler rather than the reading: at kappa = 0.958 the
least-damped
eigenvalue is isolated only for k < 1.4 (gap 0.9 -> 0.06); for k >= 1.4, which carries ~85 % of the profile
weight, it is a
REAL eigenvalue at about -kappa with real-part gap 1e-4 or less, Petermann factor 374 at the transition, and
3 % weight on
l = 0. That is the paper's own Sec. 2 statement (the hydrodynamic pole meets the branch cut at kappa ~
0.646 k): there is NO
isolated kept eigenprojector at readout for most of the profile, so "the kept-sector Riesz projector" is not
a well-posed
object there and its distance is not a ruler. R3 is therefore NOT concluded from this run: the ruler was
ill-posed
("failed this way" at the instrument, not at the reading). The biorthogonal form q was indefinite along the
path
(-1e2 .. +4e3), as B57/the cone paper allow; the Hermitian arc length is not a length there, as stated above
.
RULER (e), written now, before running it. The object that IS well-posed through decoupling is the state
itself: the
kept-sector trajectory v_r(tau) of memory_curve.py (61 moments, start in |0>, evolve under -(ikM + kappa(tau)
D_r), the
same history as the collapse runs: kappa = 8/tau^4, n = 3, eps_dec = 2, tau0 = 0.02). Ruler (e): the
principal angle

import numpy as np, json, os, sys, math

here=os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); up=os.path.join(here,'..')

def tau_of(dc): G=(dc-FL)/(INS-FL); return math.log((1-G)/G)
def load(tag):
d=json.load(open(os.path.join(up,'res_%s.json'%tag))); lo=d.get('lo',d['dc']-d['w']/2); hi=d.get('hi',d['
dc']+d['w']/2); return d['dc'],lo,hi

A1_all_r12',1.25:'K_r125_clean',2.0:'J_r2',4.0:'F_r4',16.0:'F2_r16'}

84

anc=load('H1_r1')
# ---------- chain ---------LM=int(os.environ.get('LM','60')); n=LM+1
M=np.zeros((n,n))
for l in range(1,n): M[l,l-1]=M[l-1,l]=l/np.sqrt(4.0*l*l-1)
S=np.diag([(-1.0)**l for l in range(n)])
kn,kw=roots_legendre(36); ks=0.5*(9.0-0.25)*(kn+1)+0.25; wk=0.5*(9.0-0.25)*kw; wE=wk*ks**2*np.exp(-ks**2/2);
wE/=wE.sum()
if os.environ.get('SINGLEK'): ks=np.array([float(os.environ['SINGLEK'])]); wE=np.array([1.0])
KAP=float(os.environ.get('KAP','0.958'))
def Dr(r,fam):
d=np.full(n,r if fam=='all' else 1.0); d[0]=0.0; d[1]=1.0
if fam=='shear': d[2]=r
return d
def proj(k,r,fam,rank):
L=-1j*k*M-KAP*np.diag(Dr(r,fam))
ev,R=np.linalg.eig(L); Lf=np.linalg.inv(R)
# rows of Lf are left eigenvectors (biorthonormal to R)
o=np.argsort(-ev.real); ev,R,Lf=ev[o],R[:,o],Lf[o,:]
if rank=='pair':
idx=[0] if abs(ev[0].imag)<1e-9 else ([0,1] if abs(ev[0].real-ev[1].real)<1e-7 else [0])
elif rank=='one': idx=[0] if abs(ev[0].imag)<1e-9 else [0 if ev[0].imag>0 else 1]
P=sum(np.outer(R[:,i],Lf[i,:]) for i in idx)
m=len(idx); gap=ev[m].real-ev[0].real if m<n else 0.0
return P,gap,ev[0]
def rulers(fam,rank,us):
"""s_a (arc length, Hermitian), s_b (chord), q (biorth form along the path), theta (principal angle), each
profile-averaged; indexed by u=ln r."""
sa=np.zeros(len(us)); sb=np.zeros(len(us)); qmin=np.zeros(len(us)); th=np.zeros(len(us)); worst_gap=1e9;
chk=0.0
i1=int(np.argmin(abs(us)))
for k,w in zip(ks,wE):
Ps=[];
for u in us:
P,gap,ev=proj(k,math.exp(u),fam,rank); Ps.append(P); worst_gap=min(worst_gap,gap)
chk=max(chk,np.abs(P@P-P).max(),abs(np.trace(P).real-round(np.trace(P).real)))
Ps=np.array(Ps); du=us[1]-us[0]
dP=np.gradient(Ps,du,axis=0)
g=0.5*np.sum(np.abs(dP)**2,axis=(1,2))
# (1/2)||dP/du||_F^2
q=0.5*np.real(np.einsum('uij,uji->u',dP,dP))
# (1/2) Re Tr V^2
arc=np.concatenate([[0],np.cumsum(np.sqrt(g[1:])*du)]); arc-=arc[i1]
sa+=w*arc
chord=np.array([np.linalg.norm(P-Ps[i1])/math.sqrt(2) for P in Ps]); sb+=w*np.sign(us)*chord
qmin+=w*q
# principal angle via the Hermitian part: use range of P (right eigvecs) vs range at r=1
for j,P in enumerate(Ps):
A=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)[0][:,:int(round(np.trace(P).real))]
B=np.linalg.svd(Ps[i1],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:int(round(np.trace(Ps[i1]).real))]
sv=np.linalg.svd(A.conj().T@B,compute_uv=False); th[j]+=w*np.sign(us[j])*math.sqrt(np.sum(np.
arccos(np.clip(sv,0,1))**2))
return dict(a=sa,b=sb,q=qmin,theta=th,gap=worst_gap,chk=chk)
us=np.linspace(math.log(1/16),math.log(16),641)
def interp(arr,r): return float(np.interp(math.log(r),us,arr))
# ---------- data ---------def family_points(tab):

def tau_off(pt,a): return tau_of(pt)-tau_of(a)
def stats(rs,pts,xfun,corner=None):
"""slope of tau against xfun(r) (least squares with intercept) and odd rates per unit of xfun; corner:
None=mid, else dict r->dc"""
xs=np.array([xfun(r) for r in rs]); ts=np.array([tau_off(corner[r] if corner else p[0], corner.get(1.0,anc
[0]) if corner else anc[0]) for r,p in zip(rs,pts)])
A=np.vstack([np.ones(len(xs)),xs]).T; (a,b),res,_,_=np.linalg.lstsq(A,ts,rcond=None); rms=float(np.sqrt(np
.mean((ts-a-b*xs)**2)))
odd={}
for r in rs:
if r>1 and (1/r) in rs or any(abs(1/r-q)<1e-9 for q in rs):
q=[q for q in rs if abs(1/r-q)<1e-9]
if r>1 and q:

i,j=rs.index(r),rs.index(q[0]); odd[r]=(ts[i]-ts[j])/(xs[i]-xs[j])

def spread(odd): v=np.array(list(odd.values())); return float((v.max()-v.min())/abs(v.mean()))

v in sorted(odds.items())))

# ---------- data ---------mc = json.load(open(MEM))

def memory_curve(n):
pts = []
for key, val in mc.items():
nn, ee = key.split('_')
if int(nn) == n:
pts.append((float(ee), val['two_epoch']))
return sorted(pts)

# ---------- FIG 6 ---------wit = json.load(open(WIT))
RATIOS = [0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1, 2]
LS = [1, 2, 3, 4]
FLOOR = -32.0

def height_grid(n):
H = np.zeros((len(LS), len(RATIOS)))
for rec in wit:
if rec['n'] != n:
continue
i = int(np.argmin([abs(rec['ratio'] - r) for r in RATIOS]))
j = LS.index(rec['L'])
R = rec['res']['4.0']['R_ret']
H[j, i] = max(np.log10(max(R, 1e-40)) - FLOOR, 0.0)
return H

H = {3: height_grid(3), 6: height_grid(6)}
hmax = max(H[3].max(), H[6].max())
ZTOP = max(34.0, np.ceil(hmax) + 2)
norm = Normalize(vmin=0, vmax=ZTOP)
cmap = plt.cm.viridis
WALL_LOG = -3.0
WALL_H = WALL_LOG - FLOOR
fig = plt.figure(figsize=(11.5, 5.4))
axes = []
for k, n in enumerate([3, 6]):
ax = fig.add_subplot(1, 2, k + 1, projection='3d')
axes.append(ax)
Hn = H[n]
xs, ys, zs, dxs, dys, dzs, cols = [], [], [], [], [], [], []
for j, L in enumerate(LS):
for i in range(len(RATIOS)):
h = Hn[j, i]
xs.append(i - 0.3)
ys.append(L - 0.3)
zs.append(0.0)
dxs.append(0.6)
dys.append(0.6)
dzs.append(h)
cols.append(cmap(norm(h)))
ax.bar3d(xs, ys, zs, dxs, dys, dzs, color=cols, shade=True, alpha=1.0,
edgecolor='none')
# translucent "witness on" plane at log10 R = -3
gx, gy = np.meshgrid([-0.6, len(RATIOS) - 0.4], [0.5, 4.5])
gz = np.full_like(gx, WALL_H, dtype=float)
ax.plot_surface(gx, gy, gz, color='#555555', alpha=0.22, shade=False,
linewidth=0, zorder=0)

ax.set_xticks(range(len(RATIOS)))

import json, math, numpy as np, matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg'); import matplotlib.pyplot as plt, os
here=os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); fl,ins=0.49773,0.52832
runs={1/16:'F2_r1o16',0.25:'F_r025',0.5:'J_r05',1/1.2:'A1_all_r083',0.8:'K_r08_clean',1.0:'H1_r1',1.2:'

Ls={0.25:'L_r025',1/1.2:'A1_shear_r083',1.2:'A1_shear_r12',4.0:'L_r4'}
dc=lambda t: json.load(open(os.path.join(here,'res_%s.json'%t)))['dc']
r=np.array(sorted(runs)); G=np.array([(dc(runs[x])-fl)/(ins-fl) for x in r]); Gam=(r-1)/(r+1); x=0.5*np.log(r)
; tau=np.log((1-G)/G); t1=tau[list(r).index(1.0)]
rl=np.array(sorted(Ls)); Gl=np.array([(dc(Ls[x])-fl)/(ins-fl) for x in rl])
fig,ax=plt.subplots(1,2,figsize=(11,4.3))

commit 41a0f83.
Runner: run_split_stage.py / run_shear_stage.py = v5.1's run_split_v2.py / run_shear_v2.py with ONE change:
kappa(t) = (1/2t) min[(eps_dec/eps(t))^3, 5e5] is evaluated at each SSPRK3 stage time (t, t+dt, t+dt/2)
instead of

94

once per step at t. The stiff time-step cap 0.5/(kappa max(1,r) max A) still uses the stage-start kappa(t
).
Physics fixed as v5.1: eps_dec = 2, n = 3, Gaussian profile (q = 1), R_nu = 0.409, eps0 = 0.1, eps_max = 15,
N = 400, Nmu = 32,
Courant 0.4/0.4, cH = 0.005.
Endpoint rule (v5.1): a run moves a bracket only if it ends in a detected horizon (2M/R >= 1 with U < 0) ->
'BH', or in
dispersal. 'crash' or 'BH_breakdown' (breakdown after 2M/R > 0.95 without a detected horizon) is retried
once at
Courant 0.3; if it fails again it is reported and never moves the bracket.
Search: ONE simulation process at a time (house rule), so the two-points-per-round trisection is replaced by
sequential
bisection with the same endpoint rule. Each search first runs both ends of its starting bracket: the
lower end must
disperse and the upper end must form a horizon; if an end gives the other outcome the bracket is extended
outward by
3e-4 (that run's outcome is kept as a bracket edge) and the new end is run. Bisection stops at bracket
width <= 1e-6.
An undecided midpoint is replaced by the points at 0.4 and then 0.6 of the bracket; if all three fail the
search stops
and is reported.

A1_search.py: bisection driver.
# SCRIPT: NU-V6-SEARCH sequential bisection (one simulation at a time) with v5.1's endpoint rule: only 'BH' (
detected
# horizon) or 'dispersed' moves a bracket; 'crash' / 'BH_breakdown' get one retry at the lower Courant number,
then are
# reported and skipped. Both ends of the starting bracket are run first; an end with the other outcome
extends the bracket.
# Resumable: progress is saved after every run in prog_<tag>.json.
import sys, os, json, time, importlib
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
def main():
tag, runner = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
center, half, tol = float(sys.argv[3]), float(sys.argv[4]), float(sys.argv[5])
kw = json.loads(sys.argv[6]); cretry = float(sys.argv[7])
mod = importlib.import_module(runner)
pf = os.path.join(HERE, f'prog_{tag}.json'); lf = os.path.join(HERE, f'log_{tag}.txt'); master = os.path.
join(HERE, 'RUNLOG.txt')
if os.path.exists(pf):
P = json.load(open(pf))
else:
P = dict(tag=tag, runner=runner, kw=kw, lo=None, hi=None, lo_try=center-half, hi_try=center+half, log
=[], t_used=0.0)
def save(): json.dump(P, open(pf, 'w'), indent=1)
def say(s):
line = time.strftime('%H:%M:%S ') + s
print(line, flush=True); open(lf, 'a').write(line + '\n'); open(master, 'a').write(f'[{tag}] ' + line
+ '\n')
def one(d):
t1 = time.time(); o = mod.run(d, **kw); s = o['status']; note = ''
if s not in ('BH', 'dispersed'):
o = mod.run(d, **dict(kw, cfl_r=cretry, cfl_mu=cretry)); note = f' (retry at Courant {cretry}
after {s})'; s = o['status']
dtm = time.time() - t1; P['t_used'] += dtm
rec = (d, s, float(o['eps']), float(o['Imin']), float(o['max2MR']), note, round(dtm, 1))
P['log'].append(rec); save()
say(f'{tag} delta={d:.8f}: {s} eps={o["eps"]:.4f} minI={o["Imin"]:.3e} steps={o["steps"]}{note} ({dtm
:.0f}s)')
return s
# 1) the lower end must disperse
while P['lo'] is None:
d = P['lo_try']; s = one(d)
if s == 'dispersed': P['lo'] = d

97

elif s == 'BH': P['hi'] = d if P['hi'] is None else min(P['hi'], d); P['lo_try'] = d - 2*half
else: say('lower end undecided after retry; stop'); save(); return
save()
# 2) the upper end must form a horizon
while P['hi'] is None:
d = P['hi_try']; s = one(d)
if s == 'BH': P['hi'] = d
elif s == 'dispersed': P['lo'] = max(P['lo'], d); P['hi_try'] = d + 2*half
else: say('upper end undecided after retry; stop'); save(); return
save()
# 3) bisect
while P['hi'] - P['lo'] > tol:
lo, hi = P['lo'], P['hi']; moved = False
for f in (0.5, 0.4, 0.6):
d = lo + f*(hi - lo); s = one(d)
if s == 'BH': P['hi'] = d; moved = True; break
if s == 'dispersed': P['lo'] = d; moved = True; break
save()
if not moved: say('three points undecided; stop'); return
if P['lo'] >= P['hi']: say('inconsistent outcomes; stop'); return
lo, hi = P['lo'], P['hi']
say(f'RESULT {tag}: delta_c = {0.5*(lo+hi):.8f} +- {0.5*(hi-lo):.8f} bracket [{lo:.8f}, {hi:.8f}] ({P["
t_used"]/60:.1f} min of runs)')
json.dump(dict(tag=tag, runner=runner, kw=kw, dc=0.5*(lo+hi), w=hi-lo, lo=lo, hi=hi, log=P['log']), open(
os.path.join(HERE, f'res_{tag}.json'), 'w'), indent=1)
if __name__ == '__main__':
main()

A1_oddrate.py: odd rates and four-corner ranges.
# SCRIPT: NU-V6-ODDRATE odd rate [tau(r)-tau(1/r)]/ln r with hard four-corner bracket ranges (v5.1 App. A.9,
A.11)
import math, json, sys
DF, DI = 0.49773, 0.52832
def tau(d, df=DF, di=DI): return math.log((di-d)/(d-df))
def G(d, df=DF, di=DI): return (d-df)/(di-df)
def rate(dr, dir_, r, df=DF, di=DI): return (tau(dr,df,di)-tau(dir_,df,di))/math.log(r)
def corners(br, bir, r, df=DF, di=DI):
# br, bir = (lo, hi) brackets for r and 1/r; tau decreases in delta
vals = [rate(a, b, r, df, di) for a in br for b in bir]
return min(vals), max(vals)
def even(dr, dir_, d1, df=DF, di=DI): return 0.5*(tau(dr,df,di)+tau(dir_,df,di))-tau(d1,df,di)
def summary(name, br, bir, r, d1=None):
mr = 0.5*sum(br); mi = 0.5*sum(bir); out = dict(pair=name, r=r, dc_r=mr, dc_inv=mi, G_r=G(mr), G_inv=G(mi)
)
out['rate'] = rate(mr, mi, r); out['range'] = corners(br, bir, r)
for s, lab in ((+7.8e-5, 'df+7.8e-5'), (-7.8e-5, 'df-7.8e-5')):
out['rate_'+lab] = rate(mr, mi, r, DF+s); out['range_'+lab] = corners(br, bir, r, DF+s)
if d1 is not None: out['even'] = even(mr, mi, d1)
return out
if __name__ == '__main__':
# reproduce v5.1's inner rates from its stored brackets
for nm, br, bir, r in (('v5.1 all r=1.2', (0.51001440, 0.51001532), (0.51151422, 0.51151483), 1.2),
('v5.1 all r=1.25', (0.50984047, 0.50984092), (0.51169535, 0.51169611), 1.25),
('v5.1 shear r=1.2', (0.50995397, 0.50995435), (0.51157461, 0.51157499), 1.2)):
s = summary(nm, br, bir, r, d1=0.510741)
print(f"{nm}: rate {s['rate']:.4f} range [{s['range'][0]:.4f}, {s['range'][1]:.4f}] even {s['even
']:+.4f} "
f"df+: {s['rate_df+7.8e-5']:.4f} df-: {s['rate_df-7.8e-5']:.4f}")

B.20 Self-scattering as the flux–shear link: algebra and frozen prediction (Section 12)
A2_PREREGISTERED.txt: method, predictions and comparison.
PREREGISTERED — Task A2 (self-scattering and the flux-shear link law)
Written 2026-11-11T14:04Z, before the script that computes r_s(t) along McCormick's background was written or
run.
DISCLOSURE (what was already seen when this was written)
- The two thresholds to be predicted are in the task text and in McCormick's App. C(d): with nu-nu 0.51941 +0.00016
(res_mg_phys6_10.0.json), without 0.52639 +- 0.00011 (res_mg_nonunu6_10.0.json), fluid 0.49742 (
res_eos_pf_10.0.json).
- The paper quotes nu-nu faster than nu-e+- by 1.5 (nu_e) and 7 (nu_mu,tau). While reading, a rough mental
estimate with
those two quoted ratios and flavour weights 1/3, 2/3 was made. It is NOT the prediction below; the
prediction uses the
rates exactly as run_mg.py evaluates them along the background, with the weighting fixed here and not
changed afterwards.
- McCormick's single-rate thresholds at T_H = 1 MeV (res_eosc_<Tdec>_10.0.json) were listed: Tdec 0.75:
0.51586,
1.0: 0.52076, 1.5: 0.52583, 2.0: 0.52837, 3.0: 0.53065. These are used by the opposite (timing) reading
below.
REFERENCES FOR G AT T_H = 1 MeV
- delta_fluid = 0.49742 (Table VII; res_eos_pf_10.0.json, full EOS).
- delta_inst: McCormick's archive has NO instantaneous free-streaming threshold at T_H = 1 MeV with the full
EOS
(res_eos_fs_*.json exist only for T0 <= 5 MeV, T_H <= 0.506 MeV; earlier_start/ has T_H = 0.06, 0.12 only).
The nearest instantaneous references are pure radiation, eps0 = 0.1 (his Table II):
R_nu = 0.409 -> 0.52832, R_nu = 0.488 -> 0.53379. 0.52832 is ruled out as a reference at this mass because
his own
single-rate run with Tdec = 3 MeV at T_H = 1 MeV already sits higher (0.53065), which would put G > 1.
PRIMARY: delta_inst = 0.53379 (R_nu = 0.488, the neutrino fraction while e+- are relativistic, which is the
epoch
T >~ 1 MeV in which these neutrinos decouple). SENSITIVITY: delta_inst interpolated linearly in R_nu (his Eq
. 18
says the shift is linear in R_nu) at the background R_nu at the e+- flux-link last-scattering epoch,
computed by the
script from his thermo table.
- G = (delta_c - delta_fluid)/(delta_inst - delta_fluid), tau_G = ln[(1-G)/G].
READING 1 (the frozen link law, primary)
McCormick's C3 with the first-order target is the v5.1 all-l>=2 family exactly: flux at k1 = k_e,s, every l>=2
at
k2 = k_e,s + k_n,s, so r_s = (k_e,s + k_n,s)/k_e,s >= 1, with k_e,s IDENTICAL in his two runs (nunu only
zeroes k_n).
That is the v5.1 intervention (flux rate held, shear+higher rate changed), with r now depending on group,
flavour and time.
- r_s(t): from run_mg.rates(Tb) exactly as the code evaluates it on the background (FD rates, chi_e at the
background

101

temperature, both rates capped at 100 H, 6 groups 0.4..20, two flavours), in the transport phase (after the
switch
from the perfect fluid at total nu_mu,tau rate at x=1 = 100 H).
- Weighting (fixed now): the link rapidity u_s(t) = (1/2) ln r_s(t) is averaged
(a) over time with the VISIBILITY of the flux link of that group, g_s = (k_e,s/H) exp(-tau_e,s) per d ln a,
tau_e,s = integral of k_e,s dt to the future. Why: in v5.1 the law was measured with the flux rate's
decoupling
history held fixed and r constant; the flux link is what lets the neutrinos go, so the rapidity it reads
is the
one in force while the flux link releases — the last-scattering density of that link.
(b) over groups and flavours with the background energy weights (flavour 1/3, 2/3 times the Fermi-Dirac
energy
fraction of each group on the 0.4..20 grid, as run_mg.spectral_weights at tau = 1). Why: the threshold
reads the
neutrino anisotropic stress, which is energy-weighted.
The rapidity is averaged (not r), because the v5.1 law is linear in the rapidity.
- Prediction: tau_G(with) = tau_G(without) + rate * u_eff, rate = 1.11 (all-l>=2 family; primary) and
1.19-1.20
(shear-only bracket), with tau_G(without) from McCormick's measured 0.52639 and the references above.
Converted back
to delta_c(with) and to a predicted shift in G.
- Sensitivity weightings, reported but NOT deciding: visibility of the total rate k_e+k_n; uniform in ln a
while
0.1 < k_e,s/H < 10; v5.1's memory-form readout epochs eps* = 1.7 and 4.0 mixed 0.89/0.11.
KILL LINE (Reading 1, primary weighting, primary reference, rate 1.11):
KILLED if the predicted shift in G has the wrong sign relative to the measured shift, or if
|predicted shift in G - measured shift in G| > 0.5 * |measured shift in G|.
Measured shift in G = G(0.51941) - G(0.52639) with the same references.
READING 2 (the opposite: timing, not link rapidity)
Self-scattering keeps the neutrinos isotropic longer: it delays decoupling, as if it were part of the flux
rate.
Then the threshold follows a single-rate decoupling temperature set by the TOTAL rate, and the link rapidity
plays no role.
- Tdec_eff is where the energy-weighted mean rate over groups and flavours (same weights as above) equals H on
the
background, computed from the same rate functions: from k_e alone (no nu-nu) and from k_e + k_n (with nu-nu)
.
- Each Tdec_eff is mapped to a threshold by McCormick's single-rate family at T_H = 1 MeV (listed above),
linear in
ln Tdec. Predicts delta_c(with), delta_c(without) and their shift in G.
- Same kill line applied to Reading 2's predicted shift in G (and its two absolute thresholds reported).
Both readings predict the same direction (nu-nu lowers delta_c); the size separates them, and if both pass,
the
numbers do not separate them and the decisive simulation is stated instead.
Results are appended below this line; nothing above is edited afterwards.

A2_step2_algebra.py: the operator on the bins, from McCormick’s compiled right-hand side.
# SCRIPT: A2_STEP2_SELFSCATTER_ALGEBRA
"""Exact algebra of McCormick's multigroup collision term (App. C, Eq. C3; ms_mg.py, nnmode=0) for one frozen
group.
Checks, on his own 32-bin angle grid (mue = linspace(-1,1,33), midpoints, weight dmu):
1. the collision operator C[I] = k_e (eps/4pi - I) + k_n (eps/4pi + c mu - I), with c = q/(2pi sum mu^2 dmu),
built line-for-line from ms_mg.py, equals -diag(0, k_e, k_e+k_n, ..., k_e+k_n) in the discrete orthonormal
Legendre basis (Gram-Schmidt of 1, mu, mu^2, ... under <f,g> = sum f g dmu);
2. the k_n part of that operator, taken from McCormick's compiled rhs() itself (two calls differing only in
kbn,
with kbe = 0, so nothing else in the right-hand side moves), equals the formula of 1;
3. (1/8)||[L,L*]||_F^2 = k^2 (c0^2 k_e^2 + c1^2 k_n^2) exactly for L = -i k M - D, M the discrete mu operator
,
with c0^2, c1^2 the discrete Jacobi weights, and these tend to 1/3 and 4/15 as N_mu grows.
"""
import sys, os
import numpy as np
SIM = os.path.join(os.environ.get('NU_UPSTREAM', '.'), 'simulation') # McCormick checkout at eb7c4de (set
NU_UPSTREAM)
K = 32
mue = np.linspace(-1, 1, K+1); dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]; mu = 0.5*(mue[:-1]+mue[1:])
sum_mu2 = np.sum(mu*mu*dmu)
def coll(I, ke, kn):
"""ms_mg.py collision lines (nnmode = 0), lapse A = 1."""
eps = 2*np.pi*np.sum(I)*dmu; q = 2*np.pi*np.sum(I*mu)*dmu
cs = q/(2*np.pi*sum_mu2)
tgt = eps/(4*np.pi) + cs*mu
return ke*(eps/(4*np.pi) - I) + kn*(tgt - I)
def legendre_basis(K, mu, dmu):
"""Discrete orthonormal polynomials on the bins: columns of Q, <Q_l, Q_m> = delta (weight dmu)."""
V = np.vander(mu, K, increasing=True)
W = np.sqrt(dmu)
Q, Rr = np.linalg.qr(W*V)
# orthonormal in the plain dot product of sqrt(dmu)*f
sgn = np.sign(np.diag(Rr)); Q = Q*sgn
return Q
# columns are sqrt(dmu) * P_l(mu_k)
Q = legendre_basis(K, mu, dmu)
ke, kn = 0.7, 3.1
Cmat = np.column_stack([coll(np.eye(K)[:, j], ke, kn) for j in range(K)])
# acts on I (bin values)
# in the orthonormal basis: f = Q^T sqrt(dmu) I -> C_hat = Q^T diag(sqrt dmu) C diag(1/sqrt dmu) Q
Wh = np.sqrt(dmu)
Chat = Q.T @ (Wh*Cmat/Wh) @ Q
target = -np.diag(np.r_[0.0, ke, np.full(K-2, ke+kn)])
err1 = np.max(np.abs(Chat - target))
print(f'1. max |C_hat - (-diag(0,k_e,k_e+k_n,...))| on 32 bins = {err1:.2e} (orthonormality error {np.max(np.
abs(Q.T@Q-np.eye(K))):.1e})')
# Note: high-degree monomials make QR ill-conditioned; the claim needs only span{1}, span{1,mu} to be exact.
# Basis-free check: C = -k_e (1 - P0) - k_n (1 - P01) with P0, P01 the bin-orthogonal projectors.
one = np.ones(K)
P0 = np.outer(one, one*dmu)/np.sum(one*one*dmu)

103

P1 = np.outer(mu, mu*dmu)/np.sum(mu*mu*dmu)
Cproj = -ke*(np.eye(K)-P0) - kn*(np.eye(K)-P0-P1)
print(f'
basis-free: max |C - [-k_e(1-P0) - k_n(1-P0-P1)]| = {np.max(np.abs(Cmat-Cproj)):.2e};'
f' <1,mu> = {np.sum(mu*dmu):.1e}')
ev = np.sort(np.linalg.eigvals(Cmat).real)
print(f'
eigenvalues: one at {ev[-1]:.3e}, one at {ev[-2]:.15f} (-k_e = {-ke}), {np.sum(np.abs(ev[:-2]+ke+kn
) < 1e-12)} at -(k_e+k_n)')
# 2. the k_n part from McCormick's compiled rhs()
sys.path.insert(0, SIM)
try:
from ms_mg import rhs
N = 4; G = 1
R = np.linspace(1.0, 2.0, N+1); U = np.full(N+1, 0.3); Gam = np.ones(N+1); e = np.full(N, 1.0)
rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
I = 1.0 + 0.3*rng.standard_normal((1, N, K))
le = np.log(np.array([1e-3, 1e3])); lp = le - np.log(3.0); lg = np.zeros(2); lT = 0.25*le
lchi = np.zeros(2)
out = []
for kbn in (0.0, 1.0):
dR = np.zeros(N+1); dU = np.zeros(N+1); dG = np.zeros(N+1); de = np.zeros(N); dI = np.zeros(I.shape)
res = rhs(0.0, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, le, lp, lg, lT, np.zeros(1), np.full(1, kbn), 1.0, 0.0, 4.0, G,
1.0, 0.0,
np.zeros(1), dR, dU, dG, de, dI, lchi, 0.0, 0)
out.append((dI.copy(), res[1].copy(), res[6].copy()))
# enub = 0 makes fn = 0 in state(); use enub > 0 instead so kap_n = kbn * eps/enub
out = []
for kbn in (0.0, 1.0):
dR = np.zeros(N+1); dU = np.zeros(N+1); dG = np.zeros(N+1); de = np.zeros(N); dI = np.zeros(I.shape)
res = rhs(0.0, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, le, lp, lg, lT, np.zeros(1), np.full(1, kbn), 1.0, 1.0, 4.0, G,
1.0, 0.0,
np.zeros(1), dR, dU, dG, de, dI, lchi, 0.0, 0)
out.append((dI.copy(), res[1].copy(), res[6].copy()))
diff = out[1][0] - out[0][0]
Ac = out[1][1]; kapn = out[1][2]
err2 = 0.0
for i in range(N):
pred = Ac[i]*coll(I[0, i], 0.0, kapn[0, i])
err2 = max(err2, np.max(np.abs(diff[0, i] - pred)))
print(f'2. McCormick rhs(): k_n part minus formula, max abs = {err2:.2e} (scale of I ~ 1; kap_n = {kapn[0]
})')
except Exception as ex:
print('2. could not call ms_mg.rhs:', repr(ex))
# 3. connection energy on the discrete chain
def energy(K, ke, kn, k=1.3):
mue = np.linspace(-1, 1, K+1); dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]; mu = 0.5*(mue[:-1]+mue[1:])
# discrete Jacobi (Lanczos) on weights dmu: stable orthonormal basis
w = np.full(K, dmu)
q_prev = np.zeros(K); q = np.ones(K)/np.sqrt(np.sum(w)); a = []; b = []
Qs = [q]
for l in range(K-1):
v = mu*q; al = np.sum(w*q*v); v = v - al*q - (b[-1] if b else 0.0)*q_prev
for qq in Qs: v -= np.sum(w*qq*v)*qq
# full reorthogonalisation
be = np.sqrt(np.sum(w*v*v)); a.append(al); b.append(be)
q_prev, q = q, v/be; Qs.append(q)
M = np.diag(b, 1) + np.diag(b, -1)
# alpha = 0 by symmetry
Dd = np.diag(np.r_[0.0, ke, np.full(K-2, ke+kn)])
L = -1j*k*M - Dd
Cm = L@L.conj().T - L.conj().T@L
lhs = 0.125*np.sum(np.abs(Cm)**2)
rhs_ = k*k*(b[0]**2*ke**2 + b[1]**2*kn**2)
return lhs, rhs_, b[0]**2, b[1]**2, k*k*(ke**2/3 + 4*kn**2/15)
for KK in (32, 64, 128, 1024):
lhs, r_, c0, c1, cont = energy(KK, ke, kn)
print(f'3. N_mu={KK:5d}: (1/8)||[L,L*]||^2 = {lhs:.15f}, k^2(c0^2 ke^2 + c1^2 kn^2) = {r_:.15f}, rel diff
{abs(lhs-r_)/r_:.1e};'
f' c0^2 = {c0:.10f} (1/3 - dmu^2/12 = {1/3-(2/KK)**2/12:.10f}), c1^2 = {c1:.10f} (4/15 = {4/15:.10f}

104

);'
f' continuum form k^2(ke^2/3+4kn^2/15) = {cont:.10f}, rel {abs(lhs-cont)/cont:.1e}')

A2_step3_predict.py: link rapidity along his background and the frozen predictions.
# SCRIPT: A2_STEP3_LINK_PREDICTION
"""Prediction of McCormick's T_H = 1 MeV multigroup threshold WITH nu-nu scattering, from the one WITHOUT it,
by the frozen v5.1 flux-shear link law (Reading 1) and by the opposite timing reading (Reading 2).
Rates exactly as run_mg.rates(Tb) evaluates them on the background (G=6, 0.4..20, FD, chi_e at T_b, cap 100 H)
.
Weighting fixed in PREREGISTERED.txt before this script was written. Does NOT use the with-nu-nu threshold."""
import os, sys, json
import numpy as np
SIM = os.path.join(os.environ.get('NU_UPSTREAM', '.'), 'simulation') # McCormick checkout at eb7c4de (set
NU_UPSTREAM)
sys.path.insert(0, SIM)
import run_mg as M
G, XLO, XHI, CAP, SWITCH = 6, 0.4, 20.0, 100.0, 100.0
edges, xg, dl = M.groups(G, XLO, XHI)
NF = 2
wE = M.spectral_weights(edges, 1.0)[:, 0]
W = np.concatenate([M.FR[0]*wE, M.FR[1]*wE])
# energy weight of each (flavour, group), sums to 1
ce_e = M.coef('e_FD', xg)('nu_e'); ce_m = M.coef('mu_FD', xg)('nu_e'); cn = M.coef('e_FD', xg)('nu_nu')
c1 = M.coef('mu_FD', np.array([1.0])); c_sw = (c1('nu_e')[0], c1('nu_nu')[0])
def rates_uncapped(Tb, nunu=True):
H, Tnu, chi = M.background(Tb)
re = np.concatenate([ce_e, ce_m])*M.GF2*np.tile(xg, NF)*Tnu*Tb**4*chi/H
rn = np.tile(cn, NF)*M.GF2*np.tile(xg, NF)*Tnu**5/H*(1.0 if nunu else 0.0)
return re, rn
def rates(Tb, nunu=True):
# == run_mg.rates (mode 'phys', scale 1)
re, rn = rates_uncapped(Tb, nunu)
return np.minimum(re, CAP), np.minimum(rn, CAP)
def tight_at(Tb, nunu=True):
H, Tnu, chi = M.background(Tb)
tot = (c_sw[0]*Tb**4*chi + c_sw[1]*Tnu**4*(1.0 if nunu else 0.0))*M.GF2*Tnu/H
return tot > SWITCH
# background clock: ln a = -ln T_nu ; eps = (T_nu/H)/(T_nu/H)|_{T_H}
TH = 1.00296
# T_b at eps = 1 in res_mg_*_10.0.json
Tb = np.exp(np.linspace(np.log(10.0), np.log(0.02), 40001))
H, Tnu, chi = M.background(Tb)
lna = -np.log(Tnu)
HH, TnuH, _ = M.background(TH)
eps = (Tnu/H)/(TnuH/HH)
RE = np.zeros((len(Tb), 2*G)); RN = np.zeros((len(Tb), 2*G)); RE0 = np.zeros_like(RE); RN0 = np.zeros_like(RE)
for j, T in enumerate(Tb):

105

RE[j], RN[j] = rates(T); RE0[j], RN0[j] = rates_uncapped(T)
tight_nn = np.array([tight_at(T, True) for T in Tb]); tight_no = np.array([tight_at(T, False) for T in Tb])
jsw_nn = np.argmax(~tight_nn); jsw_no = np.argmax(~tight_no)
print(f'switch to transport: with nu-nu at T_b = {Tb[jsw_nn]:.3f} MeV (eps {eps[jsw_nn]:.3f}); without at {Tb[
jsw_no]:.3f} MeV (eps {eps[jsw_no]:.3f})')
print(f'eps at T_b = 1.6, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2 MeV: ' + ', '.join(f'{np.interp(-np.log(T), -np.log(Tb), eps):.3f}'
for T in (1.6, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2)))
r = (RE + RN)/RE
u = 0.5*np.log(r)
print('\nr_s on the background (rates as the code evaluates them, capped):')
print(' T_b
eps
' + ' '.join(f'e{g}:{xg[g]:.2f}' for g in range(G)) + ' | ' + ' '.join(f'm{g}' for g in
range(G)))
for T in (8.0, 5.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.6, 1.3, 1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.15):
j = np.argmin(np.abs(Tb - T))
print(f' {Tb[j]:5.2f} {eps[j]:5.2f} ' + ' '.join(f'{v:7.3f}' for v in r[j]))
print('\nk_e/H and k_n/H at x_s (uncapped) at T_b = 2, 1, 0.5 MeV:')
for T in (2.0, 1.0, 0.5):
j = np.argmin(np.abs(Tb - T))
print(f' T={Tb[j]:.2f}: k_e/H ' + ' '.join(f'{v:8.3f}' for v in RE0[j]) + '\n
k_n/H ' + ' '.join
(f'{v:8.3f}' for v in RN0[j]))
def visibility(rate, j0):
"""g = (k/H) exp(-tau) per d ln a, tau = integral of k/H d ln a to the future; zero before the switch j0
."""
d = np.diff(lna)
mid = 0.5*(rate[1:] + rate[:-1])
tau = np.zeros(len(rate)); tau[:-1] = np.cumsum((mid*d)[::-1])[::-1]
g = rate*np.exp(-tau); g[:j0] = 0.0
return g, tau
def wavg(f, g):
num = np.trapezoid(f*g, lna); den = np.trapezoid(g, lna)
return num/den, den
U = {}
# (a) primary: visibility of the flux link k_e,s
ueff_s = np.zeros(2*G); norm_s = np.zeros(2*G); Teps = np.zeros(2*G)
for s in range(2*G):
g, tau = visibility(RE[:, s], jsw_nn)
ueff_s[s], norm_s[s] = wavg(u[:, s], g)
Teps[s] = np.exp(-wavg(-np.log(Tb), g)[0])
U['primary: visibility of k_e'] = float(np.sum(W*ueff_s))
print('\nper (flavour, group): energy weight, <u>_vis(k_e), r_eff = exp(2<u>), visibility norm, lastscattering T_b')
for s in range(2*G):
print(f' {"nu_e " if s < G else "nu_mt"} x={xg[s % G]:5.2f} w={W[s]:.4f} <u>={ueff_s[s]:.4f} r_eff={np
.exp(2*ueff_s[s]):.3f} norm={norm_s[s]:.4f} T_ls={Teps[s]:.3f}')
for f, nm in ((0, 'nu_e'), (1, 'nu_mt')):
sl = slice(f*G, (f+1)*G)
print(f' flavour {nm}: <u> = {np.sum(W[sl]*ueff_s[sl])/np.sum(W[sl]):.4f}')
# sensitivity weightings
ueff_t = np.zeros(2*G)
for s in range(2*G):
g, _ = visibility(RE[:, s] + RN[:, s], jsw_nn); ueff_t[s] = wavg(u[:, s], g)[0]
U['sens: visibility of k_e+k_n'] = float(np.sum(W*ueff_t))
ueff_w = np.zeros(2*G)
for s in range(2*G):
g = ((RE0[:, s] > 0.1) & (RE0[:, s] < 10.0)).astype(float); g[:jsw_nn] = 0.0; ueff_w[s] = wavg(u[:, s], g)
[0]
U['sens: uniform in ln a, 0.1<k_e/H<10'] = float(np.sum(W*ueff_w))
uu17 = np.array([np.interp(1.7, eps, u[:, s]) for s in range(2*G)]); uu40 = np.array([np.interp(4.0, eps, u[:,
s]) for s in range(2*G)])
U['sens: v5.1 readout eps*=1.7/4.0 (0.89/0.11)'] = float(np.sum(W*(0.89*uu17 + 0.11*uu40)))
# R_nu at the flux-link last-scattering epoch (for the sensitivity reference)
rpl = np.interp(Tb, M._T, M._rpl); rnu = np.interp(Tb, M._T, M._rnu); Rnu = rnu/(rnu + rpl)
T_ls = float(np.exp(np.sum(W*np.log(Teps))))
Rnu_ls = float(np.interp(-np.log(T_ls), -np.log(Tb), Rnu))

106

print(f'\nenergy-weighted flux-link last-scattering T_b = {T_ls:.3f} MeV, R_nu there = {Rnu_ls:.4f}; R_nu(T_H)
= {np.interp(-np.log(TH), -np.log(Tb), Rnu):.4f}')
d_fluid = 0.49742; d_without = 0.52639
d_inst_P = 0.53379
d_inst_S = 0.52832 + (0.53379 - 0.52832)*(Rnu_ls - 0.409)/(0.488 - 0.409)
def Gof(d, di): return (d - d_fluid)/(di - d_fluid)
def tau(Gv): return np.log((1 - Gv)/Gv)
def inv(t, di):
Gv = 1/(1 + np.exp(t)); return d_fluid + Gv*(di - d_fluid), Gv
out = dict(U=U, d_inst_primary=d_inst_P, d_inst_sens=d_inst_S, Rnu_ls=Rnu_ls, T_ls=T_ls, reading1={}, reading2
={})
print('\nREADING 1 (frozen link law) predictions for delta_c WITH nu-nu, from delta_c without = 0.52639:')
for refname, di in (('primary d_inst=0.53379', d_inst_P), (f'sens d_inst={d_inst_S:.5f}', d_inst_S)):
G0 = Gof(d_without, di); t0 = tau(G0)
for wname, ue in U.items():
for rate in (1.11, 1.19, 1.20):
dpred, Gp = inv(t0 + rate*ue, di)
key = f'{refname} | {wname} | rate {rate}'
out['reading1'][key] = dict(u_eff=ue, r_eff=float(np.exp(2*ue)), G_without=G0, G_with_pred=Gp,
dG_pred=Gp - G0, delta_with_pred=dpred)
print(f' {key}: u_eff={ue:.4f} (r_eff {np.exp(2*ue):.3f}) G_without={G0:.4f} -> G_with={Gp:.4f}
dG={Gp-G0:+.4f} delta_c(with)={dpred:.5f}')
# READING 2: timing. Energy-weighted mean rate = H.
def Tdec_eff(nunu):
m = np.array([np.sum(W*(RE0[j] + (RN0[j] if nunu else 0.0))) for j in range(len(Tb))])
j = np.argmax(m < 1.0)
# log interpolation between j-1 and j
x0, x1 = np.log(m[j-1]), np.log(m[j]); t0_, t1_ = np.log(Tb[j-1]), np.log(Tb[j])
return float(np.exp(t0_ + (0 - x0)*(t1_ - t0_)/(x1 - x0)))
Td_with = Tdec_eff(True); Td_without = Tdec_eff(False)
fam_T = np.array([0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0]); fam_d = np.array([0.51586, 0.52076, 0.52583, 0.52837, 0.53065])
def fam(T): return float(np.interp(np.log(T), np.log(fam_T), fam_d))
dw, dwo = fam(Td_with), fam(Td_without)
print(f'\nREADING 2 (timing): Tdec_eff with nu-nu = {Td_with:.3f} MeV, without = {Td_without:.3f} MeV')
print(f' single-rate family at T_H = 1 MeV -> delta_c(with) = {dw:.5f}, delta_c(without) = {dwo:.5f}, shift {
dw-dwo:+.5f}')
for refname, di in (('primary', d_inst_P), ('sens', d_inst_S)):
print(f' [{refname} d_inst={di:.5f}] G(with) = {Gof(dw, di):.4f}, G(without) = {Gof(dwo, di):.4f}, dG = {
Gof(dw, di)-Gof(dwo, di):+.4f}')
out['reading2'][refname] = dict(Tdec_with=Td_with, Tdec_without=Td_without, d_with=dw, d_without=dwo,
dG_pred=Gof(dw, di) - Gof(dwo, di), G_with=Gof(dw, di), G_without=Gof(dwo,
di))
# Reading 2, variant: anchored on the measured no-nu-nu threshold (shift only, from the family's slope)
print(f' anchored on measured 0.52639: delta_c(with) = {0.52639 + dw - dwo:.5f}')
json.dump(out, open('step3_predictions.json', 'w'), indent=1)

# SCRIPT: A2_STEP4_COMPARE
"""Compare the step-3 predictions with McCormick's measured T_H = 1 MeV multigroup thresholds; kill lines from

def Gof(d, di): return (d - d_fluid)/(di - d_fluid)

A2b_gate_operator.py: operator, energy and momentum gates.
# SCRIPT: A2B_GATE_OPERATOR
"""Gate for the r1 variant (nnmode = 2), single frozen group, through the compiled rhs()/state() of the copies
.
Why this checks what it claims: the collision term is the only part of dI that depends on (kbe, kbn) except
through the
plasma momentum kq -> ae -> lapse A and the angular term mu*ac. With a uniform, enormous plasma density (e ~ 1
e60) the
pressure gradient is zero and ae = kq/(e+p) ~ 1e-60, so A = 1 exactly in double precision and mu*ac*I is ~1e
-60 of the
collision term. Then dI(kbe,kbn) - dI(0,0) IS the collision term, from the compiled code itself. Columns are
taken on
perturbations orthogonal to the constant (so eps and hence kap_n are unchanged), and the constant column is C
(1)."""
import os, sys, numpy as np
which = sys.argv[1]
# 'r1' or 'orig'
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.environ.get('NU_WORK', '.'), 'A2b_decisive', which))
import ms_mg
nn = 2 if which == 'r1' else 0
K = 32; N = 4; PI = np.pi
mue = np.linspace(-1, 1, K+1); dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]; mu = 0.5*(mue[:-1]+mue[1:])
le = np.linspace(100.0, 180.0, 50); lp = le + np.log(1/3); lg = le/4; lT = le/4 - 30.0
lchi = np.zeros(50)
R = np.linspace(1.0, 2.0, N+1); U = np.zeros(N+1); Gam = np.ones(N+1)
e = np.full(N, np.exp(140.0)); Tb = float(np.exp(140.0/4 - 30.0)); pe = 4.0
rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
def dI_of(I, kbe, kbn, enub):
dR = np.zeros(N+1); dU = np.zeros(N+1); dG = np.zeros(N+1); de = np.zeros(N); dI = np.zeros(I.shape)
ms_mg.rhs(0.0, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, le, lp, lg, lT, np.array([kbe]), np.array([kbn]), Tb, enub, pe, 1,
0.1, 0.0,
np.zeros(1), dR, dU, dG, de, dI, lchi, 0.0, nn)
return dI
I0 = (1.0 + 0.3*rng.standard_normal((1, N, K)))
eps0 = 2*PI*I0[0].sum(axis=1)*dmu
ke, kbn = 0.7, 1.9
enub = float(eps0[1])
# kap_n = kbn * eps/enub; cell 1 then has kap_n = kbn exactly
kn = kbn*eps0/enub
def coll(I): return dI_of(I, ke, kbn, enub) - dI_of(I, 0.0, 0.0, enub)
# discrete orthonormal Legendre basis on the bin centres, inner product sum_k f g dmu
V = np.vander(mu, K, increasing=True); Q, _ = np.linalg.qr(V*np.sqrt(dmu)); Q = Q/np.sqrt(dmu)
for j in range(K):
if Q[:, j] @ V[:, j] < 0: Q[:, j] *= -1
i = 1
Cmat = np.zeros((K, K))
const = np.ones((1, N, K)); Cmat[:, 0] = coll(const)[0, i]
for j in range(1, K):
Ij = I0.copy(); Ij[0, i] = I0[0, i] + 1.0*Q[:, j]
Cmat[:, j] = coll(Ij)[0, i] - coll(I0)[0, i]
# operator in the Legendre basis: L = Q^T W C Q with W = dmu (Q orthonormal under that product)
L = (Q.T*dmu) @ Cmat
kk = ke + kn[i]
target = -kk*np.diag([0.0] + [1.0]*(K-1)) if nn == 2 else -np.diag([0.0, ke] + [ke+kn[i]]*(K-2))
print(f'{which}: nnmode {nn}, k_e = {ke}, k_n(cell) = {kn[i]:.15f}')

109

print(' max |L - target| =', np.abs(L - target).max(), ' (|target| ~', np.abs(target).max(), ')')
print(' diag(L)[:5] =', np.round(np.diag(L)[:5], 15))
print(' eigenvalues (sorted, first 4):', np.round(np.sort(np.linalg.eigvals(L).real)[::-1][:4], 14), '... min
', np.linalg.eigvals(L).real.min())
# nonlinear check on random anisotropic I in every cell: C(I) vs closed form
C = coll(I0)[0]
if nn == 2:
closed = -(ke + kn)[:, None]*(I0[0] - (eps0/(4*PI))[:, None])
else:
q = 2*PI*(I0[0]*mu).sum(axis=1)*dmu; cs = q/(2*PI*(mu*mu*dmu).sum())
closed = ke*((eps0/(4*PI))[:, None] - I0[0]) + kn[:, None]*((eps0/(4*PI))[:, None] + cs[:, None]*mu[None,
:] - I0[0])
print(' C(I) vs closed form, all cells: max rel err =', np.abs(C - closed).max()/np.abs(closed).max())
# energy and momentum moments of the collision term (per cell)
dE = 2*PI*C.sum(axis=1)*dmu; dQ = 2*PI*(C*mu).sum(axis=1)*dmu
q0 = 2*PI*(I0[0]*mu).sum(axis=1)*dmu
print(' energy moment of collisions / (k eps):', np.abs(dE).max()/np.abs((ke+kn)*eps0).max())
# momentum handed to the plasma: from state(); moderate plasma density so ae = kq/(e+p) is resolved
e_mod = np.full(N, np.exp(110.0))
Tb_mod = float(np.exp(110.0/4 - 30.0))
# same local/background temperature ratio (= 1) as the collision
run
args = (0.0, R, U, Gam, e_mod, I0, mue, le, lp, lg, lT, np.array([ke]), np.array([kbn]), Tb_mod, enub, pe,
lchi, 0.0)
st = ms_mg.state(*args, nn) if which == 'r1' else ms_mg.state(*args)
ae = st[8]; p_mod = np.exp(np.interp(110.0, le, lp))
kq_code = ae[N]*(e_mod[-1] + p_mod)
# ae[N] = kq[N-1]/(e+p)
loss = -dQ[N-1]
# neutrino momentum removed by collisions in the last cell
print(f' momentum: neutrino collisional loss = {loss:.15e}, handed to plasma (code kq) = {kq_code:.15e}, '
f'rel diff = {abs(loss-kq_code)/abs(loss):.2e}')
print(f' (nu-e part alone k_e q = {ke*q0[N-1]:.15e})')

A2b_PREREGISTERED.txt: kill lines and results.
PREREGISTERED — Task A2b (the two decisive simulations proposed by A2)
Written 2026-11-11T14:09Z, before any simulation in this folder was run and before the variant code was
written.
DISCLOSURE: A2's numbers are known (its REPORT.md and PREREGISTERED.txt were read): McCormick's measured
thresholds at
T_H = 1 MeV (T0 = 10, G = 6, x 0.4..20, N = 300, Nmu = 32): with nu-nu (nnmode 0) 0.51941 +- 0.00016, without
0.52639
+- 0.00011; delta_fluid = 0.49742; A2's delta_inst stand-in 0.53379.
RUN 1 — variant 'r1' of McCormick's multigroup solver (copies in r1/; originals in orig/, unchanged).
The nnmode-0 nu-nu term A kap_n (eps_s/4pi + c_s mu - I) is replaced (as nnmode = 2) by A kap_n (eps_s/4pi - I
), and
kap_n,s q_s is added to the momentum handed to the plasma (kq). Then for each group the collision operator is
-(kap_e + kap_n)(1 - P0): flux and every l >= 2 relax at the same rate (link ratio r = 1), while the l >= 2 (
isotropy)
rate kap_e + kap_n is exactly the one of the archived nu-nu run, so the total isotropy history is unchanged.
Why this decides: the timing reading says the threshold follows only the isotropy (total-rate) history, which
is the
same as in McCormick's nu-nu run, so it predicts the same threshold, 0.5194. The link law says the threshold
reads the
flux-shear link rapidity (1/2) ln r; at r = 1 with flux rate kap_e + kap_n it predicts about 0.5204 +- 0.0003
(A2).
Bisection: T0 = 10 MeV, mode = phys, G = 6, x_lo = 0.4, x_hi = 20, N = 300, K = 32, nnmode = 2, start bracket
0.517-0.523 (widened only if an endpoint check fails), tolerance width 2e-4 (+-1e-4), McCormick's bisect_mg
logic:
only BH moves hi, only dispersed moves lo, anything else stops the bisection.
KILL LINES (A2's, copied):
result above 0.5194 by more than 0.0005 (delta_c > 0.5199)
-> favours the link law (A2 predicts 0.5204
+- 0.0003)
result within +-0.0003 of 0.5194 (0.5191 <= delta_c <= 0.5197) -> favours timing
anything else (0.5197 < delta_c <= 0.5199, or delta_c < 0.5191) -> neither; reported as such.
The bisection half-width is reported with the result; if the bracket straddles a kill line, that is said.
Gates before the bisection (setup, not deciding): single frozen group, discrete operator equals
-(k_e+k_n) diag(0,1,1,...) in the discrete Legendre basis; energy conserved and momentum exchange balancing (

110

neutrino
momentum loss = momentum handed to plasma), to machine precision. Regression: with kappa_n = 0 (nunu = 0) the
variant
(nnmode 2) reproduces the unmodified code bit for bit up to eps = 0.6.
RUN 2 — instantaneous free-streaming threshold at T_H = 1 MeV with the full EOS (McCormick's run_eos /
bisect_eos,
kind 'fs', T0 = 10, his defaults N = 400, K = 32), to +-2e-4 (tolerance width 4e-4). No kill line; it replaces
A2's
delta_inst stand-in 0.53379, and A2's step3/step4 scripts (copied) are rerun with it. Expectation (untested):
near
0.533, since R_nu = 0.488 at T > 1 MeV.
Results are appended below this line; nothing above is edited afterwards.

[2026-11-11T14:50Z] GATES (setup; gate_operator.py -> gate_operator_r1.txt / gate_operator_orig.txt; regress.
py, regress_compare.py)
r1 (nnmode 2), one frozen group, Nmu = 32, k_e = 0.7, k_n = 1.9, operator from the compiled rhs():
max |L - (-(k_e+k_n) diag(0,1,...,1))| = 5.3e-15 (entries 2.6); eigenvalues 0 and 31 x -2.6.
C(I) vs -(k_e+k_n)(I - eps/4pi) on random anisotropic I: rel err 1.2e-15. Energy moment of collisions 1.5e
-16 (rel).
momentum: neutrino collisional loss -1.219742786974912e-01 = momentum handed to plasma -1.219742786974893e
-01 (rel 1.5e-14).

B.22 The recorder in McCormick’s multigroup solver (Section 12)
A3_PREREGISTERED.txt: definitions, readings and results.
A3 -- two-cut quantities read out of McCormick's multigroup model (nnmode=1). Written 2026-11-11T14:06:02Z,
before any of the four runs.
(Only the eps=0.6 bit-for-bit regression has been run so far: identical I and e, 3342 steps; log_regress.txt.)
RUNS. nnmode=1, mode=phys, G=6, x 0.4-20, N=300, K=32, all other options at McCormick's defaults; recorder
every 20 steps.
T_H=1 MeV (T0=10): archived bisection edges in res_mg_phys6_10.0.json: 0.51925 dispersed, 0.5195625 BH (
nnmode=0).
T_H=0.51 MeV (T0=5): archived edges in res_mg_phys6_5.0.json: 0.52475 dispersed, 0.525125 BH (nnmode=0).
These edges were bisected with nnmode=0. Whether they still split under nnmode=1 is untested; Table VIII
only shows
the +-0.001 pair kept. Rule set now: if an edge pair does not split, that is reported as a setup result, and
the pair is
replaced by the archived nnmode=1 pair of Table VIII (validation/res_test_boosted_*: 0.518406/0.520406 at T0
=10,
0.523938/0.525938 at T0=5), which is known to split.
DEFINITIONS (recorder; cells: centre cell i=0, and the cell whose Lagrangian centre is nearest r=r_m=1, i=95).
Discrete Legendre moments: p_l, l=0..4, Gram-Schmidt of 1,mu,..,mu^4 on McCormick's 32 midpoint bins with
<f,g> = (1/2) sum_k f_k g_k dmu (so p_0=1 and I_0 = eps_s/4pi exactly); I_l = <p_l, I>.
Kept share (v5.1 Sec. 6, A=|v_0|^2 with ||v||=1, read on the local angular chain instead of a Fourier mode):
A = I_0^2 / ||I||^2, ||I||^2 = <I,I> over all 32 bins (all discrete moments). Also A_4 = I_0^2/sum_{l<=4}
I_l^2.
Computed per (flavour, group), per flavour (summed intensity), and for the whole gas (sum of all 12
intensities).
Link rapidity per group (v5.1 Sec. 7; brief item 2: k1=k_e, k2=k_e+k_n): rho_s = (1/2) ln[(k_e,s+k_n,s)/k_e,
s],
r_s = 1 + k_n,s/k_e,s, with the LOCAL rates kap_e, kap_n returned by McCormick's state().
Caveat fixed now: with nnmode=1 the nu-nu target is a common boosted frame, so k_n leaves only the total
momentum
alone, not each group's dipole; D=diag(0,k_e,k_e+k_n,...) is exact for nnmode=0 and for the gas as a whole
.
Energy-frame velocity of the whole neutrino gas: v_L = 2q/[e+P_r+sqrt((e+P_r)^2-4q^2)] from the summed e, q,
P_r.
Speed-limit ratio: R_SL = (dA/dtau)^2 / (4 E_B A(1-A)) for the whole-gas kept share, tau = proper time of
the cell
(dtau = lapse*dt, finite difference between records), E_B = k^2/3 with k = sqrt(3)/R(r_m) (the rms k of v5
.1's
profile weight k^2 e^{-k^2/2} is sqrt(3)/r_m), so E_B = 1/R(r_m)^2. The bound's hypotheses (one Fourier
mode,
flat space, ||v|| non-increasing) do not hold in the GR model, so R_SL > 1 would not be a violation; it is
a reading.

112

PREDICTION (i) -- where the collapsing and dispersing runs first separate (same T_H, values compared at equal
eps by
interpolating the BH run onto the dispersing run's eps records, at the r_m cell):
separation of a quantity X at eps = first record where |X_BH - X_disp| > 0.01 * max_{eps'<=eps} |X_disp|.
Predicted: for X = v_L and X = 1-A (whole gas) the first 1% separation lies in 3.0 <= eps <= 5.5 at both T_H
, and the
two quantities separate within 0.5 of each other in eps. Kept share A itself (not 1-A) differs by more than
1%
only after eps=4, if at all, before the earlier run stops.
Why: the edges differ by 6e-4 relative in amplitude; before the critical phase every field differs at that
level;
a factor ~16 of growth is needed, which only the near-critical phase after the first compaction maximum (eps
~1.7)
supplies; the archived edge runs stop at eps 6.17/6.35 (T0=10) and 6.03/6.46 (T0=5).
KILL: first 1% separation of v_L or of 1-A before eps=3.0 or after 5.5 (or never before the earlier stop) at
either
T_H; or v_L and 1-A separating more than 0.5 apart in eps.
PREDICTION (ii) -- link rapidity of the dominant energy group (largest eps_s among the 12 slots at the r_m
cell) over
the readout epoch 1.7 <= eps <= 4.
Predicted: the dominant group is a nu_mu,tau group; its r = 1+k_n/k_e is about 6-9 (rapidity 0.9-1.1),
OUTSIDE v5.1's admissible window 3/4 <= r <= 3/2 (rapidity -0.144..+0.203), on the high side, at both T_H
and in all
four runs. The nu_e groups sit at r about 2.2-2.8, also outside.
Why: McCormick App. C: nu-nu faster than nu-e+- by 1.5 (nu_e) and 7 (nu_mu,tau) at E~T; the local factor
(T_nu/T)^4/chi_e grows as e+- annihilate below ~0.5 MeV, which pushes r up, not down.

A3_analyse.py: separation epochs and link rapidity.
# SCRIPT: analyse_A3
"""Derived two-cut quantities from raw_<label>.json (recorder output), the preregistered separation readings,
the compact
JSON and the plots. usage: python3 analyse.py <label> [<label> ...]"""
import sys, os, json, numpy as np
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
CELLS = ('centre', 'rm')
def derive(raw):
G, NF = raw['G'], raw['NF']; rec = raw['rec']
out = dict(eps=np.array([x['eps'] for x in rec]), t=np.array([x['t'] for x in rec]), Tb=np.array([x['Tb']
for x in rec]),

113

Rm=np.array([x['Rm'] for x in rec]), Cmax=np.array([x['Cmax'] for x in rec]))
for c, name in enumerate(CELLS):
g = lambda k: np.array([x['cells'][c][k] for x in rec])
mom = g('mom'); n2 = g('norm2'); es = g('es'); qs = g('qs'); ps = g('ps'); ke = g('ke'); kn = g('kn')
momT = g('momT'); nT = g('normT'); momF = g('momF'); nF = g('normF'); lapse = g('A')
e, q, P = es.sum(1), qs.sum(1), ps.sum(1)
d = {}
d['vL'] = 2*q/(e + P + np.sqrt(np.maximum((e+P)**2 - 4*q*q, 0.0)))
d['q_e'] = q/e; d['P_e'] = P/e
d['A'] = momT[:, 0]**2/nT
# whole-gas kept share, full discrete norm
d['one_minus_A'] = 1.0 - d['A']
d['A4'] = momT[:, 0]**2/np.sum(momT**2, axis=1)
d['ratio_l'] = np.abs(momT[:, 1:])/momT[:, :1]
# |I_l|/I_0, l=1..4, whole gas
d['AF'] = momF[:, :, 0]**2/nF
# per flavour
d['As'] = mom[:, :, 0]**2/n2
# per (flavour, group)
d['qs_es'] = qs/es; d['ps_es'] = ps/es
d['r_s'] = 1.0 + kn/ke; d['rho_s'] = 0.5*np.log(d['r_s'])
d['ke_H'] = ke/np.array([x['Hb'] for x in rec])[:, None]; d['kn_H'] = kn/np.array([x['Hb'] for x in
rec])[:, None]
d['dom'] = np.argmax(es, axis=1)
d['rho_dom'] = d['rho_s'][np.arange(len(rec)), d['dom']]; d['r_dom'] = d['r_s'][np.arange(len(rec)), d
['dom']]
d['es_share'] = es/e[:, None]
# speed-limit ratio for the whole-gas kept share, proper time of the cell, E_B = 1/R(r_m)^2
tau = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(0.5*(lapse[1:]+lapse[:-1])*np.diff(out['t']))])
A = d['A']; dA = np.gradient(A, tau)
EB = 1.0/out['Rm']**2
d['R_SL'] = np.where(1-A > 1e-10, dA**2/(4*EB*A*np.maximum(1-A, 1e-300)), np.nan)
# undefined where
1-A is at roundoff
d['lapse'] = lapse
out[name] = d
return out
def first_sep(a, b, key, cell='rm', thr=0.01, rel_to_self=False, skip=0):
"""first eps (on a's records, within the common range) where |X_b - X_a| > thr * running max |X_a|"""
ea, eb = a['eps'], b['eps']; m = ea <= min(ea.max(), eb.max())
get = lambda z: z[cell][key] if key in z[cell] else z[key]
Xa = get(a)[m]; Xb = np.interp(ea[m], eb, get(b))
scale = np.abs(Xa) if rel_to_self else np.maximum.accumulate(np.abs(Xa))
idx = np.nonzero(np.abs(Xb - Xa) > thr*scale)[0]
idx = idx[idx >= skip]
return (float(ea[m][idx[0]]) if len(idx) else None), float(ea[m].max())
if __name__ == '__main__':
labels = sys.argv[1:]
raws = {L: json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, f'raw_{L}.json'))) for L in labels}
D = {L: derive(raws[L]) for L in labels}
for L in labels:
r = raws[L]; d = D[L]; m = (d['eps'] >= 1.7) & (d['eps'] <= 4.0)
print(f"{L}: delta={r['delta']} {r['status']} eps_end={r['eps_end']:.3f} TH={r['TH']:.4f} records={len
(r['rec'])} wall={r['seconds']/60:.1f} min")
for cell in CELLS:
c = d[cell]
doms = sorted(set(int(x) for x in c['dom'][m]))
print(f"
{cell}: readout 1.7-4: dominant slots {doms} (s=f*G+g); r_dom range [{c['r_dom'][m].min
():.3f}, {c['r_dom'][m].max():.3f}] "
f"rho_dom [{c['rho_dom'][m].min():.3f}, {c['rho_dom'][m].max():.3f}]; nu_e r range [{c['r_s
'][m][:, :6].min():.3f},{c['r_s'][m][:, :6].max():.3f}] "
f"nu_mutau r range [{c['r_s'][m][:, 6:].min():.3f},{c['r_s'][m][:, 6:].max():.3f}]")
print(f"
max|v_L| {np.abs(c['vL']).max():.4f} at eps {d['eps'][np.argmax(np.abs(c['vL']))]:.3
f}; 1-A max {c['one_minus_A'].max():.3e}; "
f"min A {c['A'].min():.6f}; max R_SL (eps 0.2-end) {np.nanmax(c['R_SL'][d['eps'] > 0.2]):.3e
}; "
f"max R_SL in 1.7-4 {np.nanmax(c['R_SL'][m]):.3e}; max |q_s|/e_s {np.abs(c['qs_es']).max()
:.3f}")
pairs = [(a, b) for a, b in (('T10_lo', 'T10_hi'), ('T5_lo', 'T5_hi'), ('T10b_lo', 'T10b_hi'), ('T5b_lo',
'T5b_hi')) if a in D and b in D]
for a, b in pairs:

114

for cell in CELLS:
s = {k: first_sep(D[a], D[b], k, cell) for k in ('vL', 'one_minus_A', 'A', 'Cmax')}
print(f"
separation {a} vs {b} [{cell}]: " + '; '.join(f"{k} first >1% at eps {v[0]} (common
range to {v[1]:.3f})" for k, v in s.items()))
s2 = {k: first_sep(D[a], D[b], k, cell, skip=1) for k in ('vL', 'one_minus_A', 'A', 'Cmax')}
print(f"
same, first record (X = 0 to roundoff, metric undefined) excluded: " + '; '.join(f"{
k} {v[0]}" for k, v in s2.items()))

B.23 The restart test (Section 11)
A4_PREREGISTERED.txt: every prediction, kill line and result, in time order.
# SCRIPT: A4-PREREGISTERED (preregistration text, not code)
Written: 2026-11-11T14:04Z (UTC). Before any threshold run and before the linear-level check.
Only the restart-surface verification (check_restart_out.txt: moments, positivity) had been run.
SETUP
Solver: copies of S. McCormick's ms_nuc.py / ms_fluid.py (commit eb7c4de; ms_nuc physics identical to the
41a0f83 era used by v5.1), single-rate collision model (the r=1 case), driver restart.py = McCormick's
run_nuc.py loop with
kappa(t) = (1/2t)(eps_dec/eps)^3 re-evaluated at each SSPRK3 stage time, Gaussian profile, R_nu = 0.409,
eps_dec = 2, n = 3, N = 400, Nmu = 32, eps0 = 0.1. The step that would cross t_s is shortened to land on t_s
exactly.
Restart surface eps_s in {2.0, 0.5}. At eps_s, in every cell i: I_ik -> I_ik + a (eps_i/4pi) p_l(mu_k),
p_l the discrete orthogonal polynomial on the 32 bin midpoints (weight dmu), scaled to the leading
coefficient of P_l.
This is I(1 + a P_l) applied to the isotropic part of each cell; applying it to the full anisotropic I would
move l=2
(coupling of l=1 with P_3), so the carrier is the isotropic part. It changes only the discrete l component.
Pair: a = +0.5 and a = -0.5, for l = 3 (main) and l = 4.
Positivity bounds (from check_restart_out.txt): isotropic 32-bin grid |a| < 1/max|p_3| = 1.21498 for l=3;
l=4: a < 2.35086, -a < 1.38855. On the actual delta=0.5109 states: eps_s=2: l=3 +0.8435/-0.8325, l=4
+2.026/-0.951;
eps_s=0.5: l=3 1.212/1.212, l=4 2.350/1.386. a = 0.5 is inside every bound.
Verified at the restart: discrete l=0,1,2 components, e_nu, q, P_r of the two members agree to <= 9e-16
relative per cell;
fluid and metric arrays bit-identical.
THRESHOLD SEARCH
Trisection, one process, strict endpoint rule: only a detected apparent horizon ('BH': 2M/R >= 1 with U < 0)
lowers hi,
only McCormick's dispersal rule ('dispersed') raises lo; 'BH_breakdown', 'crash', 'undecided' never move a
bracket.
Endpoints verified by runs for each member. Start bracket [0.5095, 0.5125] (width 3e-3), five rounds -> full
width
w = 3e-3/243 = 1.23e-5 (target <= 2e-5). eps_max = 15. Thresholds quoted as midpoint, full width w.
PREDICTIONS (from Jeromie's matched-pair witness: survival exp[-(eps_dec/eps_s)^n / n]; v5.1 table at tau_s/
eps_dec)
P1 eps_s = 2.0 (tau_s/eps_dec = 1, survival e^{-1/3} = 0.72; witness R_ret = 0.081 for l=3, 0.074 for l=4):
the hidden difference returns into density/flux/stress and MOVES the threshold:
|delta_c(+a) - delta_c(-a)| > 3 w (with w = 1.23e-5: > 3.7e-5; if w reached is larger, 3 x that w).
Kill line P1: |difference| <= 3 w at eps_s = 2 -> prediction P1 killed for that l.
P2 eps_s = 0.5 (tau_s/eps_dec = 0.25, survival e^{-64/3} = 5e-10; witness R_ret = 6e-10 (l=3), 6e-11 (l=4)):
the difference is erased in the coupled era; |delta_c(+a) - delta_c(-a)| <= 2 w.
Kill line P2: |difference| > 2 w at eps_s = 0.5 -> prediction P2 killed.
Between 2w and 3w at eps_s = 2: inconclusive, reported as such (bracket tightened if time allows).
OPPOSITE READING (registered as the alternative): the nonlinear collapse washes out l >= 3 regardless of
timing:
both pairs agree within 2 w. P1's kill line is this reading's success line.
SIGN: Jeromie's corpus does not give the sign of a threshold shift from hidden l=3 structure. The P3 slab
result
(audit: d/dt (P_r+ - P_r-) = -(4/35) e a') gives, for a centre-peaked e and +a, an early rise of core
radial pressure
in the + member; whether radial pressure here resists collapse (neutrino pressure gradient) or aids it (
pressure

115

gravitates, -4 pi R P_r in dU/dt) is not settled by it. NO SIGN IS PREDICTED.
Magnitude (lean, not a kill line): Dc_3/c_0 = 0.14 at the restart, witness return ~8% of that, the full freestreaming
shift ~0.03 in delta -> |difference| of order 1e-4 at eps_s = 2.
NUMERICAL GUARD
Both members use identical code, grid and step rule; the pair at a = 0 vs no restart is not used for the
verdict.
Any member whose run logs min I < 0 is reported with that value; a crash/breakdown at a trial point is
reported.
=================== RESULTS (appended below; nothing above edited) ===================
--- 2026-11-11T14:09Z RESULT of the uniform-a linear check (linear_check.py, run after the text above, before
any threshold run)
Uniform a = 0.5 in every cell, delta = 0.5109 (file linear_check_uniform_out.txt):
eps_s=2, l=3: dCmax(+ minus -) = -2.3e-4 (eps 2.5), -2.6e-3 (eps 3), -6.5e-2 (eps 4); centre nu density
differs by 91% of base at eps 2.5.
eps_s=2, l=4: dCmax = +1.2e-4, +1.3e-3, +1.3e-2.
eps_s=0.5, l=3: dCmax = -4.2e-5, -7.3e-5, -2.5e-4; l=4: -2.1e-5, -2.9e-5, -8.3e-5; max_cell |dP_r|/e_nu ~
2e-3.
The eps_s=0.5 entries are ~1e6 x above the survival law (5e-10). Diagnosis (computed, not a fit): a uniform a
is irregular
at r=0 (a regular l-th angular moment vanishes like r^l), and at the centre cells (dR ~ 0.03 at eps 0.5, 1/
kappa ~ 0.04)
streaming across one cell is as fast as collisions, so (k/sigma)^l ~ 1 at grid scale. The uniform-a pair
therefore
measures a centre artefact, not the hidden-moment door. It is NOT used for any threshold verdict.

A4_restart.py: driver: stage-time rate, exact landing on the restart surface, the hidden perturbation.
# SCRIPT: A4-RESTART McCormick run_nuc.py loop (r=1 single-rate collision model, unchanged physics) split at
a restart surface eps_s
"""
Driver for the matched-pair restart test.
Same loop as McCormick's run_nuc.py (ms_nuc.py rhs/state/timestep imported unchanged), with:
(1) kappa(t) = (1/2t)(eps_dec/eps)^n re-evaluated at EACH SSPRK3 stage time (audit item 1), kapcap 5e5 (
inactive here);
(2) the step that would cross t_s is shortened to land exactly on t_s = eps_s^2/(4 t0);
(3) at t_s the state (t, R, U, Gam, e, I, peakC) is saved; the continuation starts from a saved state, with
an
optional perturbation dI_ik = a * (eps_i/4pi) * p_l(mu_k) that changes only the discrete l component.
Status 'BH' only from a detected apparent horizon (2M/R >= 1 with U < 0); 'dispersed' from McCormick's rule
(eps > 3 and max C < peakC/2); a solver breakdown is 'BH_breakdown' or 'crash' and never moves a bracket.
"""
import numpy as np, os, time
from ms_nuc import rhs, state, timestep, rho_b
from ms_fluid import initial_data, make_grid
FNU = 0.409; N = 400; K = 32; EPS_DEC = 2.0; NEXP = 3; KAPCAP = 5.0e5
RMAX = 9.0; BETA = 3.0; EPS0 = 0.1; CFL_R = 0.4; CFL_MU = 0.4; CH = 0.005
def kap_of(t, t0):
return (1/(2*t))*min((EPS_DEC/(2*np.sqrt(t*t0)))**NEXP, KAPCAP)
def discrete_legendre(mue, lmax=4):
"""Discrete orthogonal polynomials on the bin midpoints, weight dmu, leading coefficient of continuous P_l
."""
mu = 0.5*(mue[1:]+mue[:-1]); dmu = mue[1]-mue[0]
lead = {0: 1.0, 1: 1.0, 2: 1.5, 3: 2.5, 4: 35/8}
P = []
for l in range(lmax+1):
v = mu**l
for p in P:
v = v - (np.sum(v*p)*dmu/(np.sum(p*p)*dmu))*p
P.append(v*lead[l])
# mu^l minus lower part has leading coeff 1; scale to P_l's leading coefficient
return mu, np.array(P)
def initial(delta):
r = make_grid(N, RMAX, BETA)
t0, R, U, rho = initial_data(r, delta, EPS0, 0.0, 1.0)

119

e = (1-FNU)*rho
mue = np.linspace(-1, 1, K+1)
I = np.repeat((FNU*rho/(4*np.pi))[:, None], K, axis=1)
Mi = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum((4*np.pi/3)*np.diff(R**3)*rho)])
Gam = np.ones(N+1); Gam[1:] = np.sqrt(1.0 + U[1:]**2 - 2*Mi[1:]/R[1:])
return dict(t=t0, t0=t0, r=r, mue=mue, R=R, U=U, Gam=Gam, e=e, I=I, peakC=-1.0, step=0, Imin=float(I.min()
))
def evolve(st, t_stop=None, eps_max=15.0, snap_eps=(), stop_rule=True):
"""Advance state dict st. If t_stop given, stop exactly at t_stop (no stop rules applied before it)."""
t, t0 = st['t'], st['t0']; r, mue = st['r'], st['mue']
R, U, Gam, e, I = st['R'].copy(), st['U'].copy(), st['Gam'].copy(), st['e'].copy(), st['I'].copy()
peakC = st['peakC']; step = st['step']; Imin = st['Imin']
dR = np.zeros(N+1); dU = np.zeros(N+1); dG = np.zeros(N+1); de = np.zeros(N); dI = np.zeros((N, K))
snaps = {}; snap_eps = list(snap_eps); status = 'undecided'; mtwo = 0.0
dmu_ = mue[1]-mue[0]; w2 = (mue[1:]**3-mue[:-1]**3)/(3*dmu_); rec = []
while True:
kap = kap_of(t, t0)
Ib = FNU/(1-FNU)*e[-1]/(4*np.pi)
try:
M, Ac, ac, S, V = rhs(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, FNU, Ib, kap, dR, dU, dG, de, dI)
except (ZeroDivisionError, FloatingPointError):
status = 'crash'; break
epsn = 2*np.sqrt(t*t0)
twoMR = 2*M[1:]/R[1:]
C = 2*(M[1:] - (4*np.pi/3)*rho_b(t)*R[1:]**3)/R[1:]
iC = np.argmax(C); peakC = max(peakC, C[iC])
e0n = I[0].sum(); ePk = I[iC].sum()
rec.append((epsn, C[iC], e[0]/rho_b(t), 2*np.pi*e0n*dmu_/rho_b(t), (I[0] @ w2)/e0n, (I[iC] @ w2)/ePk,
r[1+iC]))
while snap_eps and epsn >= snap_eps[0] - 1e-12:
eps_c, q_c, Pr_c = state(t, R, U, Gam, e, I, mue, FNU, kap)[:3]
snaps[snap_eps.pop(0)] = dict(eps=epsn, Cmax=float(C[iC]), rC=float(r[1+iC]), ec=float(e[0]/rho_b(
t)),
nuc=float(eps_c[0]/rho_b(t)), Prc=float(Pr_c[0]/rho_b(t)), q=q_c.
copy(), Pr=Pr_c.copy(),
C=C.copy(), e=e.copy(), nu=eps_c.copy())
if t_stop is not None and t >= t_stop*(1-1e-15):
status = 'surface'; break
if stop_rule and t_stop is None:
if np.any((twoMR >= 1.0) & (U[1:] < 0)):
status = 'BH'; break
if epsn > 3.0 and C[iC] < 0.5*peakC:
status = 'dispersed'; break
if epsn > eps_max: break
try:
dt = min(timestep(R, U, e, I, mue, Gam, Ac, ac, S, CFL_R, CFL_MU), CH*2*t, (0.5/(kap*np.max(Ac))
if kap > 0 else 1e30))
if t_stop is not None and t + dt > t_stop:
dt = t_stop - t
R1 = R + dt*dR; U1 = U + dt*dU; G1 = Gam + dt*dG; e1 = e + dt*de; I1 = I + dt*dI
rhs(t+dt, R1, U1, G1, e1, I1, mue, FNU, FNU/(1-FNU)*e1[-1]/(4*np.pi), kap_of(t+dt, t0), dR, dU, dG
, de, dI)
R2 = 0.75*R + 0.25*(R1+dt*dR); U2 = 0.75*U + 0.25*(U1+dt*dU); G2 = 0.75*Gam + 0.25*(G1+dt*dG)
e2 = 0.75*e + 0.25*(e1+dt*de); I2 = 0.75*I + 0.25*(I1+dt*dI)
rhs(t+0.5*dt, R2, U2, G2, e2, I2, mue, FNU, FNU/(1-FNU)*e2[-1]/(4*np.pi), kap_of(t+0.5*dt, t0), dR
, dU, dG, de, dI)
R = R/3 + 2/3*(R2+dt*dR); U = U/3 + 2/3*(U2+dt*dU); Gam = Gam/3 + 2/3*(G2+dt*dG)
e = e/3 + 2/3*(e2+dt*de); I = I/3 + 2/3*(I2+dt*dI)
except (ZeroDivisionError, FloatingPointError):
coll = U[1:] < 0
status = 'BH_breakdown' if (coll.any() and np.max(twoMR[coll]) > 0.95) else 'crash'
break
t += dt; step += 1
if t_stop is not None and abs(t - t_stop) < 1e-14*t_stop:
t = t_stop
Imin = min(Imin, float(I.min())); mtwo = max(mtwo, float(np.max(twoMR)))
if np.any(e <= 0) or not np.all(np.isfinite(U)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(I)):

120

status = 'crash'; break
return dict(t=t, t0=t0, r=r, mue=mue, R=R, U=U, Gam=Gam, e=e, I=I, peakC=peakC, step=step, Imin=Imin,
status=status, eps=2*np.sqrt(t*t0), snaps=snaps, max2MR=mtwo, rec=np.array(rec))
KEYS = ('t', 't0', 'r', 'mue', 'R', 'U', 'Gam', 'e', 'I', 'peakC', 'step', 'Imin')
def surface_state(delta, eps_s, cache_dir):
global K
"""Run from eps0 to the restart surface eps_s (cached on disk)."""
os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
fn = os.path.join(cache_dir, f'surf_d{delta:.10f}_es{eps_s:g}_K{K}.npz')
if os.path.exists(fn):
z = np.load(fn); return {k: (z[k] if z[k].ndim else z[k].item()) for k in KEYS}
st = initial(delta)
t_s = eps_s**2/(4*st['t0'])
out = evolve(st, t_stop=t_s)
assert out['status'] == 'surface', out['status']
np.savez(fn, **{k: out[k] for k in KEYS})
return {k: out[k] for k in KEYS}
def perturb(st, l, a):
"""dI_ik = a * (eps_i/4pi) * p_l(mu_k): changes only the discrete l component of every cell."""
mu, P = discrete_legendre(st['mue'])
dmu = st['mue'][1]-st['mue'][0]
s = dict(st); I = st['I']
Ibar = I.sum(axis=1)*dmu/2.0
# = eps/(4 pi) with eps = 2 pi sum I dmu
s['I'] = I + a*Ibar[:, None]*P[l][None, :]
return s
def positivity_bound(st, l):
mu, P = discrete_legendre(st['mue']); p = P[l]
dmu = st['mue'][1]-st['mue'][0]; I = st['I']; Ibar = I.sum(axis=1)*dmu/2.0
ratio = I/Ibar[:, None]
amax_plus = np.min(ratio[:, p < 0]/(-p[p < 0])[None, :])
amax_minus = np.min(ratio[:, p > 0]/(p[p > 0])[None, :])
return float(amax_plus), float(amax_minus), float(1/np.max(-p)), float(1/np.max(p))
def moments(st):
"""Discrete l=0..4 Legendre components per cell, plus McCormick's e, q, P_r."""
mu, P = discrete_legendre(st['mue']); dmu = st['mue'][1]-st['mue'][0]
comp = np.array([st['I'] @ (P[l]*dmu) for l in range(5)])
t = st['t']; kap = kap_of(t, st['t0'])
eps, q, Pr = state(t, st['R'], st['U'], st['Gam'], st['e'], st['I'], st['mue'], FNU, kap)[:3]
return comp, eps, q, Pr
def bump(rc, r1=0.3, r2=2.0):
"""C-infinity bump, 1 at the middle of [r1, r2], identically 0 outside (regular at r=0, away from the
outer edge)."""
x = (rc - 0.5*(r1+r2))/(0.5*(r2-r1))
b = np.zeros_like(rc); m = np.abs(x) < 1
b[m] = np.exp(1.0 - 1.0/(1.0 - x[m]**2))
return b
def angvec(mue, l):
"""Angular direction on the bins: l >= 0 -> discrete p_l; l == -1 -> A5 L-infinity-optimal hidden
direction (eps_s=2, T=4)."""
if l >= 0:
return discrete_legendre(mue)[1][l]
import json
d = json.load(open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'restart_direction_A5.json')))
v = np.array(d['directions']['ed2_n3_s2_T4']['candidates']['Linf_optimal']['d_on_32_bins'], float)
assert len(v) == len(mue)-1
return v
def cell_margin(st, l):
"""Per-cell positivity margin for +/- the l perturbation on the discrete bins: largest |a_i| keeping every
bin >= 0 for both signs."""
p = angvec(st['mue'], l)

121

dmu = st['mue'][1]-st['mue'][0]; I = st['I']; Ibar = I.sum(axis=1)*dmu/2.0
ratio = I/Ibar[:, None]
mp = np.min(ratio[:, p < 0]/(-p[p < 0])[None, :], axis=1)
mm = np.min(ratio[:, p > 0]/(p[p > 0])[None, :], axis=1)
return np.minimum(mp, mm)
def perturb_bump(st, l, f):
"""dI_ik = f * b(r_i) * m_i * (eps_i/4pi) * p_l(mu_k); m_i the cell's positivity margin, b the compact
bump in r.
|f| < 1 keeps every bin positive; +f and -f are exact mirrors."""
p = angvec(st['mue'], l)
dmu = st['mue'][1]-st['mue'][0]; I = st['I']
Ibar = I.sum(axis=1)*dmu/2.0
rc = 0.5*(st['r'][1:]+st['r'][:-1])
amp = f*bump(rc)*cell_margin(st, l)
s = dict(st); s['I'] = I + (amp*Ibar)[:, None]*p[None, :]
return s
def run_member(delta, eps_s, l, a, cache_dir, eps_max=15.0, snap_eps=(), mode='uniform'):
st = surface_state(delta, eps_s, cache_dir)
if a == 0:
s2 = st
elif mode == 'bump':
s2 = perturb_bump(st, l, a)
else:
s2 = perturb(st, l, a)
s2 = dict(s2); s2['Imin'] = min(s2['Imin'], float(s2['I'].min()))
return evolve(s2, eps_max=eps_max, snap_eps=snap_eps)

A4_screen.py: paired screening runs.
# SCRIPT: A4-SCREEN D4: fixed-amplitude paired runs (+f, -f, 0) at delta 0.5100 and 0.5115, per (eps_s, l);
selection rule applied
import sys, os, json, time, numpy as np
from scipy.interpolate import CubicSpline
import restart
from restart import run_member, initial, evolve
from cpuwait import wait
F = 0.5; DELTAS = (0.5100, 0.5115)
PAIRS = [(2.0, 3), (0.5, 3), (4.0, 3), (2.0, 4), (0.5, 4), (4.0, 4), (2.0, -1)]
if len(sys.argv) > 1: PAIRS = [PAIRS[int(i)] for i in sys.argv[1].split(',')]
os.makedirs('screen', exist_ok=True)
def P(*a): print(*a, flush=True)
def get(tag, fn):
p = f'screen/{tag}.npz'
if os.path.exists(p):
z = np.load(p, allow_pickle=True); return dict(status=str(z['status']), eps=float(z['eps']), rec=z['
rec'], Imin=float(z['Imin']), max2MR=float(z['max2MR']))
w = wait(P); t1 = time.time()
o = fn()
np.savez(p, status=o['status'], eps=o['eps'], rec=o['rec'], Imin=o['Imin'], max2MR=o['max2MR'])
P(f' run {tag}: {o["status"]} at eps={o["eps"]:.3f} Imin={o["Imin"]:.3e} ({time.time()-t1:.0f}s, waited
{w:.0f}s)')
return dict(status=o['status'], eps=o['eps'], rec=o['rec'], Imin=o['Imin'], max2MR=o['max2MR'])
def spl(rec, col):
e = rec[:, 0]; m = np.concatenate([[True], np.diff(e) > 0]); return CubicSpline(e[m], rec[m, col])
summary = json.load(open('screen_res.json')) if os.path.exists('screen_res.json') else {}
for eps_s, l in PAIRS:
key = f'es{eps_s:g}_l{l}'; res = {}
P(f'== pair eps_s={eps_s} l={l} (f = +/-{F}, bump support 0.3-2 r_m)')
for d in DELTAS:
cont = get(f'cont_d{d}', lambda: evolve(initial(d)))
z = get(f'zero_d{d}_es{eps_s:g}', lambda: run_member(d, eps_s, l, 0.0, 'cache'))
p = get(f'plus_d{d}_es{eps_s:g}_l{l}', lambda: run_member(d, eps_s, l, +F, 'cache', mode='bump'))
m = get(f'minus_d{d}_es{eps_s:g}_l{l}', lambda: run_member(d, eps_s, l, -F, 'cache', mode='bump'))
hi = min(6.0, cont['eps'], z['eps'], p['eps'], m['eps']) - 0.02
g = np.linspace(eps_s + 0.02, hi, 400)
noise = max(float(np.max(np.abs(spl(z['rec'], 1)(g) - spl(cont['rec'], 1)(g)))), 1e-12)

122

dC = spl(p['rec'], 1)(g) - spl(m['rec'], 1)(g)
dPc = spl(p['rec'], 4)(g) - spl(m['rec'], 4)(g)
dPk = spl(p['rec'], 5)(g) - spl(m['rec'], 5)(g)
sep = float(np.max(np.abs(dC)))
rows = [(float(E), float(spl(p['rec'], 1)(E) - spl(m['rec'], 1)(E)), float(spl(p['rec'], 4)(E) - spl(m
['rec'], 4)(E)))
for E in (2.5, 3.0, 4.0, 5.0, 5.5, 6.0) if eps_s + 0.02 <= E <= hi + 1e-9]
res[str(d)] = dict(cont=(cont['status'], cont['eps']), zero=(z['status'], z['eps']), plus=(p['status
'], p['eps']), minus=(m['status'], m['eps']),
Imin=(p['Imin'], m['Imin']), noise=noise, sep=sep, sepPc=float(np.max(np.abs(dPc)))
, sepPk=float(np.max(np.abs(dPk))),
rows=rows, eps_hi=float(hi), C5=(float(spl(p['rec'], 1)(min(5.0, hi))), float(spl(m
['rec'], 1)(min(5.0, hi))), float(spl(z['rec'], 1)(min(5.0, hi)))))
P(f' delta={d}: outcomes cont {cont["status"]}@{cont["eps"]:.2f}, zero {z["status"]}@{z["eps"]:.2f},
+f {p["status"]}@{p["eps"]:.2f}, -f {m["status"]}@{m["eps"]:.2f}')
P(f'
record eps {eps_s+0.02:.2f}-{hi:.2f}: max|dCmax(+ - -)| = {sep:.3e}; noise floor {noise:.3e};
ratio {sep/noise:.1f};'
f' max|d(P_r/e_nu) centre| = {res[str(d)]["sepPc"]:.3e}, at C-peak cell = {res[str(d)]["sepPk"]:.3e}
')
for E, a, b in rows: P(f'
eps={E}: dCmax={a:+.3e} d(P_r/e_nu)_centre={b:+.3e}')
flip = any(res[str(d)]['plus'][0] != res[str(d)]['minus'][0] for d in DELTAS)
above = any(res[str(d)]['sep'] > 10*res[str(d)]['noise'] for d in DELTAS)
sel = flip or above
# linearised estimate: dCmax at common eps (<=5) over dCmax/d delta from the zero runs at the two deltas
E = min(res[str(d)]['eps_hi'] for d in DELTAS); E = min(E, 5.0)
def at(tag): return float(spl(np.load(f'screen/{tag}.npz')['rec'], 1)(E))
slope = (at(f'zero_d{DELTAS[1]}_es{eps_s:g}') - at(f'zero_d{DELTAS[0]}_es{eps_s:g}'))/(DELTAS[1]-DELTAS
[0])
dCE = np.mean([at(f'plus_d{d}_es{eps_s:g}_l{l}') - at(f'minus_d{d}_es{eps_s:g}_l{l}') for d in DELTAS])
est = -dCE/slope
P(f' SELECTION: outcome flip={flip}, separation>10x noise={above} -> {"TRISECT" if sel else "NOT SELECTED
(null)"};'
f' linearised estimate delta_c(+)-delta_c(-) ~ {est:+.2e} (dCmax at eps={E:.2f} = {dCE:+.3e}, dCmax/d
delta = {slope:.3f})')
summary[key] = dict(res=res, selected=sel, flip=flip, above=above, est=est, slope=slope, dCE=dCE, E=E)
json.dump(summary, open('screen_res.json', 'w'), indent=1)

A4_trisect.py: threshold search.
# SCRIPT: A4-TRISECT strict-endpoint trisection, one process, for one member (eps_s, l, f) of a restart pair
# usage: python3 trisect.py TAG eps_s l f lo hi wtarget [K]
import sys, os, json, time
import restart
from restart import run_member
from cpuwait import wait
tag = sys.argv[1]; eps_s = float(sys.argv[2]); l = int(sys.argv[3]); f = float(sys.argv[4])
lo, hi, wt = float(sys.argv[5]), float(sys.argv[6]), float(sys.argv[7])
if len(sys.argv) > 8: restart.K = int(sys.argv[8])
def P(*a): print(*a, flush=True)
log = []
def trial(d):
w = wait(P); t1 = time.time()
o = run_member(d, eps_s, l, f, 'cache', mode='bump')
log.append((d, o['status'], float(o['eps']), float(o['Imin']), float(o['max2MR'])))
P(f'{tag} delta={d:.7f}: {o["status"]} eps={o["eps"]:.3f} Imin={o["Imin"]:.2e} ({time.time()-t1:.0f}s,
waited {w:.0f}s)')
json.dump(dict(tag=tag, eps_s=eps_s, l=l, f=f, K=restart.K, lo=lo, hi=hi, log=log), open(f'trisect_{tag}.
json', 'w'), indent=1)
return o['status']
t0 = time.time()
# endpoint verification: lo must disperse, hi must form a horizon; widen by the bracket width until they do
s = trial(lo)
while s != 'dispersed':
if s == 'BH': hi = lo; lo = lo - 1.5e-3
else: lo = lo - 2e-4
# undecided/crash/breakdown: does not move the bracket;
step aside
s = trial(lo)
s = trial(hi)

123

while s != 'BH':
if s == 'dispersed': lo = hi; hi = hi + 1.5e-3
else: hi = hi + 2e-4
s = trial(hi)
while hi - lo > wt:
a = lo + (hi-lo)/3; b = lo + 2*(hi-lo)/3
sa = trial(a)
if sa == 'BH': hi = a; continue
sb = trial(b)
if sa == 'dispersed': lo = a
if sb == 'BH': hi = b
elif sb == 'dispersed': lo = b
if sa not in ('BH', 'dispersed') and sb not in ('BH', 'dispersed'):
P(f'{tag}: both trial points undecisive; bracket not moved; stopping'); break
dc = 0.5*(lo+hi)
P(f'RESULT {tag}: delta_c = {dc:.7f} bracket [{lo:.7f}, {hi:.7f}] width {hi-lo:.2e} ({(time.time()-t0)
/60:.1f} min)')
json.dump(dict(tag=tag, eps_s=eps_s, l=l, f=f, K=restart.K, lo=lo, hi=hi, dc=dc, w=hi-lo, log=log), open(f'
trisect_{tag}.json', 'w'), indent=1)
