# SCRIPT: A5-PREREGISTERED (preregistration text, not code)
Written: 2026-11-11T14:10Z (UTC). Before any computation in this folder. Only reading done so far
(v5.1 Secs 2, 5, 6, App. A.5; witness_run.py, witness_kernel.py, memory_curve.py; A4's restart.py grid).
Prior-art fetch: 4 web requests spent (2 curl downloads refused by proxy 403, 1 WebFetch refused as not on
allowlist, 1 WebSearch returned titles only). The two papers were NOT read this session.
COMMON OBJECT (same as v5.1 memory_curve.py / witness_run.py)
dv/dtau = -(i k M + D(tau)) v, M the normalized-Legendre Jacobi matrix (c_l=(l+1)/sqrt((2l+1)(2l+3))),
D = sigma(tau) diag(0,1,1,1,...) (single rate) or sigma(tau) diag(0,1,r,r,...) (split).
sigma(tau) = ed^n / tau^(n+1). Start v = |0> at tau0 = 0.02. LM = 60. Profile weights w_k ~ k^2 exp(-k^2/2)
on 36 Gauss-Legendre k in [0.25, 9]. A(tau) = sum_k w_k |v_0|^2. A_inst = same with sigma = 0.
Memory form g(e*) = (1-A(e*))/(1-A_inst(e*)), g2 = 0.89 g(1.7) + 0.11 g(4.0) (w frozen at v5.1's 0.890).
Real basis used for computation: w_l = i^l v_l, generator k K - D with K real antisymmetric.
=== TEST 1: RATE-ORDER SWAP ===
Family R (time reversal inside a window W=[ta,tb]; sigma unchanged outside W; reversed sigma_R(t)=sigma(ta+tbt) on W):
R1: n=3, ed=1, W=[0.5,1.5]
R2: n=3, ed=1, W=[0.25,1.5]
R3: n=6, ed=1, W=[0.5,1.5]
R4: n=3, ed=2, W=[1.0,3.5] (readout 1.7 lies INSIDE W; only e*=4 is after W)
R5/R6: split versions of R1 with r = 4 and r = 1/4 (sigma1=sigma, sigma2=r sigma, both reversed together).
Family S (n=6 v5.1 histories ed6 in {1,2,3} against an n=3 history whose ed3 is rescaled so that the optical
depth
int_W sigma dtau is equal): windows W in {[0.02,4] (whole run), [0.5,4], [1.0,4]}.
Readouts at e* = 1.7 and 4.0: A, the profile-weighted l<=2 moment powers |v_l|^2 (l=0,1,2), the relative
difference of the complex l<=2 vectors R_diff = sqrt(sum_k w|dv_{0..2}|^2 / sum_k w|v_{0..2}|^2), g(1.7), g
(4), g2.
PREDICTIONS (scalar summaries):
P1a Optical depth int_W sigma: identical within each pair, by construction -> BLIND to order (both families).
P1b Time-integrated connection energy int_W (1/8)||[L,L*]||^2 dtau = (k^2/3) int sigma^2 (split: k^2[s1
^2/3+4(s2-s1)^2/15]):
identical under time reversal (any int f(sigma) is) -> BLIND in family R. NOT equal in family S (n=6 vs n
=3 put
different sigma^2 mass on W): it is not order-blind there, it is simply a different number; values
reported.
P1c Instantaneous operator at readout (sigma(e*), connection energy at e*, E_B=k^2/3): identical in family R
whenever
W closes before e* -> BLIND (R1,R2,R3,R5,R6 at both e*; R4 at e*=4). Not equal in family S.
P1d The exact propagated readout is NOT blind: for every pair, the l<=2 readout differs.
KILL for P1d (per pair): R_diff < 1e-3 AND |dg(e*)| < 1e-3 at both readout epochs -> order is invisible to
the linear
readout for that pair; P1d killed for that pair.

=== TEST 2: READOUT GRAMIAN OF THE HIDDEN SECTOR ===
G(s,T) = int_s^T Q^T Phi(t,s)^T R^T R Phi(t,s) Q dt, R = rows l=0,1,2, Q = embedding of l>=3, T = 4.
Plant epochs: ed=0.5 (witness history): s = 0.125, 0.5, 1.0 (coupled / transition / free, tau_s/ed = 0.25, 1,
2);
also ed=2, n=3 (v5.1 reference): s = 0.5, 2.0, 3.0. n=3 main; n=6 at ed=0.5 as a second history.
G is computed per k, parity-symmetrized over k and -k (G(-k) = P G(k) P, P = diag((-1)^l)), and profilesummed.
Also the terminal (snapshot) Gramian Phi(T,s)^T R^T R Phi(T,s) restricted to Q (the witness's R_ret object).
P2a (algebraic): for k != 0 and every c_l != 0 the truncated chain is observable from l<=2: N_inf = {0} and G
is
positive definite. There is no exact predictive quotient smaller than the full hidden space; the quotient
that
matters is numerical (eigenvalues of G above a tolerance). Checked by rank of the Kalman matrix [R; RB;
RB^2..]
in exact-size floating point for the time-frozen generator (reported with its singular values).
P2b Coupled planting suppresses the whole spectrum of G relative to the transition planting by roughly the
square
of the hidden survival factor, exp[-2(ed^n/n)(1/s_c^n - 1/s_t^n)] (n=3, ed=0.5: s=0.125 vs 0.5 -> ~ e^{

Kill: ratio of top eigenvalues off from that factor by more than 10^{+-3}.
P2c The top eigenvector at the transition and free epochs lies in the odd-parity sector and has l=3 weight >
0.5.
Kill: top eigenvector even, or l=3 weight <= 0.5.
P2d Effective rank (eigenvalues > 1e-3 of the top) at the free epoch is larger than at the transition epoch.
Lean, kill if smaller.
Output for the restart agent: the direction d(mu) on McCormick's 32 bin midpoints, built from discrete
orthogonal

The JSON states plainly that the mapping chain -> radially uniform angular perturbation is not exact.
=== TEST 3: CLOSURE COMPARISON (linear level) ===
Histories: n=3 ed in {0.25,0.5,1,2,4,8}; n=6 ed in {1,2,3}; start |0> at tau0=0.02; horizon tau in [tau0,4].
Comparators (fixed now):
EX
exact chain, LM=60 (reference).
P1
truncation at l=1 (v_2 := 0). This is ALSO the linearized M1 closure: Levermore / Minerbo / maximumentropy
Eddington factors are chi(f)=1/3+O(f^2), so about isotropy the closed l=2 moment vanishes at linear
order.
M2
maximum-entropy (Levermore-style) closure for the l=3 moment with l<=2 evolved: f=exp(a0+a1 mu+a2 P2)
linearized
about isotropy has no l=3 part, so linearized M2 == P2. Stated as algebra; compared numerically as a
check.
P2, P3, P5
truncations at l=2, 3, 5.
CE2 P1 plus the quasi-static (Chapman-Enskog / viscous) l=2: v_2 = -i k c_1 v_1 / sigma (sigma>0), the
linear shear-viscosity closure.
OP2 P2 plus a short-memory (Markovian) approximation of the exact elimination kernel of the hidden block l
>=3:
d v_2/dt += -k^2 c_2^2 theta(t) v_2, theta(t)=int_tau0^t exp(-int_r^t sigma) dr (streaming inside the
hidden
block neglected; the "crescendo" form I recall from optimal-prediction closures -- recalled, not read).
MFe his memory form as an exact block elimination: l<=2 kept, hidden block l>=3 eliminated with its closedform
propagator exp(-[Sigma(t)-Sigma(r)]) exp(-ik(t-r)M_QQ) (A.5 with the cut moved from l=0|1 to l=2|3).
Hidden moments start empty, so the source is zero. Predicted error: numerical only (< 1e-5 relative).
MFf "frozen" memory form: the same kernel with Sigma frozen to the reference history (n=3, ed=2) for every
history,
while l<=2 use the actual sigma. Defined here as the analogue of fitting once and freezing.
Error metrics (fixed now): E_traj = sqrt( sum_k w int_tau0^4 |v^c_{0..2}-v^ex_{0..2}|^2 / sum_k w int |v^ex_{
0..2}|^2 );
E_A(e*) = |A^c-A^ex| at e*=1.7, 4; E_g = |g^c - g^ex| at 1.7, 4 and for g2.
PREDICTIONS:

=================== RESULTS (appended 2026-11-11, after the runs; nothing above edited) ===================
Setup check (t0_reproduce): real-basis chain gives g(1.7)=0.3432, g2=0.4110 (n=3 ed=2) vs v5.1 0.344/0.411; n
=3 ed=0.25 0.9404/0.9467 vs
0.941/0.947; n=6 ed=3 0.0256/0.1175 vs 0.026/0.118. (v5.1's 1.7 index is the first grid point >= 1.7, hence
the 0.001.)
TEST 1 P1a, P1b, P1c: confirmed (optical depths equal to all digits printed; int sigma^2 equal under reversal
; sigma(e*) equal
wherever W closes before e*). Family S: int sigma^2 of the n=6 member is 2.09-2.15x the n=3 member's at equal
optical depth.
P1d: HOLDS for every pair. R_diff 0.33-0.72; |dg| 0.009-0.184; dg2 R1 -0.064, R2 -0.107, R3 -0.118, R4
+0.161, R5 -0.063, R6 -0.067.
Lean (reversed member loses more): FAILED - the reversed member keeps MORE (g smaller) in R1,R2,R3,R5,R6 at
both epochs and R4 at e*=4.
Lean (family S, n=6 keeps more when W starts late): FAILED in 5 of 6 late-window cases (holds only W=[1,4],
ed6=1).
TEST 2 P2a: confirmed. (R B^j)_{2,2+j} = k^j c_2...c_{j+1} != 0 for all j: Kalman matrix full rank, N_inf={0}
, G positive definite.
Floating-point: smallest singular values 1e-29..1e-11; G eigenvalues below ~1e-19 are round-off.
P2b: KILLED for the integral Gramian (coupled/transition top ratio 2.5e-9 for n=3 ed=0.5 vs predicted ~e^{-42
}=6e-19; n=6: 5e-27 vs
e^{-1365}). Cause: the prediction left out the leakage (k/sigma)^l through damped rungs into the conserved l
=0 (v5.1's witness
predictions had it). Terminal Gramian ratio 4.8e-17 (n=3) is within 10^1.1 of the survival factor.
P2c: HOLDS (top eigenvector odd, l=3 weight 0.919, 0.923, 0.918, 0.922, 0.953, 0.990).
P2d: KILLED (effective rank free < transition: 8 vs 9 at ed=0.5, 3 vs 5 at ed=2; confounded with the shorter
remaining horizon).
TEST 3 P3a: HOLDS (entropy closures: closed moment = 0.3333 eps^2 (M1) and 0.300 eps^2 (M2) as eps->0;
linearized M1==P1, M2==P2).
P3b: HOLDS in all 9 histories. P3c: HOLDS (worst for P1,P2,P3,P5 is n=3 ed=0.25). P3d: HOLDS (MFe max
E_traj 4.6e-6).
P3e: HOLDS (smallest instantaneous-closure E_traj at ed<=0.5 is P2 0.433).
Lean (OP2 loses for early decoupling): FAILED - OP2 beats P2 in all 9 histories (ed=0.25: 0.247 vs 0.496).

A5_chain_lib.py: the chain.
# SCRIPT: A5-CHAIN-LIB linear angular chain (v5.1 memory_curve / witness object) in the real basis w_l = i^l
v_l
import os
os.environ.setdefault('OMP_NUM_THREADS', '1')
import numpy as np, scipy.sparse as sp
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
from scipy.special import roots_legendre
kn, kw = roots_legendre(36); KMIN, KMAX = 0.25, 9.0
KS = 0.5*(KMAX-KMIN)*(kn+1)+KMIN; WK = 0.5*(KMAX-KMIN)*kw
WE = WK*KS**2*np.exp(-KS**2/2); WE /= WE.sum()
TAU0 = 0.02
def cl(l):
return (l+1)/np.sqrt((2*l+1)*(2*l+3))
def Kreal(LM):
"""real generator of streaming in w basis: dw/dt = k K w;
N = LM+1; K = np.zeros((N, N))

126

K[l,l-1]=c_{l-1}, K[l-1,l]=-c_{l-1}"""

for l in range(1, N):
K[l, l-1] = cl(l-1); K[l-1, l] = -cl(l-1)
return K
def sigma_powerlaw(ed, n):
return lambda t: ed**n/t**(n+1)
def Sigma_powerlaw(ed, n):
# antiderivative, Sigma' = sigma
return lambda t: -ed**n/(n*t**n)
def reversed_in_window(sig, ta, tb):
def s(t):
return sig(ta+tb-t) if ta <= t <= tb else sig(t)
return s
def damp_vec(LM, r=1.0, shear_only=False):
d = np.ones(LM+1); d[0] = 0.0
if shear_only:
d[2] = r
else:
d[2:] = r
return d
def evolve(k, sig, LM, tgrid, w0=None, breaks=(), dvec=None, rtol=1e-9, atol=1e-12, t0=TAU0):
"""exact (truncated at LM) chain; returns w[l, t] on tgrid (real basis). sig(t) scalar rate; dvec diag
pattern."""
N = LM+1; K = sp.csr_matrix(Kreal(LM)*k)
d = np.r_[0.0, np.ones(LM)] if dvec is None else dvec
Dm = sp.diags(d, format='csr')
if w0 is None:
w0 = np.zeros(N); w0[0] = 1.0
jac = lambda t, y: (K - sig(t)*Dm)
rhs = lambda t, y: K@y - sig(t)*(d*y)
edges = [t0]+[b for b in breaks if t0 < b < tgrid[-1]]+[tgrid[-1]]
out = np.zeros((N, len(tgrid))); y = np.array(w0, float)
for a, b in zip(edges[:-1], edges[1:]):
m = (tgrid >= a) & (tgrid <= b) if b == edges[-1] else (tgrid >= a) & (tgrid < b)
te = np.unique(np.r_[tgrid[m], b])
s = solve_ivp(rhs, (a, b), y, method='BDF', jac=jac, t_eval=te, rtol=rtol, atol=atol)
assert s.success, s.message
out[:, m] = s.y[:, np.searchsorted(te, tgrid[m])]
y = s.y[:, -1]
return out
def to_complex(w):
"""v_l = (-i)^l w_l"""
l = np.arange(w.shape[0])
return ((-1j)**l)[:, None]*w if w.ndim == 2 else ((-1j)**l)*w

A5_t1_order.py: order swap.
# SCRIPT: A5-T1-ORDER rate-order swap: same int sigma over a window, different order; readouts at e*=1.7, 4
import os; os.environ['OMP_NUM_THREADS']='1'
import numpy as np, json, time
from scipy.integrate import quad
from A5_chain_lib import *
LM=60; EP=np.array([1.7,4.0]); W2=0.89
t1=time.time()
def readout(sig,breaks=(),dvec=None):
A=np.zeros(2); P=np.zeros((3,2)); V=[]
for k,w in zip(KS,WE):
wl=evolve(k,sig,LM,EP,breaks=breaks,dvec=dvec)
v=to_complex(wl[:3]); V.append(v)
A+=w*wl[0]**2; P+=w*np.abs(v)**2
return A,P,np.array(V)
# V[k, l, epoch]
Ai,_,_=readout(lambda t:0.0)
def g_of(A): return (1-A)/(1-Ai)
def cmp(name,sigF,sigR,ta,tb,brF=(),brR=(),dvec=None,r=1.0):

127

AF,PF,VF=readout(sigF,brF,dvec); AR,PR,VR=readout(sigR,brR,dvec)
dv2=np.einsum('k,kle->e',WE,np.abs(VF-VR)**2); n2=np.einsum('k,kle->e',WE,np.abs(VF)**2)
gF,gR=g_of(AF),g_of(AR)
s1=lambda f: quad(f,ta,tb,limit=400,points=[ta,tb])[0]
ce=lambda f: s1(lambda t: f(t)**2/3+4*((r-1)*f(t))**2/15)
# per k^2
row=dict(name=name,W=[ta,tb],r=r,
optdepth=[s1(sigF),s1(sigR)], conn_energy_int_per_k2=[ce(sigF),ce(sigR)],
sigma_at_readout=[[sigF(e) for e in EP],[sigR(e) for e in EP]],
A=[AF.tolist(),AR.tolist()], P_l=[PF.tolist(),PR.tolist()],
R_diff=np.sqrt(dv2/n2).tolist(), g=[gF.tolist(),gR.tolist()], dg=(gR-gF).tolist(),
g2=[float(W2*gF[0]+(1-W2)*gF[1]),float(W2*gR[0]+(1-W2)*gR[1])])
print(name,'W',[ta,tb],'r',r,'| optdepth %.6g %.6g | connE/k2 %.6g %.6g'%(*row['optdepth'],*row['
conn_energy_int_per_k2']),flush=True)
for i,e in enumerate(EP):
print('
e*=%.1f A %.5f -> %.5f | P0 %.5f %.5f P1 %.5f %.5f P2 %.5f %.5f | R_diff %.3e | g %.4f ->
%.4f (dg %+.4f) | sigma(e*) %.3g %.3g'%(
e,AF[i],AR[i],PF[0,i],PR[0,i],PF[1,i],PR[1,i],PF[2,i],PR[2,i],row['R_diff'][i],gF[i],gR[i],gR[i]gF[i],sigF(e),sigR(e)),flush=True)
print('
g2 %.4f -> %.4f'%tuple(row['g2']),flush=True)
return row
out=dict(A_inst=Ai.tolist(),family_R=[],family_S=[])
# family R: time reversal inside W
for name,n,ed,ta,tb,r in [('R1',3,1,0.5,1.5,1),('R2',3,1,0.25,1.5,1),('R3',6,1,0.5,1.5,1),('R4',3,2,1.0,3.5,1)
,
('R5',3,1,0.5,1.5,4.0),('R6',3,1,0.5,1.5,0.25)]:
s=sigma_powerlaw(ed,n); sR=reversed_in_window(s,ta,tb)
dv=None if r==1 else damp_vec(LM,r)
out['family_R'].append(dict(n=n,ed=ed,**cmp(name,s,sR,ta,tb,(ta,tb),(ta,tb),dv,r)))
# family S: n=6 v5.1 histories against n=3 rescaled to equal optical depth on W
for (ta,tb) in [(TAU0,4.0),(0.5,4.0),(1.0,4.0)]:
for ed6 in (1,2,3):
I6=ed6**6/6*(ta**-6-tb**-6); ed3=(3*I6/(ta**-3-tb**-3))**(1/3)
row=cmp('S_W[%g,%g]_ed6=%g'%(ta,tb,ed6),sigma_powerlaw(ed3,3),sigma_powerlaw(ed6,6),ta,tb)
row.update(ed3=ed3,ed6=ed6); print('
(n=3 member ed3 = %.5f; first member n=3, second n=6)'%ed3,
flush=True)
out['family_S'].append(row)
json.dump(out,open('t1_order.json','w'),indent=1)
print('time %.0fs'%(time.time()-t1))

A5_t2_gramian.py: Gramian.
# SCRIPT: A5-T2-GRAMIAN readout Gramian of the hidden sector l>=3 (R = l<=2), parity-symmetrized, profilesummed
import os; os.environ['OMP_NUM_THREADS']='1'
import numpy as np, json, time, sys
from scipy.linalg import expm
from A5_chain_lib import *
def gramian(ed, n, s, T=4.0, LM=60, dt=2e-3, ks=KS, ws=WE):
N=LM+1; K=Kreal(LM); d=np.r_[0.0,np.ones(LM)]; Sg=Sigma_powerlaw(ed,n)
nst=int(np.ceil((T-s)/dt)); tg=np.linspace(s,T,nst+1); h=tg[1]-tg[0]
G=np.zeros((N-3,N-3)); GT=np.zeros((N-3,N-3))
for k,w in zip(ks,ws):
X=np.zeros((N,N-3)); X[3:,:]=np.eye(N-3)
Gk=0.5*h*X[:3].T@X[:3]
for i in range(nst):
seff=(Sg(tg[i+1])-Sg(tg[i]))/h
# exact step-average rate
X=expm(h*(k*K-seff*np.diag(d)))@X
Gk+=(h if i<nst-1 else 0.5*h)*X[:3].T@X[:3]
GTk=X[:3].T@X[:3]
P=np.diag((-1.0)**np.arange(3,N))
# k -> -k
G+=w*0.5*(Gk+P@Gk@P); GT+=w*0.5*(GTk+P@GTk@P)
return G,GT
def summarize(G,label,ntop=3):
lam,V=np.linalg.eigh(G); idx=np.argsort(lam)[::-1]; lam=lam[idx]; V=V[:,idx]
ls=np.arange(3,3+G.shape[0])
rows=[]

128

for j in range(ntop):
v=V[:,j]; v=v*np.sign(v[np.argmax(abs(v))]); wts=v**2
par='odd' if wts[ls%2==1].sum()>0.999 else ('even' if wts[ls%2==0].sum()>0.999 else 'mixed')
rows.append(dict(eig=float(lam[j]),parity=par,l_weights={int(l):float(x) for l,x in zip(ls[:10],wts
[:10])},vec=v.tolist()))
effrank=int(np.sum(lam>1e-3*lam[0]))
print(' %s: top eigs %s | eff.rank(>1e-3 top) %d | min eig %.2e'%(label,' '.join('%.3e'%x for x in lam
[:6]),effrank,lam[-1]),flush=True)
for j,r in enumerate(rows):
print('
v%d [%s] l-weights: %s'%(j,r['parity'],' '.join('%d:%.3f'%(l,x) for l,x in list(r['
l_weights'].items())[:7])),flush=True)
return dict(eigs=lam.tolist(),effrank=effrank,top=rows)
if __name__=='__main__':
t1=time.time(); out={}
confs=[('ed0.5_n3',0.5,3,[0.125,0.5,1.0]),('ed0.5_n6',0.5,6,[0.125,0.5,1.0]),('ed2_n3
',2.0,3,[0.5,2.0,3.0])]
for name,ed,n,ss in confs:
for s,lab in zip(ss,['coupled','transition','free']):
G,GT=gramian(ed,n,s)
print('%s plant s=%g (%s) [%.0fs]'%(name,s,lab,time.time()-t1),flush=True)
out['%s_s%g'%(name,s)]=dict(ed=ed,n=n,s=s,epoch=lab,integral=summarize(G,'integral G'),terminal=
summarize(GT,'terminal G_T'))
np.save('G_%s_s%g.npy'%(name,s),G)
json.dump(out,open('t2_gramian.json','w'),indent=1)
print('time %.0fs'%(time.time()-t1))

A5_t3_closures.py: closures.
# SCRIPT: A5-T3-CLOSURES linear-level closure comparison against the exact chain, v5.1 histories (comparators
fixed in PREREGISTERED.txt)
import os; os.environ['OMP_NUM_THREADS']='1'
import numpy as np, json, time, sys
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
from scipy.linalg import expm
from A5_chain_lib import *
H=float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 1e-3
HIST=[(3,0.25),(3,0.5),(3,1),(3,2),(3,4),(3,8),(6,1),(6,2),(6,3)]
if len(sys.argv)>2: HIST=[HIST[int(i)] for i in sys.argv[2].split(',')]
TG=TAU0+np.arange(int(round((4.0-TAU0)/H))+1)*H; TG[-1]=4.0
i17=int(np.argmin(abs(TG-1.7))); i40=len(TG)-1
c0,c1,c2=cl(0),cl(1),cl(2)
LMX=60; KQQ=Kreal(LMX)[3:,3:]
def ode(rhs,jac,y0,sig):
s=solve_ivp(rhs,(TAU0,4.0),y0,method='BDF',jac=jac,t_eval=TG,rtol=1e-10,atol=1e-13); assert s.success,s.
message; return s.y
def run_CE2(k,sig):
rhs=lambda t,y: np.array([-k*c0*y[1], k*c0*y[0]-(sig(t)+k*k*c1*c1/sig(t))*y[1]])
jac=lambda t,y: np.array([[0,-k*c0],[k*c0,-(sig(t)+k*k*c1*c1/sig(t))]])
y=ode(rhs,jac,np.array([1.0,0.0]),sig); w2=k*c1*y[1]/np.array([sig(t) for t in TG]); return np.vstack([y,
w2])
def run_OP2(k,sig):
def rhs(t,y):
s=sig(t); return np.array([-k*c0*y[1], k*c0*y[0]-k*c1*y[2]-s*y[1], k*c1*y[1]-s*y[2]-k*k*c2*c2*y[3]*y
[2], 1-s*y[3]])
def jac(t,y):
s=sig(t); return np.array([[0,-k*c0,0,0],[k*c0,-s,-k*c1,0],[0,k*c1,-s-k*k*c2*c2*y[3],-k*k*c2*c2*y
[2]],[0,0,0,-s]])
return ode(rhs,jac,np.array([1.0,0,0,0]),sig)[:3]
def run_MF(k,sig,Sg_kernel):
"""l<=2 kept; hidden l>=3 eliminated: dw2 += -k^2 c2^2 int exp(-(S(t)-S(r))) g(t-r) w2(r) dr; implicit
trapezoid."""
Nt=len(TG); E=expm(H*k*KQQ); e=np.zeros(KQQ.shape[0]); e[0]=1; g=np.zeros(Nt)
for j in range(Nt): g[j]=e[0]; e=E@e
Sk=Sg_kernel(TG); sv=np.array([sig(t) for t in TG])
Y=np.zeros((3,Nt)); Y[0,0]=1.0; C=k*k*c2*c2
def Aloc(s): return np.array([[0,-k*c0,0],[k*c0,-s,-k*c1],[0,k*c1,-s]])
mem=np.zeros(Nt)
# m(t_i)

129

I3=np.eye(3)
for i in range(Nt-1):
j=np.arange(i+1); Kr=np.exp(-(Sk[i+1]-Sk[j]))*g[i+1-j]; wq=np.full(i+1,H); wq[0]=H/2
known=-C*np.sum(wq*Kr*Y[2,j])
# memory at t_{i+1} from r<t_{i+1} (trapezoid, last node weight
H/2 implicit)
Mimp=np.zeros((3,3)); Mimp[2,2]=-C*H/2 # r=t_{i+1}: exp(0) g(0)=1
lhs=I3-H/2*(Aloc(sv[i+1])+Mimp)
rhsv=Y[:,i]+H/2*(Aloc(sv[i])@Y[:,i]+np.array([0,0,mem[i]])+np.array([0,0,known]))
Y[:,i+1]=np.linalg.solve(lhs,rhsv); mem[i+1]=known-C*H/2*Y[2,i+1]
return Y
def trap(y): return np.sum(0.5*(y[1:]+y[:-1])*np.diff(TG))
Sref=Sigma_powerlaw(2.0,3)
t1=time.time()
Ainst=sum(w*evolve(k,lambda t:0.0,LMX,TG[[i17,i40]])[0]**2 for k,w in zip(KS,WE))
COMPS=['P1','M1lin','P2','M2lin','P3','P5','CE2','OP2','MFe','MFf']
res={}
for (n,ed) in HIST:
sig=sigma_powerlaw(ed,n); Sg=Sigma_powerlaw(ed,n)
num={c:0.0 for c in COMPS}; den=0.0; Ac={c:np.zeros(2) for c in COMPS+['EX']}
for k,w in zip(KS,WE):
ex=evolve(k,sig,LMX,TG,rtol=1e-10,atol=1e-13)[:3]
tr={}
for N in (1,2,3,5):
y=evolve(k,sig,N,TG,rtol=1e-10,atol=1e-13); z=np.zeros((3,len(TG))); m=min(3,N+1); z[:m]=y[:m]; tr
[N]=z
Y={'P1':tr[1],'M1lin':tr[1],'P2':tr[2],'M2lin':tr[2],'P3':tr[3],'P5':tr[5],'CE2':run_CE2(k,sig),'OP2':
run_OP2(k,sig),
'MFe':run_MF(k,sig,Sg),'MFf':run_MF(k,sig,Sref)}
den+=w*trap(np.sum(ex**2,0)); Ac['EX']+=w*ex[0,[i17,i40]]**2
for c in COMPS:
num[c]+=w*trap(np.sum((Y[c]-ex)**2,0)); Ac[c]+=w*Y[c][0,[i17,i40]]**2
gex=(1-Ac['EX'])/(1-Ainst); row={}
for c in COMPS:
gc=(1-Ac[c])/(1-Ainst)
row[c]=dict(E_traj=float(np.sqrt(num[c]/den)),E_A=np.abs(Ac[c]-Ac['EX']).tolist(),E_g=np.abs(gc-gex).
tolist(),
E_g2=float(abs(0.89*(gc[0]-gex[0])+0.11*(gc[1]-gex[1]))),g=gc.tolist())
row['EX']=dict(g=gex.tolist(),g2=float(0.89*gex[0]+0.11*gex[1]))
res['n%d_ed%g'%(n,ed)]=row
print('n=%d ed=%g exact g(1.7)=%.4f g(4)=%.4f g2=%.4f
[%.0fs]'%(n,ed,gex[0],gex[1],row['EX']['g2'],time
.time()-t1),flush=True)
for c in COMPS:
r=row[c]; print('
%-6s E_traj %.3e | E_A(1.7) %.2e E_A(4) %.2e | E_g(1.7) %.2e E_g(4) %.2e E_g2 %.2e
'%(c,r['E_traj'],*r['E_A'],*r['E_g'],r['E_g2']),flush=True)
json.dump(dict(h=H,res=res),open('t3_closures_h%g_%s.json'%(H,sys.argv[2].replace(',','-') if len(sys.argv)>2
else 'all'),'w'),indent=1)
print('time %.0fs'%(time.time()-t1))

B.26 The onset of the kept-sector pole (Section 2)
The closed pole and non-orthogonality factor of Appendix A.3a against the moment chain, and the
truncation onset against 2/π.
# SCRIPT: NU-V7-ONSET exact onset kappa_c = 2k/pi of the kept-sector pole and its non-orthogonality K = 4 phi
^2/sin^2(2 phi)
import numpy as np
from mpmath import mp, mpf, cot, atan, pi, sin
mp.dps = 30
def chain(N, kap, k=1.0):
l = np.arange(N-1); c = (l+1)/np.sqrt((2*l+1)*(2*l+3))
M = np.diag(c,1)+np.diag(c,-1)
D = np.full(N, -kap); D[0] = 0.0
return -1j*k*M + np.diag(D)
def least(N, kap):
w, V = np.linalg.eig(chain(N, kap)); i = np.argmax(w.real); return w[i], V[:, i]
print('1) exact pole from I4: s = k cot(k/kappa) - kappa, kappa_c = 2k/pi =', float(2/pi))
for kap in [1.0, 2.0, 0.8]:
phi = 1/mpf(kap); s = cot(phi) - kap
g = atan(1/(s+kap)); print(' kappa=%.2f s=%.12f 1-kappa*g=%.2e' % (kap, float(s), float(1-kap*g)))
print('2) chain eigenvalue vs exact pole and K vs 4phi^2/sin^2(2phi)')
for kap in [2.0, 1.0, 0.8, 0.7]:
w, v = least(400, kap); phi = 1/kap
Kc = (np.vdot(v, v).real)**2/abs(v @ v)**2; Ke = 4*phi**2/np.sin(2*phi)**2
print(' kappa=%.2f chain s=%.10f%+.1ej exact s=%.10f K chain=%.6f exact=%.6f' % (kap, w.real, w.imag,
np.cos(phi)/np.sin(phi)-kap, Kc, Ke))
print('3) truncation onset (least-damped mode real) vs N')
def isreal(N, kap): w, _ = least(N, kap); return abs(w.imag) < 1e-9
for N in [50, 100, 200, 400, 800]:
lo, hi = 0.55, 0.75
for _ in range(40):
m = 0.5*(lo+hi)
if isreal(N, m): hi = m
else: lo = m
print(' N=%4d onset kappa/k = %.5f
minus 2/pi = %+.5f' % (N, hi, hi-2/np.pi))
1) exact pole from I4: s = k cot(k/kappa) - kappa, kappa_c = 2k/pi = 0.6366197723675814
kappa=1.00 s=-0.357907384066 1-kappa*g=0.00e+00
kappa=2.00 s=-0.169512278288 1-kappa*g=0.00e+00
kappa=0.80 s=-0.467726582745 1-kappa*g=0.00e+00
2) chain eigenvalue vs exact pole and K vs 4phi^2/sin^2(2phi)
kappa=2.00 chain s=-0.1695122783+6.9e-17j exact s=-0.1695122783 K chain=1.412283 exact=1.412283
kappa=1.00 chain s=-0.3579073841+1.1e-16j exact s=-0.3579073841 K chain=4.837802 exact=4.837802
kappa=0.80 chain s=-0.4677265827+0.0e+00j exact s=-0.4677265827 K chain=17.449868 exact=17.449868
kappa=0.70 chain s=-0.5568083072-4.4e-15j exact s=-0.5568083072 K chain=103.656747 exact=103.656747

# SCRIPT: NU-V7-FIG-ONSET figure for the exact onset kappa_c = 2k/pi
import numpy as np, matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg'); import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import importlib.util, sys, os
plt.rcParams.update({'font.size': 11, 'font.family': 'serif'})
def chain(N, kap):
l = np.arange(N-1); c = (l+1)/np.sqrt((2*l+1)*(2*l+3))
D = np.full(N, -kap); D[0] = 0.0
return -1j*(np.diag(c,1)+np.diag(c,-1)) + np.diag(D)
kc = 2/np.pi
ka = np.linspace(kc+1e-4, 3.0, 600); s = 1/np.tan(1/ka) - ka
kd = np.array([0.66, 0.7, 0.8, 1.0, 1.3, 1.7, 2.2, 3.0])
sd = [max(np.linalg.eigvals(chain(400, x)), key=lambda z: z.real).real for x in kd]
fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(10, 3.8))
ax[0].plot(ka, s, color='#1f4e79', lw=2, label='pole $k\\cot(k/\\kappa)-\\kappa$')
ax[0].plot(ka, -ka, '--', color='#a33', lw=1.2, label='branch-cut centre $-\\kappa$')
ax[0].plot(kd, sd, 'o', color='#e08a00', ms=6, label='400-moment chain')
ax[0].axvline(kc, color='0.4', lw=0.8, ls=':'); ax[0].text(kc+0.04, -0.95, '$2k/\\pi$', color='0.3')
ax[0].set_xlabel('$\\kappa/k$'); ax[0].set_ylabel('$s/k$'); ax[0].set_ylim(-3, 0.05); ax[0].legend(frameon=
False, fontsize=9, loc='center right')
ph = np.linspace(0.01, np.pi/2-0.02, 500); K = 4*ph**2/np.sin(2*ph)**2
ax[1].semilogy(ph, K, color='#1f4e79', lw=2)
ax[1].axvline(np.pi/2, color='0.4', lw=0.8, ls=':'); ax[1].text(np.pi/2-0.3, 2e3, '$\\phi=\\pi/2$', color
='0.3')
ax[1].set_xlabel('$\\phi=k/\\kappa$'); ax[1].set_ylabel('$K=4\\phi^2/\\sin^2 2\\phi$')
ax[1].text(0.05, 300, 'chain onset $\\kappa/k$:\n50: 0.665\n200: 0.645\n800: 0.639\nexact: $2/\\pi$ = 0.637',
fontsize=9)
fig.tight_layout(); fig.savefig(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '../../figs/fig_v7_onset.png'), dpi
=200)

# SCRIPT: NU-A6-LEVEL3-RATES the odd rate at three resolution levels, from the stored brackets, as fixed in
PREREGISTERED.txt
# Reads res_L3_r125.json and res_L3_r08.json (this folder). Level 1 and 2 thresholds are those of
PREREGISTERED.txt.
import json, math, itertools
DF, DI = 0.49773, 0.52832
tau = lambda d: math.log((DI - d) / (d - DF))
rate = lambda d125, d08: (tau(d125) - tau(d08)) / math.log(1.25)
L1 = dict(a=0.50984158, b=0.51169233); L2 = dict(a=0.50991687, b=0.51175643)
ra = json.load(open('res_L3_r125.json')); rb = json.load(open('res_L3_r08.json'))

138

la, ha, lb, hb = ra['lo'], ra['hi'], rb['lo'], rb['hi']; ma, mb = 0.5 * (la + ha), 0.5 * (lb + hb)
R1, R2, R3 = rate(L1['a'], L1['b']), rate(L2['a'], L2['b']), rate(ma, mb)
corners = [rate(x, y) for x in (la, ha) for y in (lb, hb)]
print(f'level 3 thresholds: r=1.25 {ma:.8f} +- {0.5*(ha-la):.8f} [{la:.8f}, {ha:.8f}]; r=0.8 {mb:.8f} +- {
0.5*(hb-lb):.8f} [{lb:.8f}, {hb:.8f}]')
print(f'odd rate: R1 = {R1:.5f}, R2 = {R2:.5f}, R3 = {R3:.5f}, four-corner range of R3 [{min(corners):.5f}, {
max(corners):.5f}]')
s12, s23 = R2 - R1, R3 - R2
print(f's12 = {s12:+.5f}, s23 = {s23:+.5f} (four-corner range {min(corners)-R2:+.5f} .. {max(corners)-R2:+.5f}
)')
print(f'KILL LINE (b): |R3 - R2| = {abs(s23):.5f} against |R2 - R1| = {abs(s12):.5f}: {"NOT converging" if abs
(s23) > abs(s12) else "passes (the shift shrinks)"};'
f' at the four corners |R3 - R2| <= {max(abs(c - R2) for c in corners):.5f}; sign of s23 {"same as" if
s12 * s23 > 0 else "opposite to"} s12')
for name, lo2, d12, lo, hi, mid in (('r=1.25', L2['a'], L2['a'] - L1['a'], la, ha, ma), ('r=0.8', L2['b'], L2
['b'] - L1['b'], lb, hb, mb)):
f = (mid - lo2) / d12
print(f'{name}: shift L1->L2 {d12:+.3e}, L2->L3 {mid-lo2:+.3e} (range {lo-lo2:+.3e} .. {hi-lo2:+.3e});
factor {f:.3f} (range {(lo-lo2)/d12:.3f} .. {(hi-lo2)/d12:.3f})')
for lab, fac in (('first order in the cell size (2/3)', 2/3), ('second order in the cell size (4/9)', 4/9)
, ('first order in the time step (1/3)', 1/3)):

predicted by {lab}: {p:.8f} {"inside" if lo <= p <= hi else "outside
"} the bracket')

if s12 / s23 > 1:

print(f'RICHARDSON as fixed before the runs: p_obs = {p_obs:.2f}, R_inf = {Rinf:.5f}' + (' -- p_obs
outside [0.5, 4]: reported, marked untrustworthy' if not 0.5 <= p_obs <= 4 else ''))
vals = []
for c in corners:

# SCRIPT: NU-V6-RICHARDSON-A6 three-level odd rate (r = 1.25 / 0.8), kill line and Richardson extrapolation
as preregistered
import json, math, itertools, os, sys

139

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
import oddrate as o
q = 1.5
L1 = ((0.5098412859375, 0.509841871875), (0.5116919799999999, 0.5116926831249999))
L2 = ((0.5099165789062501, 0.50991716484375), (0.5117560815625, 0.5117567846875))
def br(tag):
d = json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, f'res_{tag}.json'))); return (d['lo'], d['hi'])
L3 = (br('L3_r125'), br('L3_r08'))
def rich(R1, R2, R3):
s12, s23 = R2-R1, R3-R2
if s23 == 0 or s12/s23 <= 1: return float('nan'), float('nan')
p = math.log(s12/s23)/math.log(q); return p, R3 + s23/(q**p - 1)
out = {}
for df_lab, df in (('central', o.DF), ('df+7.8e-5', o.DF+7.8e-5), ('df-7.8e-5', o.DF-7.8e-5)):
S = [o.summary('', *L, 1.25) for L in (L1, L2, L3)]
R = [o.rate(0.5*sum(L[0]), 0.5*sum(L[1]), 1.25, df) for L in (L1, L2, L3)]
rng = [o.corners(L[0], L[1], 1.25, df) for L in (L1, L2, L3)]
p, Rinf = rich(*R)
corner = [rich(*c)[1] for c in itertools.product(*rng)]
corner = [c for c in corner if c == c]
part_i = 0.5*(max(corner)-min(corner)) if corner else float('nan')
s23 = R[2]-R[1]
two = {pp: R[2] + s23/(q**pp - 1) for pp in (1, 2)}
part_ii = max(abs(Rinf-two[1]), abs(Rinf-two[2]))
out[df_lab] = dict(R=R, ranges=rng, s12=R[1]-R[0], s23=s23, ratio=(s23/(R[1]-R[0])), p_obs=p, R_inf=Rinf,
err_bracket=part_i, err_order=part_ii, err=part_i+part_ii, two_level=two,
kill=abs(s23) > abs(R[1]-R[0]), corner_n=len(corner))
dc3 = (0.5*sum(L3[0]), 0.5*sum(L3[1]))
out['thresholds_L3'] = dict(r125=dc3[0], r08=dc3[1], shift23_r125=dc3[0]-0.509916871875, shift23_r08=dc3
[1]-0.511756433125,
brackets=L3)
print(json.dumps(out, indent=1))

C Lean statements
The statements below are proved in Lean 4 with Mathlib (v4.34.1) in the repository https:
//github.com/dicipler-pixel/neutrinos-leave-lean. Corrected in this version. The tworate connection-energy identity and the kill-line arithmetic of Tests B, C and D (GB = 970/3059,
GC = 1983/3059, GD = 472/3059) are in the file set at commit 9cd71fa (KillLine.lean,
splitRateEnergy). The verdict arithmetic for the link-rate Tests J, K (crash-safe) and L, items 7,
9, 10 and 12 below (gam_inv, odd_part_free_of_anchor, testK_*, testJ_straddles, testL_*),
is in Neutrinos/LinkVerdict.lean, which exists only on branches v4-shear-link-runs and
fable-audit-lean, not at 9cd71fa; it concerns the November 4 fixed-step values, not the rate
1.10 ± 0.01. Version 12’s statement that a full build at 9cd71fa checks “all 87 theorems” of the
library, including these, is withdrawn, and its “earlier build at commit c372d3f” was on a different
branch. The build and axiom audit were not re-checked for this version. Lean checks the finite
moment model and the verdict arithmetic. It does not check McCormick’s code, the collapse runs,
the classification of runs near threshold, the bracket edges (inputs copied from the run logs) or the
numerical witness.
1. crossLink: |2 Re(ā(−ih)b)| ≤ 2|h||a||b|; crossLink_sq: with A = |a|2 , |a|2 + |b|2 ≤ 1, the
square is at most 4|h|2 A(1 − A) (Appendix A.6).
2. twoMomentConnectionEnergy: 18 k[L, L∗ ]k2F = κ2 k 2 /3 for the two-moment L.
2

6. testB/C/D_verdict: GB = 970/3059, GC = 1983/3059, GD = 472/3059 against the preregistered kill lines.

O(δr )/O(δ1/r ), the odd rate is at least p/q exactly when rp ≤ Qq ; O decreases in δ, so the two
corners of the bracket pair that make Q smallest and largest are the ones checked, and the kernel
decides each inequality exactly.

D

Corrections to version 12

This version keeps every section, figure, table, proof and listing of version 12 and corrects it in
place. The corrections, with the evidence for each, are listed in the file changes_v12_to_v11.md
that ships with this version. In short:
1. Mass scaling (Appendix D): M ≃ MH (Tdec )/ϵ2dec , not ϵ2dec MH . The door runs (ϵdec = 2) sit
at a horizon mass of about 3.7 × 104 M⊙ , not 6 × 105 .
2. Recorder prediction (i) killed (Section 12); version 12 reported the recorder as “the kept
share leads” without saying so.
3. One baseline for G(1): S1, 0.4266 (Section 9), with the shifts it causes.
4. Lean: the “87 theorems at commit 9cd71fa” build claim is withdrawn (Appendix C).
5. References: McCormick’s preprints and code given their correct records; Escrivà given
its title; Ma and Bertschinger cited; Beyond the Event Horizon cited with its record; the
previously uncited Colour Selects the Leader now cited where its folder of checks is used
(Appendix B.12).
6. The door: its per-run logs are missing; it is one engineered amplitude; one cell is linear in
the amplitude; its size depends on the collision model by 37% (Section 11).
143

7. The link rate 1.10 ± 0.01 is labelled not converged wherever it appears.
8. Every failed prediction is listed in Section 15.
9. Smaller numbers: Test A’s predicted range, the Test E error, the Gramian order count,
the closure and elimination accuracies, and the annihilation-control maximum.
Added: the author’s question and frame (Section 2), two posed tests (Section 17), readings from
Beyond the Event Horizon (Section 18) and Limitations (Section 16).

D.1 Mass at horizon entry
